# Exponential 6.0.15

Not released yet. The 6.0.15 line follows v6.0.14 (2026-06-07); no tag `v6.0.15` existed on 2026-10-12. This page
lists what the line contains on that date.

Read this page if you run 6.0.14 or older and plan to move to the current code, or if you want to know what the
line brings. It is long because the line is: four months of work. Use the sections by period.

## Upgrading

Follow the [Upgrading guide](../../eng-us/guides/upgrading.md), part C. Then read the checklist of each period you pass,
in this order:

1. [Behaviour changes of July and August 2026](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-07-08.md)
2. [Behaviour changes of 16 to 30 September 2026](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-09b.md)
3. [Behaviour changes of 1 to 2 October 2026](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-10.md)
4. [Upgrade notes: e-mail preferences and the mail gate](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/mail-preferences.md)
5. For the changes of 10 to 12 October 2026: the operator notes of
   [the autoload generator](#operator-note) (run `php bin/php/ezpgenerateautoloads.php -e` once) and of
   [user accounts that exist only as a draft](#operator-note-1); `expai` and `expqueue` arrive inactive, see
   [Exponential Intelligence in the distribution](#12-october-2026-exponential-intelligence-in-the-distribution).

6. [Authorizing an OAuth client needs the policy oauth/authorize](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/oauth-authorize-policy.md): run
   `php bin/php/addoauthauthorizepolicy.php --dry-run`, then without `--dry-run`, so the roles that let users sign in
   keep authorizing REST clients.
7. [Well-known passwords are refused, and a forgot-password link expires](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/weak-passwords-and-reset-key-lifetime.md):
   new passwords are checked against a list of well-known ones (`WeakPasswordCheck`), and a reset link works for one
   hour (`ForgotPasswordKeyLifetime`). Existing passwords keep working.
8. [Demo mode](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/demo-mode.md): nothing changes unless the environment variable `EXP_DEMO_MODE=1` is set,
   which is only for public demos. Check that no production process has it.
9. [Translated documentation](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/translated-documentation.md): the documentation moved to
   `doc/eng-us/`, the German translation is `doc/ger-de/` (a language directory is the locale code in lower case).
   Links, scripts and settings overrides of your own that name a documentation file need `eng-us/` after `doc/`, and
   ones written for the interim names (`en/us/` and `de/` below `doc/`) need the new names; help addresses do not change, and
   the old ones (`/exphelp/viewer/en/us/<id>`, `?lang=en-us`) are redirected or still accepted.
10. [Help maintained as content: DITA export, stored e-books, overdue reviews](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/help-content-dita-ebooks-reviews.md):
    on a site that runs help as content, setting a help object to Released now builds and stores the manuals'
    e-books as file objects (`EbooksOnRelease`); run `./console exp:help:content ebooks` once after the deploy.
11. [PKCE and state for the OAuth authorization server](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/oauth-pkce.md): `ezprest_authcode` has
    two new columns, `code_challenge` and `code_challenge_method` (in the database update below); until they exist
    no authorization code can be issued. Clients made in the administration are not affected otherwise.
    [CORS for the REST API](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/rest-cors.md) is new and off by default.

The database update for the line is `update/database/<engine>/6.0/dbupdate-6.0.0-6.0.15.sql`, followed once by
`php update/common/scripts/6.0/createaudittables.php`.

## July and August 2026

The changes between 1 July and 31 August 2026. The stories are in [July 2026](../../eng-us/history/2026/2026-07.md) and
[August 2026](../../eng-us/history/2026/2026-08.md); the upgrade checklist is
[Behaviour changes of July and August 2026](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-07-08.md).

### Added

- `ezpm.php`, the package manager, runs through `bin/php/console`. It installs content packages with a lock, delayed
  indexing, batched commits, a destination node and a progress line.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/ezpm-package-manager-cli.md).
- `bin/php/resetuserpassword.php` resets a password, authorised by an administrator or by the root user.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/reset-user-password.md).
- The Kickstarter (`bin/php/kickstarter.php`, `exp:kickstarter`) installs a site from `kickstart.ini`.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/kickstarter-cli.md), [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/kickstartercli.md).
- The Template Editor orders overrides by priority, chooses the source, matches by object id, edits and removes match
  conditions, and has a menu entry. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/template-editor-overrides.md).
- `ShowTemplatePathComments` (`site.ini [TemplateSettings]`) wraps every template in START and STOP comments that
  name the override source. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/template-path-comments.md).
- `AdditionalExtensionDirectories[]` and `eZExtension::extensionName()`: extensions in more than one folder.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/additional-extension-directories.md).
- Icon themes in extensions (`icon.ini [ExtensionSettings] IconExtensions[]`) and a default `job` icon.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/icon-themes-in-extensions.md).
- Hooks for a Redis or Valkey content view cache and compiled INI cache.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/valkey-cache-hooks.md), [the extension](../../eng-us/features/6.0/extensions/sevenx_valkey_cache.md).
- **Setup > Extensions** sorts, shows info cards and downloads an extension in four formats.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/extension-list-and-downloads.md).
- The installation name output filter, and the `installation_name` and `is_production_system` operators.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/installation-name-in-pages.md).
- `fetch( 'content', 'class_list' )`, `ezscript.ini [eZScriptSettings] RootDelay`, a Layouts tab in the admin node
  view ([Layouts](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/LAYOUTS.md)), and the PHPUnit workflow of GitHub Actions.
  [Specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/continuous-integration.md).

### Updated

- The setup wizard needs privileges only on your own database; packages install their dependencies first; the seed
  data defaults to `eng-US` and describes Exponential; the kickstart siteaccesses are `site` and `admin`.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/clean-install-defaults.md).
- Security: tokens and activation hashes come from `random_bytes()`; header and shell injections are fixed; a login
  fails closed when the user has no `ezuser_setting` row; CDN scripts load over https; jQuery 3.7.1 with Migrate
  3.4.1 at that time. [Specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/security-hardening-2026-08.md).
- HTML5 markup in kernel and design output; PHP 7.4 to 8.5 clean-ups; INI files are saved so that untouched keys stay
  as they were; permission SQL works with empty limitation lists; imported Ibexa DocBook output; better diagnostics
  for image aliases and missing objects; SQLite schema and debug output fixes; MongoDB logs through `eZDebug`.
  [Upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-07-08.md).
- The media theme directory is part of the repository, no longer a submodule.
- The default sort of the extension list is the loading order since 29 September, not A to Z.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/extension-loading-order.md).

### Renamed

- `kickstart.ini` is shipped as `kickstart.ini--example`.
- The progress reporter `eZPMStatus` is `expScriptStatus`.

Related: [Continuous integration and the test suite](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/continuous-integration.md),
[Template override ordering](../../eng-us/features/6.0/template-override-ordering.md) (how the editor's `Priority` is used).

## 16 to 30 September 2026

The story is in [September 2026, second half](../../eng-us/history/2026/2026-09b.md); the upgrade checklist is
[Behaviour changes of 16 to 30 September 2026](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-09b.md).

### Added

- Velocity (`./console exp:velocity`): a persistent-worker application server with the Qbix, FrankenPHP and PHP
  built-in engines, HTTPS, HTTP/2, and the `deploy`, `config`, `ssl` and `ext` verbs.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/velocity-persistent-worker-server.md), [Deploying guide](../../eng-us/guides/deploying.md).
- The server control commands `exp:webserver`, `exp:frankenphp` and `exp:solr`.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/web-server-and-solr-commands.md).
- `./console exp:install`: an installation in one command on SQLite, MySQL, PostgreSQL, MongoDB or Oracle, with the
  logs `var/log/setup.log` and `var/log/kickstart.log`.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/install-in-one-command.md),
  [specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/installer-logs-and-seed-data.md),
  [Getting started](../../eng-us/guides/getting-started.md).
- Maintenance mode (`bin/php/maintenance.php`, `exp:maintenance`, **Setup > Maintenance**).
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/maintenance-mode.md).
- The role-aware HTTP cache and the SQL query cache, both off by default.
  [HTTP cache](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/httpcache.md), [SQL query cache](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/sql-query-cache.md).
- The store dashboard (`shop/dashboard`) with fourteen order statuses, and permanent order receipts
  (`shop/orderreceipt/<token>`, `shop.ini [OrderViewSettings] OrderLinkView`).
  [Store dashboard](../../eng-us/features/6.0/store-dashboard.md), [order receipts](../../eng-us/features/6.0/order-receipts.md).
- A package contents browser, package compare and selective import (`package/compare`), package licenses from
  `package.ini [LicenseSettings]`, and Semantic Versioning for packages.
  [Compare and import](../../eng-us/features/6.0/package-compare-and-import.md),
  [licenses and versions](../../eng-us/features/6.0/package-licenses-and-versions.md).
- Cache clears that move directories aside (`site.ini [FileSettings] RenameBeforeDelete`) and the `cache_cleanup`
  cronjob part. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/cache-clear-rename-aside.md).
- The "form expired" 403 page (`design:error/kernel/6.tpl`). [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/form-expired-page.md).
- The loading order card of **Setup > Extensions** and `[SiteAccessSettings] ExtensionSettingsSiteAccess`; drag and
  drop order of template overrides; policy IDs, sorting and order buttons in roles; moving a class attribute to the
  top or bottom. [Loading order](../../eng-us/features/6.0/extension-loading-order.md),
  [override ordering](../../eng-us/features/6.0/template-override-ordering.md),
  [role policy order](../../eng-us/features/6.0/role-policy-order.md),
  [class attribute move](../../eng-us/features/6.0/class-attribute-move-to-edge.md).
- `exp:cache`; `./console --version`, `--about` and `--copyright`; view templates for DocBook tags.
  [Cache console](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/cache-console.md),
  [chronicle](../../eng-us/history/2026/2026-09b.md#small-changes-explained).

### Updated

- Security defaults: headers on every page, the session cookie, uniform answers of login and forgot-password,
  stricter file rules, `[HttpCacheSettings] TagHeader`.
  [Specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/security-defaults-2026-09.md),
  [Security and audit guide](../../eng-us/guides/security-and-audit.md).
- About 60 datatype and helper fixes: arrays posted as text, script URLs, directory parts in file names, header and
  INI injection. [Specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/datatype-input-hardening.md).
- Driver and installer fixes for SQLite, PostgreSQL, MySQL, Oracle and MongoDB; SQLite is the wizard's default.
  [Specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/database-drivers-2026-09.md),
  [MongoDB](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/MONGODB_KERNEL_SUPPORT_EXPANSION.md).
- The administration: the content structure tree starts at the top node; the header tabs read Media, Users and
  Store; the About and Copyright pages list every extension with description, license and website.
  [Content tree](../../eng-us/features/6.0/admin-content-tree-top-node.md),
  [About and package pages](../../eng-us/features/6.0/about-and-package-pages.md).
- Translations: a finished `eng-US`, complete German, and valid `lang` values for twenty locales.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/translations-and-languages.md).
- Translations: the French administration is complete, a new and complete Ukrainian one is offered by the interface
  language switch, and the German one uses one term per concept (Siteaccess, Extension, Sektion, Eigentümer,
  Ausstehend, USt) with the wrong translations of "Action" and "Discard" corrected.
  [Languages](../../eng-us/languages.md#4-every-administration-translation).
- Translations: the German administration passes the translation quality test, which now checks it too: lost
  placeholders, tags, entities, spaces and line breaks restored, and the English texts left in it translated.
  [Languages](../../eng-us/languages.md#4-every-administration-translation).
- **Start preloading** runs in the background.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/preload-sites-view.md#it-runs-in-the-background).

### Removed

- The setting `AnonymousOrderLinkView`: every order links to its receipt when `OrderLinkView=orderreceipt`.
- The SQLite demo data files that no installer loads.

### Renamed

- `[HttpCacheSettings] ProxyHeaders` is `TagHeader`; `ProxyHeaders=enabled` in an override still means `xkey`.
- The site's service worker is `/index.js`; `/sw.js` loads it.
- The version class is `ExponentialSDK`, and the example crontab is `exponential.cron`.
- The engine package is `se7enxweb/exponential-velocity`.
- The top-level alias of Setup is `x-setup`.

All five: [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-09b.md).

## 1 and 2 October 2026

The story is in [October 2026](../../eng-us/history/2026/2026-10.md); the upgrade checklist is
[Behaviour changes of 1 to 2 October 2026](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-10.md).

### Added

- **Bookmark folders**: bookmarks can be organised in a tree of virtual folders of any depth, in the bookmark page,
  the Bookmarks box, the browse dialog, the online editor (TinyMCE 3 and 8) and the `expbookmark` remote services.
  Needs the database update (a table `expbookmark_folder` and two columns on `ezcontentbrowsebookmark`); existing
  bookmarks stay at the top level and the flat bookmark list is unchanged.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/bookmark-folders.md), [upgrade](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/bookmark-folders.md).
- **Audit trail**: a hash-chained, signed, searchable record of sign-ins, content, settings, commerce and deploys.
  It comes with the `exp:audit` command, the Audit tab and console, sinks (syslog, journald, signed webhook, mail),
  alert rules, archives in five formats and the import of the 4.x logs. On by default.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/audit-trail.md), [specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/audit-event-model.md),
  [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/audit.md).
- **Content jobs**: large remove, copy, move, hide, section, state and location operations run as resumable
  background jobs, with `content/job/<id>`, `content/jobs`, `./console exp:expcontentjob`, the `contentjobs`
  cronjob part and `[ContentJobSettings]`. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/content-jobs.md),
  [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/content-jobs.md).
- **`exp:ini`**, a settings editor that changes INI files line by line in every scope.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/exp-ini-command.md), [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/console-exp-ini.md).
- **The Exp Debug bar**: tabs, a live summary, settings with presets and undo, cache controls, minimise.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/exp-debug-bar.md), [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/debug-bar.md).
- **Table options of the sub-items list**: 129 registered columns, attribute columns, presets, CSV export, columns
  defined by INI and PHP or template. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/subitems-table-options.md),
  [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/subitems-table-options.md).
- **Request rules** (`settings/requestrules.ini`, `bin/php/ezrequestrules.php`) and the policy
  `content/view_system_url`. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/request-rules.md),
  [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/view_full_security.md).
- **expservices**: about 1,100 remote services over ezjscore, personal API tokens, `expfeed::runImport`, the
  `expeditor` services and two portal front ends. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/remote-services-expservices.md),
  [specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/expservices.md),
  [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/backend_ezjscore_services.md),
  [Remote services guide](../../eng-us/guides/remote-services-and-apps.md).
- **The online editor on TinyMCE 8.9.2**, an opt-in second engine, with ezoe dialogs for embed, links, custom
  tags, literal tags, tables and general tags, an engine registry, `/ezoe/engine` and browser tests.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/online-editor-tinymce8.md), [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/ezoe-tinymce8.md).
- **The admin4 design**, light and dark. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/admin4-design.md).
- The editor siteaccess and design, installed by the setup wizard, with `EditorAccess`, `EditorAccessPort` and
  `EditorAccessHostname` in `kickstart.ini`; a new look for the setup wizard.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/setup-wizard-and-editor-siteaccess.md).
- `HiddenTabs[]` in `menu.ini [TopAdminMenu]` and `admininterface.ini [WindowControlsSettings]`.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/hidden-admin-tabs.md).
- A page for missing Composer libraries that repairs the installation from the browser, with `exprepair.php`.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/repair-from-the-browser.md), [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/repair.md).
- `Exponential\Runnable` base classes (`Runnable`, `Command`, `CronjobPart`, `ModuleView`), events around every
  command, cronjob part and view, `[RunnableSettings]`, and shared services (trash, sessions, drafts).
  [Specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/runnable-commands-cronjobs-views.md),
  [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/cli_cronjob_view_abstractions.md).
- Every column of the order list sorts it. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/order-list-sorting.md).
- The trash records who trashed each item and from where. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/trash-who-and-where.md).
- A queue for SQLite writers: a transaction waits at its start for the writers ahead of it, up to
  `[DatabaseSettings] SQLiteTransactionWait` seconds (default 25). [Upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/sqlite-transactions.md).
- An opcode cache profile for comparing engines, off unless the file `var/tmp/opcache_profile.on` exists.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/velocity-opcode-cache-and-profile.md).
- English and German texts for the debug bar, the audit console, content jobs, `setup/rad` and about 500 other
  strings that showed up untranslated.

### Updated

- jQuery 4.0.0, jQuery Migrate 4.0.2 and jQuery UI 1.14.2 in ezjscore 1.4.0; the admin runs on jQuery 4 and
  Exponential UI. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/jquery4-and-yui-removal.md).
- The SQL query cache knows Oracle's statements (ezoracle 2.3.2).
  [Specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/database-drivers-sqlite-oracle.md).
- The static cache refreshes the pages of a publish once each, in parallel (`staticcache.ini [CacheSettings]
  FetchConcurrency`, default 8). In the measured case 4975 ms became about 500 ms.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/static-cache-generator.md).
- Velocity keeps the kernel's regenerated cache files in the opcode cache (`file_update_protection` 0 for the
  server), so a rendered page costs about what it does under PHP-FPM. Before, the opcode cache served 2.7% of the
  includes under Velocity against 97% under PHP-FPM; 644 ms against 166 ms of CPU.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/velocity-opcode-cache-and-profile.md).
- The admin dashboard, menus and top tabs show a link only to a user who can open it.
  [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/admin-links-follow-permissions.md),
  [upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-10.md).
- The online editor's upload checks the file type on the server; poll votes, form submissions and the VAT country
  choice need `content/read`. [Upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-10.md#access-and-security).
- `exp:velocity deploy` always clears the `design_base` cache and, with `--packer`, the ezjscore packer cache.
  [Deploying guide](../../eng-us/guides/deploying.md#7-ship-a-php-change-with-one-command).
- Admin details: the header search popup works in both copies; the Store sidebar lists orders and products first;
  long names in the side columns wrap; the page-not-found error names the missing page; the debug report remembers
  whether it is open.
- The classic grey administration design has the Exponential logo, a left menu that collapses and resizes, and the
  Layouts pages in its look. Every logo image of the kernel designs shows the Exponential logo.
- Every copyright notice names 1998 - 2026 7x & Exponential Foundation first.
  [Specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/runnable-commands-cronjobs-views.md#copyright-and-headers).
- Kernel commands, cronjob parts and module views are classes; their entry points are unchanged. Every command shows
  a description in `bin/php/console list`.
- **Empty trash** purges in batches, like the command; **Remove timed out sessions** also removes their baskets; the
  `clusterpurge` part uses 30 days; a copy of an object with an image owns its own image files.
- Fixed: SQLite "database is locked" under concurrent writes (transactions now queue for the write lock);
  `subString()` without a length; the asynchronous publisher shares no connection with its fork and no longer stops
  with a type error on PHP 8; the template path comments of a request no longer read another siteaccess's override
  list.
- Fixed: PHP 8.5 notices on Oracle (preferences, class attribute categories, `ezpExtension::getInfo`, the template
  compiler, `eZHTTPTool::redirect()`, the INI setting datatype, `ristring`, `{if}`); `E_STRICT` in the tree menu and
  the REST front controllers; recurring warnings in the logs.
- Fixed: removing a draft removes every image file of that draft; removing a media attribute keeps a file that
  another row names; removing a subtree that holds every location of an object removes the object; a node's
  `path_identification_string` is rewritten from the stored value; removing a VAT rule no longer stops with a fatal
  error; the PDF export of a content node no longer answers 500.
- Fixed: the CSS packer keeps the space before a colon in selectors; the Velocity warm-up never takes the setup
  wizard's lease.
- Fixed: a module redirect carries the request query (`?gclid=...`) only to relative targets and to targets on the own
  host; redirects to another host, including another host-matched siteaccess such as the admin host, and
  protocol-relative (`//host`) or other-scheme targets are left untouched, so a sealed payment URL is no longer
  invalidated by tracking parameters. The query joins with `&` when the target has one and stays before a `#fragment`.
  See [the redirect note](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/redirect-query-and-image-alias-guards.md).
- Fixed: `ezjscore/call` answers a PHP `Error` in a server function with a generic text and writes the details to the
  log, instead of breaking the JSON response; exceptions still pass their message, and the `{ ok, error }` envelope of
  the remote services is unchanged.
- Fixed: an image attribute that never stored a file no longer asks the image manager for every alias on every request.
- Fixed: the lazy image alias write-back no longer reverts a published image to stale draft paths.
- New releases of the required extensions; see [Required packages](#required-packages-root-composerjson-2026-10-01-to-2026-10-02).

### Removed

- YUI, from the admin, admin3, admin4 and standard designs and from the bundled ezjscore 1.5.0: the YUI libraries,
  `ezjsc::yui2`, `ezjsc::yui3`, `ezjsc::yui3io` and their settings, and the YUI 2 calendar script of the standard
  design. [Upgrade notes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/yui-removal.md).

### Renamed

- explayouts 1.4.10: the component blocks are `exp_component_<type>`, and block links name their node by its remote
  id. Two upgrade scripts rename the stored blocks and links. The media theme 1.5.29 and expsite_data_media 1.0.6
  follow.
- The debug report, and the bar, are named "Exp Debug".

## 3 and 4 October 2026: notifications

The upgrade checklist is [Upgrade notes: notifications](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/notification-ui-and-commands.md). The system is
described in [the user's guide](../../eng-us/features/6.0/notifications.md), [the administrator's guide](../../eng-us/guides/notifications-administrator.md)
and [the specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/notifications.md).

### Added

- **Notification status page** (Setup, Notification) with pending events, digest items, subscriptions, the last runs and a
  problem list; **Run now** and a dry-run preview as background jobs; cleanup of handled events, old events and
  subscriptions to deleted content.
- **Commands** `exp:notification:status`, `run`, `events` and `subscriptions` (aliases `exp:notify:*`), with `--dry-run`,
  `--mail-file-dir` (mail to files for one process), `--at`, `--event`. [Command reference](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/notifications-cli.md).
- `expNotificationService`: one place that runs, plans, records and audits notification runs, under a lock. The cronjob
  part `notification` can run alone (`php runcronjobs.php notification`).
- Confirmation pages for adding and removing a notification, and guides for administrators and developers, the
  settings reference and the command reference.
- **Failed mail is kept**: a notification the mail transport refuses stays in the queue and is tried again at every run for
  `[RuleSettings] RetryHours` (default 72); the run record, `exp:notification:run` (exit code 1) and the status page report it, and a message older than
  that is given up and counted.

### Updated

- **My notification settings** is an overview (admin4, admin4l): the followed items with path, class and last change, a filter, paging,
  a confirmed removal, notices, a clearer digest and collaboration choice; English and German texts.
- Opening `/notification/addtonotification/<node>` asks first instead of subscribing on a plain GET.
- The notification filter survives a failing handler, can run for given events, and removes handled events nothing waits for.
- Fixes: the digest settings are validated and a user without a settings row can save them, a single address no longer
  breaks the mail transport, the subtree form refuses unreadable, missing and duplicate nodes, collaboration rules are looked
  up by user id, `eZNotificationCollection::addItem()` stores its send date and `fetchByDate()` finds due items.
- `eZFileTransport` writes to `[MailSettings] FileTransportDirectory` (default `var/log/mail`).

## 4 October 2026: e-mail preferences and consent

The upgrade checklist is [Upgrade notes: e-mail preferences](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/mail-preferences.md). The system is described in
[the user's guide](../../eng-us/features/6.0/mail-preferences.md), [the administrator's guide](../../eng-us/guides/mail-preferences-administrator.md),
[the developer's guide](../../eng-us/guides/mail-preferences-developer.md), [the specification](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/mail-preferences.md)
and [the law checklist](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/mail-preferences-compliance.md) (CAN-SPAM, GDPR and ePrivacy, CCPA/CPRA,
CASL, RFC 8058).

### Added

- **E-mail preferences**: one page (`/mailpreferences/settings`, also in the administration under **My e-mail
  preferences**, and without login by a personal link) where every person turns each optional kind of e-mail on or off,
  chooses how often, turns all optional e-mail off with one button, sees the history of their choices and downloads their
  data as JSON or CSV. Every optional kind is off until the person turns it on. Essential mail (account security, orders,
  legal notices, admin alerts) is listed and always sent.
- **The mail gate** in `eZMailTransport::send()`, for every transport: mail declares a category with
  `eZMail::setCategory()`; optional mail goes only to people who turned it on, one mail each, with a footer (why, one-click
  unsubscribe, preferences, the organisation and its postal address) and the RFC 8058 headers `List-Unsubscribe` and
  `List-Unsubscribe-Post`. Mail without a category is sent as before and counted.
- **Unsubscribe in one click** (`/mailpreferences/unsubscribe/<token>`), also from the mail program's own button; a page
  opened by a link changes nothing until its button is pressed. **Double opt-in** for newsletters, offers and a new e-mail
  address. **Send me a link** for people without an account.
- **Consent log** of every change with the exact text shown, the source, the IP address and the acting user; **suppression
  list** stored as salted hashes; links encrypted with AES-256-GCM under a site secret; export and erasure that keeps the
  proof of a withdrawal; retention by the cronjob part `mailpreferences` (group `infrequent`).
- Administrator's pages under **Setup > E-mail preferences**: status, categories (create, rename, describe), suppression
  list, consent log with filters and CSV export, a user's preferences. Policies `mailpreferences/administrate` and
  `mailpreferences/export`.
- Commands `exp:mail:status`, `exp:mail:preferences`, `exp:mail:suppression`, `exp:mail:consent`, `exp:mail:gate`.
  [Command reference](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/mail-preferences-cli.md).
- Unticked boxes for the optional kinds on the registration form; English and German texts.
- Tables `expmail_category`, `expmail_preference`, `expmail_consent_log`, `expmail_suppression`, `expmail_pending` in the
  kernel schema and the database update.
- Tests: `MailPreferencesCoreTest` (20 cases of the core and the gate) and `MailPreferencesComplianceTest` (one case per
  law requirement of the checklist), live style with the file transport and `.invalid` addresses.

### Updated

- Optional mail to several recipients is sent as one mail per recipient (`[GateSettings] SplitRecipients`).
- The profile, the notification settings, the current user box and the menus link to the e-mail preferences.
- The text footer of optional mail keeps its line breaks: each link ends its line.
- Collaboration mail follows the frequency chosen on the preference page: daily or weekly it goes into the digest, which
  now has a collaboration part, and the digest lists its items again (its loops and includes rendered nothing).
- A new e-mail address of an account (user/edit, the administration's edit, the account services) takes effect only once
  it is confirmed from the new mailbox, and the old address gets a notice (`[EmailChangeSettings] Confirm`).
- The downloads of a person's e-mail data, the consent log and suppression list exports and the RFC 8058 one-click
  answer end the request through one helper (`MailPreferencesPage::sendResponse()`). Under Velocity they never answered
  (504) although the change was saved: the loop that emptied the output buffers did not stop at the worker's own buffer.
  The setup views that stream (preloader, static cache, cronjob log) and the Ajax answer of the cronjob console had the
  same loop; all of them now use `eZExecution::discardOutputBuffers()`.
- The history shows a waiting e-mail address change as **E-mail address: New address asked for, waiting for
  confirmation**, no longer as "All optional e-mail".
- The footer and the confirmation and "send me a link" mails name the public site (the siteaccess of the request when
  it is public, else the default siteaccess), never an administration siteaccess, also from a command or a cronjob;
  `OrganisationName` still wins when set.
- Closed gaps of the law checklist: `DefaultOn=true` of an optional category is treated as off and shown on the status
  page unless `[CategorySettings] AllowDefaultOn=enabled`; removing an account erases its e-mail preferences and
  anonymises its consent log (the withdrawals stay as proof, the suppression list stays); optional mail with an empty
  From gets `[MailSettings] EmailSender` or `AdminEmail` (logged, and not sent without either); the footer and the
  preference page link the privacy notice (`[FooterSettings] PrivacyURL`, else the node of menu.ini `[SiteInfo]
  PrivacyPolicyID`); unsubscribe and manage links never live shorter than 60 days.
- Tests: `MailPreferencesHardeningTest` (MPH-01 to MPH-09), including the one-click POST and the downloads over HTTP on
  the site's web server and on Velocity.

## 5 October 2026: PHP 8.0 is the oldest supported version

PHP 8.0, the stock PHP of Red Hat Enterprise Linux 9, runs the kernel and the shipped extensions again (Velocity
needed 8.1 until its release 0.0.4.54 of 20 October 2026, which runs on 8.0 too; see
[20 October 2026](#20-october-2026-exponential-velocity-is-required-again-and-runs-on-php-80)). Details, the full list of findings and the RHEL 9 notes: [PHP 8.0 support](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/php-8.0-support.md).

### Added

- `lib/phpcompat.php`: `array_is_list()` for PHP 8.0, read by `autoload.php` only on PHP older than 8.1.
- The Quality workflow's job `php80`: `php -l` with PHP 8.0 on every PHP file, a production install with 8.0 and the
  boot smoke test `tests/bin/bootsmoke.php` (every kernel and extension class, the compatibility functions, a
  template with `wash`); `make lint80` and `make lint80-all`.

### Updated

- `htmlspecialchars()` and the other HTML escaping functions get the PHP 8.1 default flags explicitly in 28 kernel,
  library, ezjscore, ezoe and expservices files, the `wash` operator and the simple tags operator included: single
  quotes are escaped on PHP 8.0 as on 8.1 and later. Clear the template cache once after upgrading.
- The CSV export of sub-items writes its `"\r\n"` lines on PHP 8.0 too (`fputcsv()` has no `$eol` there).
- The OAuth authorize view passes `implode()` its arguments in the order PHP 8 requires; the LDAP login no longer
  calls `crypt()` without a salt.
- The checks of pull request #113 test PHP 8.0 instead of 8.1: `phpcs.xml.dist` `testVersion` `8.0-`, the pre-commit
  hook (`EXP_PHP80`), `composer.dev.json` platform PHP 8.0.30.
- README, the PHP 8 page, the console and CI pages state PHP 8.0 as the minimum.

## 5 October 2026: related objects sorted by class name

### Updated

- Fixed: `eZContentObject::relatedObjects()` (and the fetches `related_objects` and `reverse_related_objects`) sorted
  by `class_name` no longer fails on MySQL and PostgreSQL with "Unknown column 'ezcontentclass.id' in 'on clause'":
  `ezcontentclass` is now the last table of the FROM list, the one the class name join can see. SQLite was not
  affected. The other queries that append the sorting joins of `createSortingSQLStrings()` (the node list and
  count of `eZContentObjectTreeNode`, the content structure tree, the keyword fetch, the trash list) were checked:
  they build their FROM as a chain of `INNER JOIN`s that includes `ezcontentclass`, so the class name join sees it;
  only `relatedObjects()` used a comma list.

## 5 October 2026: database sessions

### Updated

- Fixed: `ezpSessionHandler::setSaveHandler()` no longer calls `session_module_name( 'user' )`, which throws a
  `ValueError` on PHP 8 and stopped every request of an installation with `[Session] Handler=ezpSessionHandlerDB`.
  See bug 4 on [the session handler page](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/ezpsessionhandlerdb-php8-bugfix-and-tests.md).
- Updated: `setSaveHandler()` gives PHP the handler as an object (`ezpSessionHandlerAdapter`, implementing
  `SessionHandlerInterface`) instead of six callbacks, which PHP 8.4 reports as deprecated on every request; the
  handlers themselves are unchanged. See bug 5 on the same page.

## 5 October 2026: view cache files of cache_ttl=0

### Updated

- Fixed: a node view whose template sets `cache_ttl` to 0 no longer stores the whole page in its view cache file. The
  file keeps only the no-cache advice (`no_cache`, `cache_ttl` 0, a few bytes), which the next requests still read to
  generate the page without the cache lock and without storing it. The number of files stays the same: one per cache
  key, as before.

## 5 October 2026: expiry timestamps per siteaccess VarDir

### Updated

- Fixed: with siteaccesses that have a `[FileSettings] VarDir` of their own (several sites on one installation),
  clearing a cache of one site no longer expires the caches of every site. The expiry handler is created before the
  siteaccess is known, with the `expiry.php` of the default VarDir, and kept it; `eZSiteAccess::change()` now calls
  `eZExpiryHandler::resetForCurrentCacheDirectory()`, which stores the old instance and drops it when its file is not
  the one of the current cache directory. Installations with one VarDir for all siteaccesses see no change.
  Under Exponential Velocity the shared instance does not outlive a request either: `$GLOBALS['eZExpiryHandlerInstance']`
  is not in `velocity.ini [ApplicationSettings] KeepGlobals[]` or its built-in defaults, so a persistent worker reads `expiry.php` anew
  for every request and sees the timestamps another worker or PHP-FPM wrote.

## 5 October 2026: benchmark command

### Added

- `./console exp:benchmark` (`exp:bench`, `bin/php/benchmark.php`): measures an installation. HTTP mode times the
  front page, the first menu pages and the admin login page (or `--url`, `--urls-file`) with PHP's curl, warm or
  `--cold` (a unique query string past every response cache), and compares two or more servers back to back with
  `--compare=A,B` in alternating rounds over kept-alive connections. Kernel mode times boot, the full view render
  cold and warm, INI load, content fetch, node list, database round trip, cache write and read and an image alias
  lookup in-process. Output as a table with min, median, p95, p99, max, requests per second, errors, status codes,
  bytes and cache answers, or `--json` / `--csv`; `--save` writes a run and `--baseline` compares with one, exiting
  with status 1 when a metric got worse by more than `--threshold` (15 %) and the `--min-delta` noise floor (1 ms).
  `ab`, `wrk` and `oha` are used with `--tool=` when installed. Read-only and polite by default (100 requests,
  concurrency 4); more than 1000 requests or a concurrency above 16 against a host that is not loopback needs
  `--force`, and a host the site does not serve is only measured when its URLs are named. Guide:
  [Benchmark](../../eng-us/features/6.0/benchmark.md).
- `expBenchmark`, `expBenchmarkHttp` and `expBenchmarkKernel` (`kernel/classes/`), with tests in
  `tests/tests/kernel/classes/benchmark/` that need no network: the HTTP client is tested against a `php -S`
  server on 127.0.0.1 that the test starts and stops.

## 5 October 2026: digest send date without an item

### Updated

- Fixed: `eZNotificationSchedule::setDateForItem()` accepts `null` as item and then only returns the next send date
  of the digest, instead of failing with "Call to a member function setAttribute() on null". A digest handler of an
  extension that needs the date without an item no longer stops the request. The kernel handlers always pass an item
  and are unchanged.
- Fixed: a digest goes out at the chosen hour of the local clock across a change between summer and winter time; the
  send date was the current time plus a fixed number of seconds and was an hour off then. The date is now built from
  calendar fields (`eZNotificationSchedule::nextSendDate()`).
- Fixed: a monthly digest on a day the next month does not have (the 31st, in a month with 30 days or in February)
  falls on that month's last day; it used to run into the month after (31 January + 31 days = 3 March).
- Added: `setDateForItem()` takes the time to compute from as an optional third argument, for tests.

## 5 October 2026: edit access to objects that were never published

### Updated

- Fixed: `eZContentObject::checkAccess( 'edit' )` on an object that was never published:
  - The rule that someone who may create the object under its parent may edit it applies at every version, not only
    at version 1; a rejected first version is edited as version 2 and later. Without a node assignment the rule
    denies instead of failing with "Call to a member function attribute() on null".
  - A Subtree limitation also counts the location the object will be published under (the parent of the main node
    assignment of the current version) for edit, so an approver with an edit policy for that subtree can edit it.
    Read and the other functions are unchanged, the owner rule too, and a policy for another subtree still gives no
    access.
- Updated: the same edit rules reach every place that wrote them out. A role for edit assigned for a subtree
  (User_Subtree) counts the location an object that was never published will be published under, as the Subtree
  limitation does; `eZContentObjectTreeNode::checkAccess()` (the preview node of `content/versionview`),
  `eZContentObjectVersion::checkAccess()` and `content/removeeditversion` apply the create rule at every version
  and deny without a node assignment, through the new `eZContentObject::draftCreateAccess()`; the rule reads the
  main node assignment (`draftMainNodeAssignment()`), not the first one. A parent node that no longer exists is
  skipped instead of warning. Who may now edit what, and how a site notices:
  [Edit access to objects that were never published](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/draft-edit-access.md). Tests:
  `eZContentObjectDraftLocationTest` (no database), `eZContentObjectDraftAccessLiveTest` (live).

## 5 October 2026: new version of an object that was never published

### Updated

- Fixed: a new version of an object that was never published keeps the location of the version it is copied from.
  `eZContentObject::copyVersion()` skipped every node assignment without remote_id, and `createNewVersion()` rebuilds
  the others only from existing nodes, which such an object does not have; after an approval workflow rejected the
  first version, editing it again lost the location, and publishing asked for one again (or used the default
  location from the version view). A location still to be created (op_code CREATE without remote_id) of an object
  that was never published is now copied as it is. Published objects are unchanged.

## 5 October 2026: version preview path of an object that was never published

### Updated

- Fixed: the path of `content/versionview` for an object that was never published shows the folder the object will be
  published in. The preview node of such an object has no node ID, so its path string ends with the parent node and
  `fetchPath()` dropped the parent as the node's own. `\Exponential\View\Kernel\Content\Versionview::previewParentNodes()`
  reads that path including the last element; published objects are unchanged.
- Updated: `eZContentObjectTreeNode::fetchPath()` (the `path` attribute) reads the path of a node without node ID
  including its last element (`pathStringEndsWithParent()`), so a template that reads `$node.path` of the
  preview node gets the folder too; `previewParentNodes()` uses it. Test: `eZContentObjectTreeNodePreviewPathTest`
  (no database).

## 5 October 2026: changing your password

### Added

- `user/password` lists the password's requirements and reports each problem next to its field, with a summary at
  the top that links to the fields. A successful change gets its own success state. The rules are in one class,
  `expPasswordPolicy`. The optional rules are in the new block `site.ini [PasswordSettings]` (lowercase, uppercase,
  digit, symbol, a number of character kinds, not the login, not the current password); all of them are off by
  default, so no site's rules get stricter. Guide: [Changing your password](../../eng-us/features/6.0/modern-password-change.md).
- After a change the session id is renewed. With the database session handler the user's other sessions are ended
  (`EndOtherSessions`, new method `ezpSessionHandler::deleteOtherSessionsOfUser()`, audit event
  `access.session.revoke`), and the mail "Your password was changed" is sent in the essential category `security`
  (`ChangeNotificationMail`, template `design:user/password_changed_mail.tpl`). The mail never contains the
  password.
- Template variables for the modern page: `$password_rules`, `$field_errors`, `$error_summary`, `$password_changed`,
  `$min_length`, `$password_js_config_json` and others. Old templates keep working unchanged. See
  [the compatibility note](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/user-password-change.md).
- Translations: the contexts `kernel/user/password`, `kernel/user/password/js`, `kernel/user/password/mail` and
  `design/standard/user/password_changed_mail`, in English and German.

### Updated

- `user/password` no longer writes typed passwords back into the page (`$oldPassword`, `$newPassword` and
  `$confirmPassword` are always empty). When the current password is right, every problem with the new password is
  reported at once. `access.user.password.change.failed` names the rule that failed. Test: `expPasswordPolicyTest`.

## 5 October 2026: workflow process list

### Updated

- Fixed: `workflow/processlist` lists a waiting workflow process that has no main operation memento, for example one
  deferred to cron. The trigger is read from the child memento, and the main memento was never used; requiring it left
  such processes out, so the list could be empty although workflows were waiting. The grouping is
  `\Exponential\View\Kernel\Workflow\Processlist::processesByTrigger()`.
- Updated: `processesByTrigger()` takes the function that finds a process's trigger as an optional second
  argument (default `Processlist::triggerOfProcess()`, which reads the child memento), looks each trigger up once
  per listing and skips a memento that does not name its operation instead of warning. Test:
  `eZWorkflowProcessGroupingTest` (no database).

## 5 October 2026: more tests, and what they found

### Added

- Unit tests that need no database, in the suites CI runs: the template engine (about 150 sources rendered
  processed and compiled, both must match), every locale of `share/locale`, the input validators, character
  transformations and code pages, `eZMimeType`, `eZPHPCreator`, the PHP error handler of `eZDebug`, the template
  operators `month_overview`, `autolink`, `simpletags`, `alphabet` and `eztoc`, the author, option, range option,
  multi-option and matrix values, ISBN checksums, `toString()`/`fromString()` of the simple datatypes, object name
  patterns, mobile device detection, administration list paging, and the REST routes, prefix filter, request parser
  and OAuth token lookup. `tests/tests/kernel/common` is part of the `kernel-classes` suite. Test counts and line
  coverage: [continuous integration](../../eng-us/specifications/6.0/continuous-integration.md).

### Updated

- A debug message no longer removes the PHP error handler installed below eZDebug's (`setHandleType()` restored a
  handler for `HANDLE_TO_PHP` and `HANDLE_NONE`, which install none).
- A template edited while a process runs (a persistent Velocity worker, a CLI script) is no longer compiled again
  from the tree parsed before the edit; the stale tree had made the new compiled file carry the old template for
  every process.
- `nl2br` gives the same output processed and compiled (`<br />` before each line break).
- `eZLocale::internalCurrency()` uses the currency's thousands separator; a locale code with a charset such as
  `eng-GB.utf-8` keeps its digits; `swe-SE` numbers use the full stop for thousands instead of the comma that is also
  their decimal symbol, so "1,00" is no longer read as 100.
- `eZCharTransform::transform()` sets the charset before naming its cache file; the bork translator no longer warns
  on a text that starts with a placeholder.
- `eZDateTimeValidator` accepts digits only in a time and checks the seconds of a date and time;
  `eZRegExpValidator::fixup()` no longer changes the validator's own rule.
- `eztoc` escapes the header text it prints; `month_overview` no longer warns without a next month.
- A range option with a decimal step no longer loses its last value (0 to 0.3 by 0.1 has four options).
- Mobile device detection sees a WAP type that is the first of the Accept header.
- Object name patterns: several groups in one pattern, the alternative after a group
  (`<(<first_name> <last_name>)|login>`) and a group without values resolve correctly; objects whose class uses
  such a pattern get the corrected name when they are next published.
- REST: a request no longer keeps the API provider and version of the request before it in a persistent worker, and
  `ResponseGroups[]` or `_method[]` in a request no longer end it with a TypeError.
- `sum`, `sub`, `mul` and `div` give the same result processed and compiled, with the fractions of their operands:
  `sum(1.5,2.25)` is 3.75 (it was 3 processed), `div(1,0.5)` is 2; a word or a division by zero in a variable gives 0
  in a compiled template too, where it was a fatal error.
- Compiled `lt`, `le`, `gt` and `ge` compare counts as the processed ones do (array count, string length, the
  number); they compared the raw values, so `lt(array(1),array(2))` and `gt("abc","z")` differed.
- `eZDebug::printReport()` returns the report when `$returnReport` is set, so command line scripts write their debug
  report to STDERR instead of printing it with their output.
- Currency conversion applies shop.ini `[MathSettings] RoundingType` (none, round, ceil, floor) and a type set with
  `setRoundingType()`; every type had come out as none.
- `eZURI::convertFilterString()` writes the converted namefilter back and returns it; a URI's namefilter is decoded
  once, as before.
- `gettime` gives a Sunday its ISO-8601 week number (`date( 'W' )`); it was one higher, week 54 for 3 January 2021.

## 5 October 2026: other sessions end after a password change; the same rules on every password form

### Added

- A sign-in keeps a password stamp in the session (`eZUserPasswordStamp`: a keyed hash of the user id and the
  stored password hash, made with the site secret; never the hash itself). `eZUser::currentUser()` signs out a
  session whose password has changed since, so a password change ends the user's other sessions with every session
  handler. The session that made the change stays signed in. Sessions from before the upgrade, the anonymous user
  and accounts without a password of their own (LDAP, SSO) are not checked. The setting is
  `[PasswordSettings] EndOtherSessions`; the audit event is `access.session.revoke` (reason `password_changed`); the
  user/password page has the new template variable `$other_sessions_signed_out`. Guide:
  [Changing your password](../../eng-us/features/6.0/modern-password-change.md#other-sessions-the-password-stamp).
- Translations: the context `kernel/user/password/account`, in English and German.

### Updated

- Registration, content/edit of a user account, the administration's user edit and `bin/php/resetuserpassword.php`
  check passwords with the rules of user/password (`expPasswordPolicy::accountErrors()`). With the defaults this is
  the length check of before, with the same sentence. The edit form's unchanged password (`_ezpassword`) is no longer
  length-checked, which refused every edit of a user that left the password alone when `MinPasswordLength` was above
  11. A generated console password is generated again until it meets the rules.
- The `UpdateHash` re-hash at sign-in returns the user with the hash it stored, and purges that user's cache, which
  kept the old hash until it expired. See [the compatibility note](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/user-password-change.md). Tests:
  `expPasswordSessionStampTest`, `expPasswordPolicyTest` (PWD-12, PWD-13).

- A session is no longer signed out because a server's OPcache still held an outdated user cache file: a stamp
  that does not match is checked once against the stored row. `eZUser::purgeUserCacheByUserId()` also invalidates
  the cache file in OPcache, so a change made through the site reaches every worker of that server at once; a change
  made on the console reaches a server when its OPcache revalidates (`opcache.revalidate_freq`).

## 5 October 2026: cjw_newsletter 4.2.0

### Added

- The root `composer.json` requires `se7enxweb/cjw_newsletter` `~4.2.0`. The release takes over the features of the
  old eznewsletter extension and builds them on the e-mail preferences of this line: bounces into the suppression
  list, rate limits and batches, subscribe by e-mail; recurring sends, article pools, the approval of editions in the
  collaboration inbox; conditions, interests, an edition in several languages, new skins and plain text views; open
  and click statistics per person only with consent, A/B subject tests; newsletters by SMS with a code double
  opt-in; the CSV import with a column mapping, the subscriber export and the migration from eznewsletter. Guide:
  [cjw_newsletter](../../eng-us/features/6.0/extensions/cjw_newsletter.md#the-features-of-420); upgrade from 4.1: the
  extension's `doc/upgrade-4.2.md`.
- Translations: the e-mail preference categories "Newsletter statistics" and "Newsletters by SMS" have their names
  and descriptions in the context `kernel/mailpreferences/categories`, in English and German.

### Updated

- `expCollaborationInbox::stateOf()` asks the collaboration handler of an item for its state: a handler of another
  type than `ezapprove` with a method `inboxState( $item )` that returns waiting, approved, denied, open or closed sets
  the state of its items, so the approvals of an extension (the newsletter's `cjwnewsletterapproval`) are filtered and
  shown as approvals are. Any other answer, or no such method, keeps the state from the item's status; only active
  handlers are asked. Test: `CollaborationSampleDataTest` (CS-06b).

## 5 October 2026: forwarded headers are trusted only from configured proxies

Upgrade notes, examples for Apache, nginx, Velocity and cloud load balancers:
[Forwarded headers are trusted only from configured proxies](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/trusted-proxies.md).

### Added

- `site.ini [HTTPHeaderSettings] TrustedProxies[]`: the proxies whose forwarded headers are believed, as addresses and
  ranges, IPv4 and IPv6. Default `127.0.0.1` and `::1`, a proxy on the same machine; an empty list trusts none.
- `eZTrustedProxy` (`lib/ezutils/classes/eztrustedproxy.php`): address and range matching, the right-to-left walk of
  `X-Forwarded-For`, the trusted hop count. `eZSys::trustedProxies()` and `eZSys::isFromTrustedProxy()`.
- Tests: `eZTrustedProxyTest` and `eZSysTrustedProxyTest` (suite `lib`): a visitor's `X-Forwarded-Proto: https` is not
  HTTPS, a trusted proxy's is; spoofed `X-Forwarded-For` chains; CIDR ranges in IPv4 and IPv6; invalid addresses; an
  empty and a missing setting.

### Updated

- `eZSys::isSSLNow()` reads `X-Forwarded-Proto`, `X-Forwarded-Port` and `X-Forwarded-Server` only when `REMOTE_ADDR`
  is a trusted proxy. Before, any visitor could make a plain HTTP request count as HTTPS (absolute URLs, redirects,
  `CookieSecure=auto`, cache keys with the scheme). `$_SERVER['HTTPS']` and the port are checked first, as before.
- `eZSys::hostname()` uses `X-Forwarded-Host` only from a trusted proxy.
- `eZSys::clientIP()` with `ClientIpByCustomHTTPHeader` reads the header only from a trusted proxy, and from the right:
  the first address that is not a trusted proxy is the visitor, every entry checked with `FILTER_VALIDATE_IP`. Before,
  it took the left-most entry, which the visitor writes, so `DebugByIP`, the sign-in lockout's `TrustedIPList`, the
  audit log and the consent log could be given any address.
- The HTTP cache (`httpcache.ini`) works out scheme and host as the kernel does: the contract carries the trusted list
  and the lookup believes the forwarded headers only from a trusted `REMOTE_ADDR`. Test: HC-15, HC-16.
- The debug bar's address test reports the proxy header as used only for a request from a trusted proxy.
- Installation guide: §8.8 "Behind a reverse proxy" and §13.10 "Behind a proxy: trusted headers" describe the setting.

## 5 October 2026: saved settings files are no longer world-writable

Upgrade note, with the one-time fix for files saved before:
[Settings files saved through the admin are no longer world-writable](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/ini-save-file-permissions.md).

### Added

- `config.php` constant `EZP_INI_SAVE_FILE_PERMISSION` (default `0640`): the mode of a settings file a save creates.
  `eZINI::newSaveFileMode()`, `eZINI::saveFileMode()`, `eZINI::saveFileOwnership()`.
- Tests: `eZINISaveFilePermissionsTest` (suite `lib`), and the mode of a new file in `expIniEditorTest` INI-06.

### Updated

- `eZINI::save()` keeps the mode, owner (as root) and group of the file it replaces, without write for other users,
  and gives a new file `0640` with the owner and group of its directory, or of the nearest one above it that root does
  not own. Before, every save made the file `0666`, `settings/override/site.ini.append.php` with the database password
  included. New settings directories are `0755` instead of `0777`; the temporary file is `0600` until it is in place.
- `expIniEditor` creates a new settings file `0640` with the same owner rule (was `0644`) and keeps its temporary file
  `0600` until it is in place.
- INI cache files (`var/cache/ini`) are `0644` instead of `0666`; `EZP_INI_FILE_PERMISSION` now applies to them and to
  generated autoload files only.
- `config.php-RECOMMENDED` and the installation guide §13.11 describe both constants.

## 5 October 2026: eng-US instead of eng-GB as the content language fallback

Upgrade note: [Content language fallback: eng-US and a setting instead of a hard-coded eng-GB](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/content-object-fallback-locale.md).

### Added

- `site.ini [RegionalSettings] ContentObjectFallbackLocale` (default `eng-US`): the locale class and attribute names
  are stored in when no content language is prioritized and `ContentObjectLocale` is not set.
  `eZSerializedObjectNameList::fallbackLanguageLocale()` and `configuredLanguageLocale()`.
- Tests: `eZSerializedObjectNameListFallbackLocaleTest` (suite `kernel-classes`, no database).

### Updated

- `eZSerializedObjectNameList::defaultLanguageLocale()` falls back to that setting, then `eng-US`; it was a hard-coded
  `eng-GB`, which left class descriptions in a language the site did not have.
- The default `site.ini [RegionalSettings] ContentObjectLocale` is `eng-US` (was `eng-GB`).
- The SQL clean data (`kernel/sql/common/cleandata.sql`, `kernel/sql/sqlite/cleandata.sql`) is in `eng-US`, like
  `share/db_data.dba`.
- `kickstart.ini--example` no longer adds `eng-GB` as a second language; `kickstart.ini-dist` shows `eng-US`.
- The subitems column "Language codes" gives `eng-US, ger-DE` as its example; the micro benchmark's in-memory class
  attributes use `eng-US`.
- The multisite installer packages name the folder attributes in the configured locale, else the kernel's fallback,
  never `eng-GB`.

## 5 October 2026: personal API keys and the REST administration

Guide: [Personal API keys](../../eng-us/guides/api-keys.md). Upgrade note: [Personal API keys: a table, a module and a policy](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/api-keys.md).

### Added

- **Personal API keys**: a signed-in user with the new policy `apikey/create` makes keys on **My account > API
  access** (`apikey/list`): a name, scopes from what the user's own policies allow, a lifetime. The key
  (`expk_<id>_<secret>`) is shown once; only an HMAC-SHA-256 hash with a salt per key is stored. A REST request with
  `Authorization: Bearer expk_...` runs as the owner, limited to the key's scopes, the owner's policies and, for write
  routes, the owner's rights on the node. Last use and address are noted at most once a minute; a rate limit per key
  is counted in APCu.
- Table `expapikey` in every schema (`share/db_schema.dba`, MySQL, PostgreSQL, SQLite) and in the 6.0.0 to 6.0.15
  update files (created only when missing); `update/common/scripts/6.0/createapikeytable.php` for any engine.
- Module `apikey` (view `list`, policy `create` with a `Scope` limitation, fetch functions `can_create` and
  `counts`); `rest.ini [ApiKeySettings]`.
- **Setup > oAuth admin > API keys** (`oauthadmin/keys`): every key with owner, scopes, last use and status, a
  search, a status filter, one user's keys (`/(user)/<id>`), revoking with a confirmation (`oauthadmin/keyaction`).
  The user settings page links there.
- Audit events `access.apikey.create`, `access.apikey.revoke`, `access.apikey.use` (the first use) and
  `access.apikey.use.failed` (with the reason); the catalogue has 140 names.
- Tests: `expApiKeyTest` and `ApiKeySchemaSqlTest` (suite `kernel-classes`, no database); `ApiKeyLiveTest` (group
  `network-live`: makes, uses, refuses and revokes keys over HTTP on Apache and Velocity).

### Updated

- The REST applications pages (`oauthadmin/list`, `view`, `edit`, the removal confirmation) are redesigned in the
  look of the cronjobs page: figures, a card per application with its users and valid tokens, the secret folded away.
  Every address, form field and button is unchanged.
- A new REST application gets a client identifier and secret from `random_bytes()` (was `md5` of the name and
  `uniqid()`), and removing an application removes its authorizations, tokens and codes. Client secrets are compared
  in constant time.
- HTTP basic authentication of the REST interface (`ezpRestBasicAuthStyle`) checks the password with the user's own
  hash type through `eZUser::authenticateHash()`. It compared `md5("login\npassword")` in SQL, so every user on a
  modern hash was refused. A wrong password counts as a failed login.
- The REST layer reads `Authorization: Bearer <token>` as well as `OAuth <token>`, and answers `insufficient_scope`
  with 403 instead of 401 (the status is sent after the `WWW-Authenticate` challenge, which PHP turns into a 401).
- The form token filter keeps a view's `Cache-Control: no-store` instead of replacing it with its own
  `private, no-cache, must-revalidate`.

## 6 October 2026: REST permission checks and routes

Guide: [Permissions of the REST interface](../../eng-us/guides/api-keys.md#32-permissions-of-the-rest-interface).

### Added

- `expRestContentPermission` (`kernel/private/rest/classes/auth/content_permission.php`): one check of the current
  user's rights for a REST read, create, edit or remove, done as the content module does it (`canRead()`,
  `checkAccess( 'create', class, parent class, language )`, `canEdit()`, `canRemove()` of every location and of the
  subtree). The `ezprestapi` content controller (1.2.5) asks it before every call, for OAuth tokens, personal API keys,
  basic authentication and the anonymous user alike, and answers `403 access_denied` with the policy named; the API key
  write guard asks the same helper. Before, an OAuth token could create and remove content where its user had no
  `content/create` or `content/remove`.
- `ezpRestVersionedRoute` takes a list of versions (`array( 1, 2 )`) and `getVersions()`; a URL it generates keeps the
  version of the current request. `ezpMvcRailsRoute::anyMethod()` builds a map that runs one action for every method.

### Updated

- The REST router tries the next route when one matches the path but not the method, and answers `405` with an
  `Allow` header listing every matching route's methods only when none takes it.
- The authentication routes (`/auth/oauth/login`, `/auth/http-basic-auth`) and the error route answer every method:
  a `POST` or `DELETE` without a valid token, or refused by a key's scope, answered `405` instead of `401` or `403`,
  which made `/api/ezp/v2/content/node/create` and `.../delete/<id>` look unreachable.
- The REST answer cache keeps one entry per user and only for `GET` and `HEAD`: one user's read was served to another
  for ten minutes past `content/read`, and a repeated create or delete was answered from the cache without running.
  It keeps no refusals: a cached `403` could not be read back (its status object has no `__set_state()`), so the
  second identical refused read answered 500, on both web servers since they share the cache. A cache file that
  cannot be read back now counts as expired.
- The `content/create` check of the REST interface takes the parent class from the parent object (it asked the node
  for an attribute it does not have, logged an error and checked the ParentClass limitation against nothing).
- `ezprestapi` 1.2.5: reads answer at `/api/ezp/v1/...` again (404 since the extension took the `ezp` provider over
  from `ezprestapiprovider`; existing clients such as a mobile app read through `v1`), writes at `v2`; delete takes
  `DELETE` as well as `POST`; create answers `201` and delete `200` with the ids (a delete that worked answered 500).
  Velocity needs `exp:velocity deploy --kernel` for the kernel classes.

## 6 October 2026: access and view cache filters for extensions

### Added

- Five filters of `ezpEvent` through which an extension takes part where it had to patch the kernel; for the four
  access filters only `true` allows. Guide: [Access and view cache filters](../../eng-us/features/6.0/access-and-cache-filters.md).
  - `content/view/cachekeys`: the keys of a view cache file name, now named (`userroles`, `userlimitedlist`,
    `viewparameters`, ...), with the user and the node; the file names do not change.
  - `content/download/access`: the answer of `content/download`, with the object, the attribute and the version. That
    the attribute belongs to the object stays checked first.
  - `content/edit/access`: the new `eZContentObject::editAccess( $version, $language )`, which `content/edit`,
    `content/history`, `content/removeeditversion`, `content/versionview` and the upload and tag dialogs of the
    online editor use for their edit check.
  - `collaboration/item/access`: whether the current user may open a collaboration item; the template gets
    `is_participant`, and only a participant files the item in a group of the inbox.
  - `content/notification/create`: whether the publish operation creates the notification event.
- The events are described in the settings extension wizard. Test: `expPermissionHooksTest` (no database).

### Updated

- Every edit check of `content/edit` (a new draft, the choice of a language or of a draft, the check of one version),
  of `content/multiedit` and of the REST interface goes through `content/edit/access`, so a listener decides the same
  way on every path; `eZContentObject::filterEditAccess()` passes any edit answer of the kernel through the filter,
  and without listeners no time is spent on it (`ezpEvent::hasListeners()`).
- `content/download` refuses the attribute of another object or version before any listener is asked.
- `collaboration/action` acted on any item it was posted, for anyone (a comment on an item the user does not take
  part in; a missing item was a fatal error). It now acts only on an existing item of the posted type that the user
  may open, which `collaboration/item/access` decides.
- `content/view/cachekeys`: node, view mode, language, offset and layout always stay; non-scalar values are left out.
- A debug notice says when a listener changed an access answer.
- The settings extension wizard writes listeners of these filters with their arguments and a commented example that
  reads its rule from the extension's settings.
- The guide explains what a listener can narrow and widen, where edit access is asked and where not, with worked
  examples.

## 6 October 2026: version preview in other view modes

### Added

- `content/versionview` shows a version in another view mode with the parameter `(view_mode)`, for example
  `content/versionview/<object>/<version>/<language>/(view_mode)/print` for `node/view/print.tpl` in a print or PDF
  layout. The view modes allowed are listed in the new setting `content.ini [VersionView] ViewModes[]` (`full` by
  default); another one answers "not available". Without the parameter nothing changes. The view mode is the
  `viewmode` design key of the preview and the template variable `view_mode`; `design/standard` renders the node in
  it, and the admin designs pass it on to the preview frame. Test: `eZContentVersionviewViewModeTest` (no database).
- `design/standard/templates/node/view/print.tpl`, a print view of a node (its name and the attributes with content),
  as an example for `ViewModes[]=print`.

### Updated

- With more than one view mode listed, the View control box of the preview (admin, admin3, admin4) offers them, and
  **Update view** keeps the chosen one; it went back to `full` before.
- Only a name of letters, digits, `_` and `-` is a view mode, whatever the setting lists.
- Guide: [Version preview in other view modes](../../eng-us/features/6.0/version-preview-view-modes.md).

## 6 October 2026: Cancel in user/edit

### Updated

- Fixed: Cancel in `user/edit` goes back where the user came from: the page the form names in `RedirectIfDiscarded`
  (as in `content/edit` and `user/register`), else the page viewed last, else the sitemap of the users. It always
  went to `content/view/sitemap/5`, also for an editor changing their own account on the site. A host that
  `site.ini [SiteSettings] AllowedRedirectHosts` does not list is refused as before. Test: `eZUserEditCancelTest`
  (no database).
- Updated: One set of rules for every redirect target from outside the code, in `eZRedirectManager::safeURI()` and
  `unsafeReason()`: a path of this site or an allowed host; never a backslash (`/\evil.example`, `\\evil.example`), a
  control character (CR/LF), a scheme other than http(s) (`javascript:`, `data:`), a user in the URL, or the
  percent-encoded forms of these (`%2F%2Fevil.example`). `eZModule::redirectTo()` answers 403 to a target that breaks
  them ("Redirection requested to an unsafe address"); before, a backslash or `javascript:` target of `user/login`,
  `content/action` or `user/edit` went out as the `Location`. `eZRedirectManager::redirectURI()` checks its preferred
  URI and the page from the session the same way; `eZModule::allowedRedirectHosts()` is public.
- Updated: Cancel and Discard go back to the page the form was opened from in `user/password`, `user/setting`,
  `section/edit`, `state/edit`, `state/group_edit`, `role/edit`, `class/edit` and `class/groupedit`, as in
  `user/edit`: the form's `RedirectIfDiscarded`, else the page viewed last, else where they always went.
  `eZRedirectManager::returnURI()` never returns the running view (a loop), a view of the new
  `site.ini [SiteSettings] DisallowedReturnViews` (`content/action`, `user/logout`, `content/download`, ...) or a page
  viewed last that the user can no longer view. The views set `redirect_if_discarded` and their standard, admin and
  admin4 templates send it; `user/edit` in the standard design has a Cancel button.
- Updated: `user/password` checks its `RedirectOnCancel` and `RedirectURI` (both redirected to as posted before),
  `user/register` its `RedirectIfDiscarded` (used as the default before), and Discard in `content/edit` the page kept
  in the session; `content/edit_redirection.tpl` escapes the two hidden fields it writes.
- Added: The page [Safe redirects and "back to where you came from"](../../eng-us/features/6.0/safe-redirects.md), and
  `eZRedirectManagerSafeURITest` (no database): the rules case by case, and the Cancel target of every view.
  Velocity needs `exp:velocity deploy --kernel` for the kernel classes.

## 6 October 2026: event listeners without a callback

### Updated

- Fixed: an entry of `site.ini [Event] Listeners[]` that is not of the form `<event>@<callback>` (no `@`, or an empty
  event or callback) is logged as an error and skipped. It raised the PHP warning "Undefined array key 1" on every
  request and attached a listener that could never be called, or one to an event named ''. A callback is split off
  at the first `@` only. `[RunnableSettings] Listeners[]` already skipped such entries. Test:
  `ezpKernelResultAndEventTest` (no database).
- Blanks around the event or the callback of an entry are dropped (`content/view @ myClass::method` attached a
  listener to the event `content/view ` that was never sent). A callback that is not written as the name of a
  function or of a `Class::method` (a typo such as `:::` or a blank inside) is logged and skipped without being looked
  up. The same entry listed twice, by the site and by an extension say, is attached once; it ran twice for every
  event.
- A mistake in the entries, and a listener whose class, method or function does not exist, is logged once in the life
  of the PHP process instead of once per request or per event; under Velocity a worker logged it on every request.

## 6 October 2026: content policy limitations of extensions

### Added

- An extension can add a policy limitation of its own to a content function (`content/read`, `content/edit`, ...).
  The filter `module/functionlist` (new, in `eZModule::initialize()`) adds it to the function list, so the role editor
  offers it; a class implementing `ezpContentLimitationHandler`, named in the new setting
  `site.ini [RoleSettings] LimitationHandlers[<limitation>]`, evaluates it in the `checkAccess()` methods of objects,
  nodes and versions, in the SQL of list and tree fetches (`permissionSQL()`) and for subtree notifications. Guide:
  [Content policy limitations of extensions](../../eng-us/features/6.0/content-limitation-handlers.md); listed in
  **Setup > RAD**.
- `checkAccess()` of `eZContentObject`, `eZContentObjectTreeNode` and `eZContentObjectVersion` and
  `eZContentObject::draftCreateAccess()` take the user to check for as the last argument (`$userID`, default the
  current user); `eZUser::accessUser()` returns that user. An ID that is no user gets no access.

### Updated

- A limitation the kernel does not know and no handler evaluates denies everywhere. Fetches ignored it and listed
  objects whose pages `checkAccess()` refused; subtree notifications ignored it and sent the mail.
- `eZContentObjectVersion::checkAccess()` stops at the first limitation of a policy that denies; a `Language` that did
  not match, or a limitation it did not know, could be allowed again by the next limitation.
- Subtree notifications check the `Group` limitation ("Self group") of `content/read`.
- A class that overrides one of the `checkAccess()` methods must accept the new argument; see
  [behaviour changes](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-10.md). Tests: `ezpContentLimitationTest`,
  `eZContentPermissionSQLTest` (no database), `eZContentAccessForUserLiveTest` (live).

## 6 October 2026: content limitation handlers, hardened and extended

Guide: [Content policy limitations of extensions](../../eng-us/features/6.0/content-limitation-handlers.md).

### Added

- `exp:access:check` (`bin/php/accesscheck.php`): whether a user (ID, login or `anonymous`) may read, edit, remove ...
  a node or object, and which policy and limitation refused it, with the handler of an extension limitation or the
  note that none evaluates it; ALLOWED or DENIED, or JSON. `expContentAccessReport::check()` gives the answer to PHP.
- `eZContentObject::editAccess()` and `filterEditAccess()` take the user as a further argument: that user's
  `content/edit` and `user/selfedit`, and the filter `content/edit/access` gets that user's ID.
- The settings extension wizard writes a working limitation handler (`<class>MaxDepth`) next to a listener of
  `module/functionlist`, with the commented `[RoleSettings] LimitationHandlers[]` line; Setup > RAD points at it.
- A limitation added through `module/functionlist` may carry a `label`, which the role screens show.
- `permissionSQL()` may answer `array( 'column' => ..., 'values' => ..., 'type' => 'int'|'string' )`; the kernel writes
  the `IN` statement and casts or escapes the values.

### Updated

- Fixed: a handler that is missing, does not implement the interface, cannot be made, or throws in `checkAccess()` or
  `permissionSQL()` denies and is logged once per request. An exception ended the request (a list page with one
  such object answered 500), and a missing handler was looked up and logged for every node of a list.
- Fixed: a string condition from `permissionSQL()` must be self-contained (quotes and parentheses balanced; no `;`
  or comment outside quoted strings): `1 = 1 ) OR ( 1 = 1` closed the parentheses it was put in and gave every user
  of the role the whole tree in fetches. Any answer that is not `false`, such a string or a column condition denies.
- The handler of a limitation is made once per request and again for the next one, also in a persistent Velocity
  worker, and when `LimitationHandlers[]` changes. Handlers get the values as a list of strings.
- The listeners of `module/functionlist` run once per module and request, not on every module lookup; only when one
  is attached. What they return is kept where it has the form of `module.php`; a listener that throws or returns no
  list leaves the module its own functions instead of ending every request. The RAD survey lists the event again.
- `eZUser::accessUser()` accepts a positive whole number only, gives the current user's own object for its ID and no
  user for a disabled account, and keeps other users for the request (forgotten by the next request,
  `eZUser::cleanupCache()` and a purge of that user's cache).
- The role screens (role view and editor, policy lists of the node and user views, policy forms; admin, admin4 and
  standard designs) show a limitation's label, washed, with "(no handler, denies)" next to a content limitation no
  handler evaluates, and the stored values of one whose extension is gone. The editors store only values they
  offered; a single value posted instead of a list no longer ends the request in `in_array()`.
- The values of the Class, Section, Node and object state limitations are cast to integers in the fetch SQL.
- `expservices` decides edit access to content as `content/edit` and the REST interface do: object updates through
  `editAccess()` in the language written, price and community item changes through `filterEditAccess()`.
- Fixed: the stand-in object of `expRestContentPermissionTest` takes the user argument of `checkAccess()`; PHP refused
  to load the test since the handlers came in. The ezjscore module definition reads its function list without
  `ezjscore.ini`, and two `expservices` methods mark their optional arguments nullable (PHP 8.4).
- Tests: `ezpContentLimitationTest` (CL-11 to CL-18), `eZContentPermissionSQLTest`, `eZContentAccessForAnotherUserTest`,
  `eZPolicyLimitationLabelsAndValuesTest`, `expRadWizardListsTest` (no database). Velocity needs
  `exp:velocity deploy --kernel` for the kernel classes.

## 6 October 2026: host matching by the start of a host, and a redirect to the language for addresses without one

### Added

- The host map of siteaccess matching (`MatchOrder=host`, `HostMatchType=map`) can match a host by its start, end or
  a part, as `host_uri` already could: the new setting `site.ini [SiteAccessSettings] HostMatchMethod` (`strict` by
  default, as before) or a third field of an entry, `HostMatchMapItems[]=www.example.com;site;start`. With `start`,
