  `--a4-cell-pad-y`/`-x` (`.a4-table`), `--a4-card-pad-y`/`-x` (`.a4-card`); `html[data-a4-density="compact"]` sets
  only them and `--a4-control-h` anew (`.25em`, 4/8 px, 8/12 px, 28 px). Every target keeps 24 px (WCAG 2.5.8); the
  content tree keeps its row pitch. The normal density looks as before.
- Test: `AdminDensityTest` (the compact block sets only density tokens, compact controls stay at 24 px or more, the
  tree is not tightened, tables and cards read the tokens, the switch is a pressed button applied in the head script,
  the texts are translated; mutation-checked).

## 14 October 2026: the admin4 dashboard's figures

### Updated
- The key figures of the admin4 dashboard (`content/dashboard.tpl`) have an icon tile each (Bootstrap Icons 1.13.1:
  new `graph-up-arrow`, `person-check`, `arrow-up-right`, `arrow-down-right`, `dash` and their `.a4-bi-*` classes),
  and a figure that is a link shows an arrow in its corner. Tiles are at least 180px wide, one column on a phone.
- "Published in the last 7 days" says how its full days compare with the 6 days before them: `+5 against the 6 days before, without today` (today is not over, so the trend leaves it out), with an
  arrow, in the muted text colour (a rise is not always good). Both weeks are summed from the 14 days the chart
  already counts, so the dashboard runs one query fewer than before.
- The chart's numbers fold out under it as a table ("Show the numbers"), so the data is there in words for everyone,
  not only as bar heights.
- New strings in design/admin/dashboard (ger-DE, eng-US). Test: `AdminDashboardTest`.

## 14 October 2026: admin4 left menu groups

### Added
- menu.ini can group a long left menu: `Groups[]` (order), `GroupNames[<group>]` (title), `GroupLinks_<group>[]`
  (link keys). admin4's `parts/ini_menu.tpl` draws each group as a `<details>` with a small-capital title
  (`summary.a4-nav-title`, a caret that turns), open unless the user closed it (`admin4.js` keeps the choice per
  browser in localStorage) and always open when it holds the current page; links in no group follow under "More".
  Leftmenu_setup is grouped into Content model, Site and URLs, Workflows, Users and messages and System. Without
  `Groups[]`, or with `Groups[]` reset to no values, a menu is one list, as before; a link named in two groups shows
  in the first; the access checks per link are unchanged.
- The current page's link in a left menu carries `aria-current="page"` and takes the accent tint with a straight bar on
  its left (square left corners; a Highlight edge in forced colors); a plain menu's list is named by its heading (`aria-labelledby`). The
  links are drawn by the new include `parts/ini_menu_items.tpl`.
- The left column is a navigation landmark named "Side menu" (`page_leftmenu.tpl`).
- New strings: "Side menu" (design/admin/pagelayout), the group titles and "More" (design/admin/parts/setup/menu),
  ger-DE and eng-US.
- Test: `AdminLeftMenuGroupsTest` (every setup link in exactly one group, every group named, the details markup and
  the flat fallback, the unchanged access checks, aria-current and the current link's square left corners, the
  landmark, the stored state never closing the
  current page's group).

## 15 October 2026: a flat admin4 header

### Changed
- admin4's header is one row of 56 px from 1280 px: logo and site link, the main menu, the search, light/dark, the
  user menu and logout. From 576 px it is two rows (48 px for the logo and the tools, 40 px for the menu), on a
  phone one row of 56 px with the phone menu as before. It was 145 px at 1320 px with the menu of a few extensions:
  the tabs wrapped onto two lines and "Logout: admin" took a third.
- The main menu stays on one line at every width from 576 px. The tabs that do not fit go, in their order, into a
  "More" list at the row's end (a disclosure: `aria-expanded`, `aria-controls`; arrow keys, Home, End and Escape),
  which looks like the selected tab while the current page's tab is in it. A ResizeObserver refits the row. Without
  the script the row scrolls sideways.
- Logout is an icon button of its own (`box-arrow-right`) beside the user menu, named "Logout (admin)" in its
  `aria-label` and tooltip, on every width. The new user menu (person icon) shows the user's name and login and the
  links of the "Current user" box (change information, change password, e-mail preferences), which stays in the
  right column. On edit pages both are drawn disabled, as the menu tabs are. The markup is the new include
  `parts/a4_header_user.tpl`; admin4l's `admin_theme_switch.tpl` includes it, and its `admin_tab_menu.tpl` has the
  "More" item instead of the logout link.
- The header search is 36 px high (its button was 43 px) and narrower from 1280 px (9 to 15 rem); "/" outside a
  field puts the focus in it (`aria-keyshortcuts="/"`).
- `--header-height` lives on the root element: admin4.css sets the drawn heights as defaults per width and admin4.js
  sets the measured height on `<html>` instead of `.dashboard-flex`, so the side columns, the sticky edit bar and
  `scroll-margin-top` read one value.
- The header's cache blocks (admin4 and admin4l `pagelayout.tpl`) are keyed by the user's object id as well: the
  header names the signed-in user.
- New strings: "Logout (%login)" and "User menu (%name)" (design/admin/pagelayout), ger-DE and eng-US. New icons:
  `box-arrow-right.svg`, `chevron-down.svg` (Bootstrap Icons 1.13.1), classes `.a4-bi-box-arrow-right` and
  `.a4-bi-chevron-down`.
- Test: `AdminHeaderTest` (the rows and their heights, the "More" disclosure and its script, logout outside the user
  menu with its name, the cache keys, the "/" key, no literal colours in the new rules).

### Upgrade notes
- A site that overrides `page_topmenu.tpl` keeps its header. To take the new one, add
  `{include uri='design:parts/a4_header_user.tpl'}` and the `li.a4-nav-more` item of the new template; the old
  `#header-usermenu-logout` id is now on the logout icon.
- Clear the template cache after the update: the header's cache blocks have a new key.

## 15 October 2026: admin4's content edit forms take its components

### Changed
- The attribute forms of content/edit (also translate, multiedit, user registration and the template look editor)
  draw the datatype templates' legacy markup as admin4's components, from `modules/content.css`: inside an attribute
  (`.a4-content-attr`) every text field, select and textarea looks like `.a4-input` (32 px, 14 px text, the field
  edge, the ring on focus, a drawn chevron on a one-line select), every `input.button`, `input.button-disabled`,
  `button.button` and `input.defaultbutton` like `.a4-btn` (outlined, the main action filled, a disabled button faded
  instead of hatched), a checkbox or radio like in `.a4-check` (18 px in the accent), a file field's button like a
  button. The form itself carries `.a4-content-edit`, so the buttons of the left column and the relations box take
  the same look; the sticky button bars keep their own rules. Forced colors get the native select back and keep the
  main action's thicker edge; touch screens get 16 px field text.
- The attribute's name is a `label` or `legend` of the class `.a4-content-attr-label` (13 px, semibold) without the
  trailing colon; a grouped input (author, matrix, date and time, image, user account ...) is a fieldset of the class
  `.a4-content-attr-group` without a box or grey background of its own; the parts of an attribute take the
  `.a4-label` look (12 px, semibold) above their field, and a datatype template that still floats its parts
  (`div.block div.element`) has them in a row.
- admin4 carries its own edit templates of the date, date and time, time, URL, price, image, file, option and user
  account datatypes: their parts stand in a row (`.a4-content-parts`) with a label above each field and no colon,
  headings without a control of their own are `.a4-label` paragraphs, the calendar is an `.a4-btn-icon` with the new
  icon class `.a4-bi-calendar3` instead of `calendar_icon.png` (admin4's `theme/rounded.css` drops its `#page .datepicker-icon` rule, which the button no longer carries), the account status is a badge, the option list's
  columns name its fields and an empty option list is an `.a4-empty`. The left column's "Base translation on" radios
  are `.a4-check` rows. admin4 also carries `ezmedia`, `ezmultioption`, `ezrangeoption`, `ezmultiprice`,
  `ezinisetting`, `ezpackage` and `ezobjectrelationlist`: no colons, parts in a row, list fields named by their
  columns, the relation list's radio and checkbox lists as `.a4-check` rows and its invert-selection control as an
  icon button instead of `toggle-button-16x16.gif`, its "create new object" field with a label and shown through the
  `hidden` attribute instead of `style`, empty lists as `.a4-empty`, the INI setting warning as a status.
- The text of the rich text editors (TinyMCE 3 and 8) follows admin4's light and dark mode: `admin4.js` adds
  `components.css` and the new `editor_content.css` (named by the edit form's `data-a4-editor-css`) to each editor's
  document and copies `html[data-a4-theme]` there; text, links, literal blocks, custom tags and embeds take the
  tokens. The editor's toolbar keeps its skin.
  **Upgrade:** an override of one of these datatype templates in another design keeps working. An extension's own
  datatype template inside the edit form now gets the field and button look; one that wants to keep its own look
  gives its controls a class with more weight than `.a4-content-attr input` (one class plus the element).
- `AdminContentEditFormsTest` checks the rules and the templates (the copied datatype templates post the same fields, ids and label targets as design/standard's); `AdminKeyboardTest` and `AdminFormErrorsTest`
  follow the new classes.
- The label of an attribute in admin4's edit form is the label of its field (`for` the field's id
  `ezcoa-<class attribute id>_<identifier>`, in the edit and the translation branch), so a text line, text block,
  number or e-mail field has an accessible name of its own; a grouped input keeps its fieldset and legend. Test:
  `AdminFormLabelsTest`.
- design.ini `[AdminDesignSettings] IconList[]` follows the mask rules of components.css (the width switch's and the
  date fields' icons in, the removed compact view's icon out).

## 15 October 2026: one density in admin4

### Removed
- The compact view of admin4 and its header switch ("Compact view"): admin4 has one density, tuned for 1440 to
  1920 px windows. Removed: the button in `page_topmenu.tpl` and admin4l's `admin_theme_switch.tpl`, the head script
  that applied `html[data-a4-density]`, the `html[data-a4-density="compact"]` token block and the switch's styles,
  the class `.a4-bi-view-stacked` (the icon file stays: `theme/rounded.css` uses it).
  **Upgrade:** nothing to do. A signed-in user's eZPreference `admin_density` is no longer read and does nothing
  (delete it with `DELETE FROM ezpreferences WHERE name = 'admin_density'` if you like); a value an anonymous page
  kept in the browser (`localStorage` "exp-admin4-density") is removed on the next page. The strings "Compact view"
  and "Switch between normal and compact view" stay in the translation files, unused. An extension that styled
  `html[data-a4-density="compact"]` keeps its rule, which no longer applies.

### Changed
- One control height: `.a4-btn`, `.a4-input` and the fields of `.a4-field`, `.a4-tabs`, `.a4-chip`, the pager sizes
  and the confirm summary take `--a4-control-h` (32 px; they were 30 to 38 px), with 4 px block padding. The legacy
  text fields take the field text size (`--a4-text-m`, 14 px) like `.a4-input`.
- `table.list` cells are 8 px above and below like `.a4-table` cells and 6 px a side (`--a4-list-pad`, was `.4em`, about 5 px): a trash or class list of ten columns still fits a 944 px content column.
- No line between blocks: the dashed rule above every `.box-header` (theme/rounded.css) and admin4's hairline are
  gone, in the side columns, the node's children and every box; blocks in a side column are 24 px (`--a4-gutter`)
  apart (the header's padding, so no margins collapse), the width switch under a left menu too. The right column is `--a4-right-width` (16rem) wide, so its card
  keeps 208 px beside the gutter.
- The density tokens (`--a4-gutter`, `--a4-control-h`, `--a4-list-pad`, `--a4-cell-pad-*`, `--a4-card-pad-*`) keep
  one value each in the token block. Card padding is unchanged (12/16 px).

## 14 October 2026: each admin links to its own site

### Added
- `site.ini [SiteSettings] FrontSiteAccess`: the site an admin siteaccess belongs to. Its "Open the site" link
  (admin3, admin4, admin4l) and the node view's "View on site" go there; empty (the default) keeps
  `DefaultAccess`. Before, every admin of an installation that serves several sites from one tree linked to the
  same default site. Set it in the admin siteaccess's `site.ini.append.php`. The template operator
  `siteaccess_url()` without a parameter now calls the new `ezpSiteAccessURL::front()`; with a parameter, and
  `ezpSiteAccessURL::root()`, nothing changes. A siteaccess that is not in `AvailableSiteAccessList` falls back to
  `DefaultAccess` with a debug warning. The header cache blocks key on the address, so each admin caches its own
  link. Compiled templates still call the old code until the template cache is cleared: after updating, clear the
  caches of each admin siteaccess (`php bin/php/ezcache.php --clear-all -s <admin siteaccess>`). Tests:
  `ezpSiteAccessURLTest` (`front()` without, with and with an unknown `FrontSiteAccess`, padded value),
  `eZKernelTemplateOperatorsTest` (the compiled template calls `front()`).

## 14 October 2026: the admin4 accessibility series, merged and refined

The 24 changes above (icons, language badges, context menus, landmarks, keyboard, tree view, form names, contrast,
focus, modes, scale, tables, lists, layers, form errors, page header, notices, mode switch, timeline, density,
dashboard, cards, menu groups, front site access) were merged together and refined:

### Added
- `window.a4Pref` (the first script of `page_head_style.tpl`) and `design:parts/a4_preference_attributes.tpl`: the
  frame's remembered choices are the signed-in user's eZPreferences, written on `<html>` before the page is drawn:
  `admin_color_mode` (light, dark, system), `admin_density` (normal, compact), `admin_left_sidebar` and
  `admin_right_sidebar` (the columns on a wide window), `admin_navgroups` (closed left menu groups). An anonymous
  page keeps them in the browser only; a choice the browser held before is taken along once. Test:
  `AdminPreferencesTest`.
- `window.a4CardBusy( card, on, message )` (admin4.js): a busy card sets `aria-busy` and `inert` together and
  announces a short message in a polite live region.
- Layer tokens `--a4-z-skip-link`, `--a4-z-controlbar-top`, `--a4-z-card-veil`, `--a4-z-card-spinner`; the frame
  tokens `--a4-explayouts-nav`, `--a4-explayouts-nav-edge` and `--a4-pw-toggle-room` (each with its dark value).

### Updated
- admin4l (the admin siteaccess): its page layout has the skip link, the landmarks, the named side and top menus,
  the width grip as a focusable separator and the toast regions of admin4; its header blocks are copies of admin4's
  top menu again (Bootstrap icons, column tabs, mode and density switches), and `AdminIconsTest` keeps them in step.
- No text on an admin4 page is below 12px: `core.css` sets the body to `--a4-text-s` (13px) and every em size of
  the base and page stylesheets that would land below 12px takes `--a4-text-xs`. The styling test keeps no color
  or spacing exception list any more.
- Tables: the zebra's row filter sits in `:where()`, so a hovered or selected row shows its state; compact list
  rows keep 24px targets.
- Context menus: Enter runs an item without a link too, a letter moves to the next item starting with it, the title
  is no item of the menu, the tree's menu buttons are named "Actions for <node>", and the class and node icon menus
  carry `aria-haspopup` before their first use.
- The content tree's buttons name a node as it is called (no doubled entities for umlauts or `&`), its items name
  Shift+F10 in `aria-keyshortcuts`; the node tabs move with Arrow Left and Right only; each calendar button is named
  after its field.
- Language badge links: the accessible name starts with the code shown (WCAG 2.5.3).
- Contrast: the current and just-moved row bars, the header search edge, the language select edge and the tabs
  switch's on edge reach 3:1; mask icons and status dots stay visible in forced colors; reduced motion also stops
  jQuery's effects.
- Side column tabs name their column, report `aria-expanded`, and on a page without that column name no target;
  the narrow and wide rules meet at 1023.98px.
- Content editing: every validation message is escaped exactly once in the summary and at the field, and a summary
  link scrolls its field below the fixed header. A warning that reports a failed form is `role="alert"`.
- Toasts lie on `--a4-z-toast` above dialogs and above the debug bar; an error toast stays until dismissed; Escape
  closes a focused toast.
- Breadcrumb: the last step is the current page also while editing; a name over 20 characters keeps its full name
  as title.
- Dashboard: the trend compares full days only ("against the 6 days before, without today"); the bar chart leaves
  its numbers to the table for screen readers.
- Audit timeline results, the left menu's "More" group and the mode switch ("Color mode") are translated; the
  left menu marks only the page itself as `aria-current="page"`.
- `ezpSiteAccessURL::front()` warns about an unknown `FrontSiteAccess` once per request also on Velocity's
  persistent workers.

### Upgrade notes
- Clear the template, template-override, template-block and content caches after the update (new templates and
  stylesheets), and hard-reload a browser that still shows the old stylesheet.

## 14 October 2026: admin4's base stylesheets on the tokens

### Fixed
- Links of the base stylesheets (`core.css` `a`, `ul.tabs`, the datatable's editable cells) take `--a4-accent-text`:
  the old `#0b4e7a` was 1.8:1 on a dark card (now 7.7:1; 5.5:1 in light mode).
- The diff marks of `content.css` are drawn from the tokens everywhere, as the history page already was: added text
  `--a4-ok`, removed text `--a4-bad`, the shown side's changes ink on `--a4-warn-bg` (light text on yellow was 1.1:1
  in dark mode, now 10.5:1), block changes on the ok and bad tints.
- The focused header search field kept a pale yellow background under light text in dark mode (1.1:1); it is the
  field colour. The search button's hover is the header's own, not a teal border.
- A focused `input.button` looks like a hovered one (`theme/rounded.css` painted it `rgba(0, 0, 0, .1)` over
  admin4.css); the ring marks the keyboard focus.
- `.a4-meter` fills with `--a4-accent-text`: 5.1:1 (light) and 7.0:1 (dark) on its track, where `--a4-accent` was
  2.9:1 in light mode (both copies of components.css).
- The context menu's focus ring is `var(--a4-ring)` in both modes, without a dark-mode rule of its own.
- The search scope pane fits a phone (`min(300px, 100vw - 32px)`); its title line takes `--a4-line`.
- The tabs on the window edges that show the side columns take `--a4-field-line` with the card colour on it (3.2:1
  and 4.0:1 on the page, the arrow 3.6:1 and 3.8:1 on the tab), instead of a fixed grey.
- In forced colors (Windows high contrast) every module state shown only by border, background or shadow (a current
  chip or tab, a selected or moved card or row, a pressed preset, a changed settings bar) gets a Highlight outline
  (`modules/*.css`, both copies).

### Added
- A print style for admin4: the content alone on the full width, no header, menus, side columns, control bars,
  toasts or footer, no shadows; the head script prints in the light colours and goes back to the chosen mode
  afterwards. No dark fallback without JavaScript: the admin stays light then, its documented default.
- Tests: `AdminStylingRulesTest::testBaseStylesheetsUseOnlyColourTokens` (no colour literal in core.css, content.css,
  pagelayout.css and the three theme stylesheets; the allowlist for kept literals is empty and only shrinks),
  `AdminBaseStylesheetsTest` (links, diff marks, forced-colors outlines, meter, button focus, menu ring, header
  parts, removed files, no vendor-prefixed leftovers, print) and the meter pairs in `AdminTokenContrastTest`; each
  new assertion was mutation-checked.

### Updated
- The rest of the colour literals of the base stylesheets are tokens as well (messages, rulers, the datatable and
  modal window headers, the default button's base colours, the footer links, the phone menu); the ones admin4.css
  overrode keep looking the same.

### Removed
- `design/admin4/stylesheets/responsive.css`: all but one rule was commented out. That rule (the padding of the
  status messages) is in `pagelayout.css`; `BackendCSSFileList[]=responsive.css` is gone from `settings/design.ini`,
  and admin3 names its own `responsive.css` in `page_head_style.tpl`, so admin3 looks the same.
- `setup-extensions.css` in admin4 and admin: no kernel template loaded it. The Admin UI design (exp_adminui) loaded
  it through its design fallback and ships its own copy from exp_adminui 1.0.0.17 on.
- Rules of admin4's base stylesheets for markup no admin4 template writes: the admin3 login page (`.loginpage`,
  `.login-input-wrapper`, `#icon-login` and the old `#logintext`/`#loginbutton` look, which admin4's sign-in page
  replaced), `#header-topmenu`, `#header-usermenu`, `#canvas-top`/`-bottom`, `p.versions`; and `-moz-`, `-ms-`, `-o-`
  transitions, shadows and gradients, `filter: alpha()`, `zoom: 1` and the invalid `linear-gradient(top, …)`.

### Upgrade notes
- A site whose own `design.ini` override still lists `BackendCSSFileList[]=responsive.css` keeps working: admin4
  then finds admin3's identical file through the design fallback.
- An extension that styled admin3's login page through admin4's `pagelayout.css` (`.login-input-wrapper` ...) needs
  its own rules; admin4's sign-in page never used them.

## 14 October 2026: names and request values in the admin designs are inserted as text

### Fixed
- Node, class, section and language names in the content tree menu, the popup menus and the relations list are
  inserted as text, and the sub-items name filter is escaped. The content tree keeps each node's menu data in the
  script and its menu button carries only `data-node-id`; one click listener opens the menu (mouse, Enter, Space,
  Shift+F10 and the context menu key as before). `ezpopmenu_showTopLevel()` keeps its parameters; its
  `menuHeader` is now set as plain text, and the `%...%` values of a menu item's `content` go into the item as
  text and attribute values (in all three copies of ezpopupmenu.js). `ezpopmenu_submitForm()` also fills the
  placeholders of the form's `action`.
- admin, admin3, admin4 (and standard where the same line exists): request values and stored texts that were
  printed raw are escaped for where they stand (the login form's redirect address, the template list filter, the
  shop list offset, URL and workflow fields, currency and package error texts, validation messages, relation
  attribute and translation names, left menu names); names passed to a popup menu from an `onclick` attribute are
  escaped for the script and then for the attribute. The link class preview links only to http(s), mailto and
  addresses without a scheme and shows anything else as text.
- `wash( 'javascript' )` also escapes `<`, `>` and `&` (as `\x3C`, `\x3E`, `\x26`), line breaks (`\n`, `\r`) and the
  line and paragraph separators, so its output is safe inside a script element and inside an HTML attribute as well as
  in a JavaScript string; the value a script reads is unchanged. A JS-escaped value in an HTML attribute (an `onclick`) is HTML-escaped as well
  (`|wash(javascript)|wash`). The sub-items name filter reaches the server URL-encoded and as typed.
- After the confirmation, `content/hide` returns to the page the user came from: the view has the `administration`
  UI context, so its confirmation page is not stored as the page to return to.
- `content/hide` hides or reveals a node only on a POST; a link (GET) shows the confirmation form, in English and
  German. The Advanced popup menu's "Hide / unhide" and the Hide and Reveal buttons of the Locations tab (admin,
  admin4; new shared class `.a4-link-button`) submit a form.
- The content tree icon's title is escaped once (a translation with an apostrophe showed `&#039;`). The redirect
  page of `DebugRedirection` escapes its address.
- Tests: `AdminTreeMenuEscapingTest` (also: no kernel design links to `content/hide`), `eZTemplateOperatorMatrixTest`
  (wash javascript in a script and with `&`).

### Upgrade notes
- A custom template or extension that links to `content/hide/<node>` now gets the confirmation page first; post a
  form to the same address to act at once. A `menuHeader` passed to `ezpopmenu_showTopLevel()` with markup in it
  shows the markup as text. Output of `wash( 'javascript' )` used outside JavaScript now shows `\x3C` for `<` and `\x26` for `&`. Without
  ezformtoken, `content/hide` is protected by the POST and the browser's SameSite default only; ezformtoken adds the
  form token.
  Clear the template and template-override caches after the update: compiled templates keep the old escaping
  until then.

## 14 October 2026: the search of the administration

### Fixed
- content/search and content/advancedsearch take the section, the class, the class attribute, the period, the
  subtrees and the time limit of the request as integers; anything that is no positive number is none. The ezjscore
  search (`ezjscServerFunctionsJs::search()`) passes only positive integer ids (`makePostIDArray()`) and an offset
  and limit of at least 0 and 1. A class may still be named by its identifier (`SearchContentClassID=article`,
  `SearchContentClassIdentifier`): both searches look it up to its id (`makePostClassIDArray()`,
  `AdvancedSearch::classID()`), and an identifier that names no class is no class filter. Before, the values reached the search engine as they were sent, and eZ Find wrote
  them into its filter queries. Updating eZ Find as well is recommended: its query builder now casts the ids itself.
- A section that does not exist no longer stops content/search and content/advancedsearch with a fatal error: the
  search is not limited to a section then.
- content/advancedsearch: a class attribute that does not exist, or that belongs to another class than the one
  searched, is no attribute filter (before: an error with eZ Find, or no result at all). The page opened without
  anything to search for runs no search; before it listed every object.
- A negative offset is 0; a search text is cut to `site.ini [SearchSettings] MaximumSearchTextWords` words (16) and
  `MaximumSearchTextLength` characters (1024), with a note on the page. Every word is a clause of the search
  engine's query, and a very long text made the search fail.
- The pages of a search keep where it searches: every subtree as `SubTreeArray[]` and the section (before: the
  subtrees joined by commas, which the view dropped, and no section); also in the browse mode. The advanced
  search's pages pass on the words as typed, not with the phrase in front of them a second time.
- The title of content/advancedsearch is escaped, as content/search's already was.
- The built-in search engine (`eZSearchEngine`, without eZ Find) writes the class attribute and the subtrees into
  its SQL as integers (`eZSearchEngine::subTreeNodeIDList()`); a subtree id that was not above 0 no longer leaves a
  stray comma in the node query.

### Updated
- admin4, content/search and content/advancedsearch: a search the engine could not run shows an alert
  (`.a4-notice.is-bad`, `role="alert"`) instead of "No results" and search tips; an empty text shows a prompt
  instead of "Search for <>"; the search form is a search landmark with a visible label and a search field. Each
  result shows the path of its location, so objects of the same name can be told apart.
- admin4 pager (`navigator/google.tpl`): a navigation landmark "Pages", the current page marked with
  `aria-current="page"`.
- admin4 header search: the sections of its popup are fetched once for both copies (`page_topmenu.tpl`), the name of
  the node searched in comes from the page's path when it is on it, and the browse mode's page size never exceeds
  `MaximumSearchLimit`.
- admin4 list filters of class groups, classes, workflow groups and sections say that they search only the page
  shown when the list has more than one page. Setup > RAD survey: the filter reacts to every input (typing,
  pasting, dictation), announces its count and is translated.
- New strings (design/admin/content/search, design/admin/navigator, design/admin/setup/rad and the four lists),
  ger-DE and eng-US.
- Tests: `ContentSearchRequestTest`, `ezjscServerFunctionsJsSearchIDsTest`, `AdminSearchTest`, `eZSearchEngineIdsTest`.

### Upgrade notes
- Clear the template and INI caches after the update. A site design of your own that overrides
  content/search.tpl or content/advancedsearch.tpl can read the new template variables `search_requested` and
  `search_text_shortened`, and `$search_extras.error` (eZ Find) for a failed search.
- These rules apply on every siteaccess, the front ends included: without `AllowEmptySearch`, a search without a
  text (a link such as `content/search?SubTreeArray[]=<node>` used to list a subtree) runs no search and shows the
  prompt; a search text is cut to 16 words and 1024 characters (`MaximumSearchTextWords`, `MaximumSearchTextLength`).
  A value that is neither a positive number nor a class identifier is ignored, so the search is then not limited by
  it.

## 14 October 2026: administration hardening

> **Action required:** exporting extensions is now off by default — list the extension in package.ini
> [ExtensionExportSettings] AllowedExtensions[] (or `*` for every extension) and grant the policy
> package/export_extension to the role. Without both, Setup > Extensions shows no download buttons and the package
> wizard does not let you choose the extension; the command line (`ezpm.php add`) needs the list entry.

### Updated
- Fixed: the AJAX uploader keeps the stored file on the server between its steps; the location form carries a
  one-time token bound to the user and the upload target instead of a file path (`ajaxuploader/location.tpl` of
  design/admin and design/admin4), and the file is taken only from the storage directory. The upload step checks
  the handler's `canUpload()` as the other steps do. `[ezjscServer_ezajaxuploader]` declares `Functions[]=ezajaxuploader`
  with `RequirePolicy=disabled`; `enabled` also asks for `ezjscore/call` with that function list.
- Fixed: the AJAX uploader's browse step (`ezajaxuploader::browse`) asks the upload handler's `canUpload()` like the
  other steps, before it reads the node; the call names the handler (`ezajaxuploader::browse::<handler>::<node>::<class>`)
  and posts its target (`ajaxuploader/browse.tpl`, `ajaxuploader/location.tpl` and `expajaxuploader.js` of design/admin
  and design/admin4).
- Fixed: audit/recent shows only the channels the Channel limitation of `audit/read` allows, refuses a channel
  outside it, and its "Verify now" button needs `audit/manage`, as on the console; the button keeps the channel
  filter.
- Fixed: Setup > Extensions downloads a package through a button of its form (POST) instead of a link; it says why
  when it cannot and removes the temporary package. No package takes an extension that holds secret values or key
  material: one rule, `eZExtensionPackageHandler::packRefusal()`, refuses it for Setup > Extensions, the package
  wizard (which shows the reason and removes the package it had begun), the command line and `addExtension()`
  itself. The check runs on the extension before anything is copied and once more on the copy `addExtension()`
  makes in the package, which decides: what is packed is exactly what was read, and a refused copy is removed. It
  walks the same entries the package copies (`packEntries()`), hidden files and links included, and fails closed:
  a pattern that fails (PCRE backtrack or JIT limit), a name over 256 bytes, a value over 64 KB, more than 200,000
  entries, a missing or unreadable directory and any other error refuse the extension. It refuses an extension
  with:
  - key material by name, any case: `*.key`, `*.p12`, `*.pfx`, `*.jks`, `*.ppk`, `*.p8`, `*.gpg` ..., also
    `*.key.*` (`server.key.old`), `credentials*`, `*service-account*`, `.env` and `.env.*` (not
    `.env.example`, `.sample` or `.template`), `id_rsa*`, `.htpasswd`, `.netrc`, `.pgpass`, and every file in a
    directory named `jwt`, `.ssh`, `.gnupg`, `*.pem` or `*.key`;
  - a symbolic link that leads outside the extension, or anything that is neither file, directory nor link;
  - a copy of a settings file (`*.bak`, `*.orig`, `*.dist`, `*.old`, `*~` ...) below `settings/` or named `*.ini*`;
  - in every file, text or binary, read whole and byte for byte as it is packed (and once more without NUL bytes,
    for UTF-16 text without a byte order mark): a private key
    (`-----BEGIN ... PRIVATE KEY-----`), `Authorization: Bearer ...`, service tokens (`sk-...`, `AKIA...`,
    `AIza...`, `ghp_...`, `github_pat_...`, `xoxb-...`, `glpat-...`, `sk_live_...`), `define( '..._KEY', '...' )`
    (KEY, SECRET, TOKEN, PASS ...) and an address with a password;
  - in settings files (below `settings/`, any depth, or named `*.ini*`), every `name = value`, read as text, not as
    eZINI reads it (commented lines, `Key[]=`, blanks, quotes, BOM, CRLF), and in configuration files (`*.yml`,
    `*.yaml`, `*.conf`, `*.cfg`, `*.properties`, `*.toml`, `.env*` ...) also `name: value` and YAML values on the
    next line: under a secret name (password, secret, token, key, pass, pw, auth, bearer ...) every value but an
    empty one, a whole placeholder (`...`, `xxx`, `***`, `<your key>`, `Enter your key here`), a switch (`enabled`,
    `disabled`, `true`, `false`, `none`, `null`, `0`), a template placeholder (`%name%`, `{name}`, `${NAME}`,
    `{{ name }}`, `%env(NAME)%`) or a measure or version the name announces (`MaxTokens=4096`,
    `IEDisableWithPassword[]=11`); an address, header name, type, mode or method (`TokenURL`, `ApiKeyHeader`) and a
    public key (`PublicKey`) are read only for a secret inside them; under another name a value with a real secret
    in it;
  - a file with a UTF-16 or UTF-32 byte order mark, a settings or configuration file with a NUL byte or over 4 MB,
    and a file or directory that cannot be read to its end.
  The text is read as it is: no byte order mark is removed, no line end changed, nothing decoded. A line holds the
  one pair it starts with, and the rest of the line is its value, read for secrets inside it; lines end at CR, LF,
  FF and VT; a YAML value may stand on any following, deeper line. Certificates (`*.pem`, `*.crt`, `*.der`,
  `*.asc`, such as a CA bundle) are refused only for a private key in them. In comments, constants and addresses
  of documents and tests, the usual example values pass (`# Password=changeme`, `https://user:pass@...`,
  `Bearer test-...`); an active setting is taken at its word (`Password=secret` is refused).
  The package wizard accepts only names of extensions.
- Added: an extension can list findings of the check that are known examples in a `package_check.ini` at its root
  (`AllowedFindings[]=<path>:<setting or (kind)>`); key material, private keys, copies of settings files and links
  can never be listed. Guide: `doc/eng-us/bc/6.0/extension-package-secret-check.md`.
- Updated: an extension package leaves out `node_modules` (the dependencies of a JavaScript build), which the check
  does not read. The extensions that ship example values under secret names carry a `package_check.ini` from
  ezauthorize 1.4.1, ezodf 6.1.8, hcaptcha 1.3 and recaptcha 1.4.6 on; on alpha every extension packs except those
  with stray local files (editor backups of settings files, a test key file), which the check names.
- Updated: audit/recent shows its "Verify now" button only to users with `audit/manage`.
- Updated: the AJAX uploader's token lives an hour; files whose token expired or was dropped leave the storage. A
  location that is refused keeps the token and the stored file, so another location can be chosen, and the
  temporary copy of each upload stands in a directory of its own and is removed in every case.
- Added: `package.ini [ExtensionExportSettings]`, closed by default: `AllowedExtensions[]` lists the extensions that
  may be exported (empty, the default: none; `*`: every extension the check lets through; applies to every way, the
  command line included), and `RequirePolicy=enabled` (the default) makes exporting an extension in the
  administration (Setup > Extensions, the package wizard) need the new policy function `package/export_extension`
  (the Administrator role with all functions has it; `disabled` drops that requirement). Where exporting is not
  allowed, Setup > Extensions shows the reason instead of the download buttons, the package wizard disables the
  extension, and both say in a notice how to switch exporting on.
- Fixed: Setup > System information > PHP leaves out the web server's request headers and environment and masks
  secret values (names ending in pw, pass, passwd, auth, bearer or authorization included,
  `expSystemReportMask::isSecretSettingName()`); Setup > Site data masks secret values; Setup > Sessions no longer
  gives session keys to the template (design/standard posts and selects session references like the admin designs). The system information names the
  front site of the admin (`FrontSiteAccess`) as the public site.
- Fixed: the dashboard reads a block's `Priority` as a number.
- Tests: `ezjscServerFunctionsAjaxUploaderTokenTest`, `expAuditRecentChannelsTest`, `ExtensionDownloadTest`,
  `SetupInfoMaskTest`.

### Upgrade notes
- **Action required:** exporting extensions is now off by default — list the extension in package.ini
  [ExtensionExportSettings] AllowedExtensions[] and grant package/export_extension.
- Clear the template and INI caches after the update. A site with its own override of `ajaxuploader/location.tpl`
  replaces the hidden `UploadFile` field with `<input type="hidden" name="UploadToken" value="{$upload_token|wash()}" />`.
  A site with its own override of `ajaxuploader/browse.tpl` puts the handler in its links:
  `ezajaxuploader::browse::{$handler_id|wash()}::<node>::<class>`.

## 14 October 2026: admin4 clean-up

### Fixed
- On a siteaccess that requires a sign-in (`RequireUserLogin=true`, normally the admin), `ezjscore/call` needs a
  signed-in user: ezjscore's `site.ini` no longer lists it in `[SiteAccessSettings] AnonymousAccessList`. Front ends
  (`RequireUserLogin=false`) do not use the list and are not affected. The debug bar shown by IP on an anonymous
  admin page (the sign-in page) then shows "Could not load" in its server panels.
- The header's logo names the release only to signed-in users; the anonymous pages of the admin (the 401 page,
  `user/register`, `user/forgotpassword`) draw the same frame and said "Exponential <version>" (admin4, admin4l).
- `menu.ini`: twelve `PolicyList_*` entries named a view instead of a policy function (`visual/toolbarlist`,
  `class/grouplist`, `role/list`, `workflow/processlist` ...); those modules have no policy functions, so any
  limited policy answered "no". They are `<module>/*`. The Design tab opens `content/view/full/58` (the design top
  node, `DesignRootNode`) instead of the URL alias `Design/`.
- setup/designextension: the file anchors used the operator `simplify_tag_name`, which does not exist; they are
  numbered now (admin, admin4).
- The version preview's full-screen link has a text alternative; the product previews translate "Category".
- The crystal-admin icon of the job class was missing in the ghost and original sizes; `MimeMap[video/video/...]` in
  the crystal and crystal-admin `icon.ini` is `video/vnd.rn-realvideo`.
- Scripts (admin4, and the identical copies in admin): `ezpopupmenu.js` declares its variables, places a submenu
  without a parent menu instead of throwing, and hides the menus on an outside press through an event listener
  instead of replacing `document.onmousedown`; the content tree draws a node of a class its cached parameters do not
  know (its parent stayed busy) and loads a node again when it is pressed after an error; both tab switch links are
  handled by `node_tabs.js` (the show link reloaded the page through a GET that changed the preference); the
  relations search uses `keydown`/`e.key` and reads `error_text` only from an answer; the upload dialog decodes its
  answer inside a `try` and adds its hidden fields once; the sub-items table has no repeated `id="thumbfield"`, and
  Create here is disabled where no class may be created (the item id was passed with quotes).

### Added
- admin4 carries the 21 templates it took from `design/admin`: `audit/*` (eleven), `parts/audit/menu.tpl`,
  `tabs/audit/node_header.tpl`, `contentfinder/*` (six), `infocollector/collectionlist.tpl` and `view.tpl`, adapted
  to admin4's checks (scoped header cells, status messages, an icon button and named checkboxes).
  `AdminSelfContainedTest` fails for a template the kernel or an admin4 template names that only `design/admin` or
  `design/admin3` has.
- German translations of 87 admin4 texts (mostly the content jobs pages) and the same sources in eng-US.
- The Quality workflow and the pre-commit hook check changed JavaScript files with `node --check`.
- Tests: `AdminMenuPolicyListTest`, `AdminScriptsTest`, `AdminSelfContainedTest`, `ezjscoreAnonymousAccessTest`, the
  crystal themes in `AdminIconsTest`, the brand name in `AdminPageHeaderTest`. `AdminStylingRulesTest` scans
  `override/templates` too and fails for a listed design that does not exist (it listed `admin2`).

### Removed
- From admin4: six templates nothing used (`test.tpl`, `page_menuhead*.tpl`, `pdf/edit_validation.tpl`,
  `content/confirmtranslationremove.tpl`), the user tab overrides `window_controls_user.tpl` and `windows_user.tpl`
  (no override rule has matched them since 4.6), 37 images no admin4 template, stylesheet or script reaches
  (`images/2/*`, most of `images/3/*`, old message and sprite images), `leftmenu_widthcontrol.js` (design/admin's
  column grip), and the unused functions `wrapTable()` and `adjustHeaderHeight()` of `ezadmin_menubar.js`, whose
  other functions are no longer globals.
- The inline `style` of `embed_image.tpl` (admin, admin4) and `template_look_edit.tpl` (admin).

### Upgrade notes
- An admin siteaccess that relied on anonymous `ezjscore/call` (none of the shipped pages does) must sign the user in
  first. A front end with `RequireUserLogin=false` (the default for front ends) is unaffected; a front end with
  `RequireUserLogin=true` whose pages call `ezjscore/call` anonymously (rating, captcha refresh, …) adds
  `AnonymousAccessList[]=ezjscore/call` to its own siteaccess `site.ini.append.php`.
- The Design tab opens `content/view/full/58`, the node `content.ini [NodeSettings] DesignRootNode` names in a standard
  installation; a site whose design node has another id overrides `[Topmenu_design] URL[default]` in `menu.ini`
  as well as `DesignRootNode`.
- A role that should open Setup > Classes, Roles, Workflows, Triggers, URLs, Search statistics, Settings, the
  template list, the toolbars or the menu settings through the left menu needs the module (`class/*` ...), as before;
  a custom `PolicyList_*` that names a view of these modules is better written as `<module>/*`.
- A site whose admin override rules point at `window_controls_user.tpl` or `windows_user.tpl` removes the rules, as
  since 4.6. Clear the template and template-override caches after the update.

## 15 October 2026: admin4 on phones, tablets and wide windows; one size for legacy controls

### Updated
- admin4's legacy buttons, selects and text fields (`input.button`, `input.defaultbutton`, `button.button`, fields
  without a class) take the size of its buttons and fields (`--a4-control-h`), with a select chevron in the tokens and
  the native select back in forced colors; every left menu shares one look and ends with one width switch
  (`parts/left_width_control.tpl`: three icon buttons in a POST form, the current width pressed) and one trash link
  (`parts/left_trash.tpl`); the node's tab box is a grid, so the end of the open tab is no longer cut off. New:
  `.a4-tabs.is-segmented` / `.is-icons`. An extension's navigation part without the `ez` prefix finds its left menu
  in admin4 and in admin4l. Test: `AdminLegacyControlsTest`.
- admin4 fits phones, tablets and wide windows: an opaque phone menu, a top menu on one line from 576 to 1279px, the
  right column as a drawer below 1280px, a content column capped at `--a4-content-max` (1680px), body text from 13 to
  14px on very wide windows (`--a4-text-body`), one gutter (`--a4-gutter`), the node's tabs scrolling on a phone,
  search results with their path under the name, 24px targets on touch screens. Tests: `AdminResponsiveTest`,
  `AdminSidebarDrawerTest`.
- The right drawer remembers whether it is open: the eZPreference `admin_right_drawer` (localStorage on an anonymous
  page), saved and restored on windows from 576 to 1279px (960px at 200% zoom included), never on a phone, and
  apart from the wide-window choice `admin_right_sidebar`. Opening a drawer with its tab moves the focus into it,
  Tab and Shift+Tab stay inside the open drawer and its tab, Escape closes it and returns the focus to the tab.

## 15 October 2026: the online editor's message for a dead link

### Fixed
- A link of the online editor whose target is gone no longer says only "Invalid link: ... Target node does not
  exist". The message names the link by its text and, when the target is in the trash, its name, class, place and the
  time it was trashed, and says how to repair the link; a link by location id (`eznode://42`) also says that restoring
  the target does not repair it, because the restored object gets a new location. Name, class and place are shown
  only to a user who may read the target; a trashed object has no location, so a user whose read policy is limited
  to subtrees or nodes gets the short message that the target is in the trash. The trash is looked up only for a
  link whose target is gone, so a link to a live target costs no query. German translations included.
- The messages of the validation log are plain text, and every design prints them escaped exactly once: the online
  editor's link messages keep the target's name, its place, the link text and address as typed, the XML text
  parsers store their messages as characters (`eZXMLInputParser::plainText()`: several translations spell tag names
  as `&lt;` and `&gt;`), and `design/admin`, `design/admin4` and `design/standard` print every validation, location,
  custom rule and field message with `wash` (content/edit, info collection, user registration, the PDF export
  form); the step in admin4 that turned `&lt;` and `&gt;` back before washing is gone. exp_adminui 1.0.0.18 and
  sevenx_themes_media 1.5.56 do the same in their own edit_validation.tpl. Test: `expOEValidationMessagesEscapedOnceTest`
  renders each design's template with a name holding markup, `&`, quotes and umlauts.
- Test: `expOEDeadLinkMessageTest`.
- A script link or an invalid e-mail address in the online editor marks the input invalid
  (`eZOEInputParser::isValid()` is false): the parser set `isInputValid`, a property it does not have, instead of
  `IsInputValid`, which PHP 8.2 reported as a dynamic property, and the e-mail check read the undefined property
  `errorLevel`. The messages and the saving of the text are unchanged; ezoe does not read `isValid()` when it saves.
  Test: `expOEInputValidFlagTest`.
- The online editor recognizes a script link in every spelling a browser follows (character references with or
  without `;`, `&colon;`, `&Tab;`, tabs and line breaks in the scheme, leading spaces and control characters, any
  case) and refuses `javascript:`, `vbscript:`, `livescript:` and `data:` with anything but a plain image type;
  "javascript" in a path, query or file name stays an ordinary link. Every address of a `mailto:` link is checked
  (several addresses, percent-encoding, a query), and internationalized domains are accepted (`kontakt@bücher.de`,
  an `xn--` top level domain), which the previous pattern refused. The script link message is translatable. Test:
  `expOELinkAddressCheckTest`.

### Upgrading
- A design of your own that overrides `content/edit_validation.tpl` (or the info collection, registration and PDF
  export messages) prints each message with `|wash`, once. The messages no longer carry `&lt;`/`&gt;`; a template
  that printed them raw now shows a tag name as markup, and one that turned the entities back keeps working but can
  drop that step.

## 15 October 2026: the online editor's parser and a line break with attributes

### Fixed
- The online editor's input parser no longer warns "foreach() argument must be of type array|object, false given"
  (`eZOEInputParser::setAttributes()`) for a `<br>` that carries attributes. The output tag `br` declares
  `'attributes' => false`, and the override that maps attribute names in any case iterated over it. The handler
  writes every empty table cell as `<p><br data-mce-bogus="1"/></p>`, so an editor that posts the text back
  unchanged (switching the editor, saving without an edit) raised one warning per empty cell; where errors are
  displayed it was printed before the doctype and put the page in quirks mode. The stored XML is unchanged. Test:
  `expOEInputBreakAttributesTest`.

## 15 October 2026: findings of an end-to-end test of the administration

### Fixed
- content/history: a version's Compare button orders the two versions by number (`History::comparedPair()`); an
  explicit From/To choice stays as chosen. Test: `expContentHistoryListTest`.
- The AJAX uploader refuses a missing or incomplete `AjaxUploadHandlerData` with "Arguments error." instead of an
  `ArgumentCountError`. Test: `ezjscServerFunctionsAjaxUploaderHandlerArgumentsTest`.
- Browse: the quick select of the current location is pre-checked only for radio buttons ("Add locations" added a
  location under the start node). Test: `AdminBrowseCurrentNodeTest`.
- content/hide: after the confirmation the view returns to the page the form was opened from (`RedirectURI`,
  checked as a local return address), not to the page last viewed in the session. Test: `expContentHideReturnTest`.
- The "Edit in" menus name languages like the other menus; German and American English for the content job pages;
  a keyboard-opened context menu stays at its trigger and below the fixed header; the edit bar's save status moves no
  button and the editor toolbars stay below the bar (TinyMCE 3 and 8). Tests: `AdminJobTranslationsTest`,
  `AdminContextMenuTest`, `AdminEditBarTest`.

### Removed
- Script and stylesheet links carry no version query: with packing off the packer writes each file's own address
  (the packed files keep their time in their name), and the TinyMCE 8 edit template no longer appends its cache key
  to its links. A browser that kept an old copy is reloaded by hand. Test: `ezjscPackerNoVersionQueryTest` (no
  template, script or class of the kernel and its extensions puts `?v=` on a link).
- ezjscore 1.8.0 (dual release with se7enxweb/ezjscore): the uploader's browse step asks the handler, its arguments
  are checked, unpacked links carry no version query.

## 15 October 2026: the admin4 Setup overview

The Setup tab opens an overview of every Setup function as a tile, grouped as the Setup left menu.
[Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/admin4-design.md#setup-overview).

### Added
- `setup/menu` in admin4 (`setup/setupmenu.tpl`): a page header and one section per group of the Setup left menu
  (Content model, Site and URLs, Workflows, Users and messages, System, and "More" for links an extension adds). Each
  tile is a card with a Bootstrap icon, the function's name as its link (the whole card is the target, one focus ring
  around it) and one line on what it does; the description is the link's description (`aria-describedby`). The grid
  counts the content column (container queries): one column on a phone, then two, three and four. Light and dark,
  forced colors.
- The tiles are the left menu: `parts/ini_menu.tpl` takes `menu_layout='tiles'` and draws the same links, groups and
  access checks (`PolicyList_<link>[]`, `CheckViewAccess` with `fetch( 'user', 'can_open' )`, `NoAccessLinks`) through
  the new include `parts/ini_menu_tiles.tpl`. A function the user may not open has no tile (with
  `NoAccessLinks=disabled`: a tile without link and without figure); a link an extension adds to `[Leftmenu_setup]`
  shows under "More".
- Icon, description and figure of a tile are settings of the menu's section, per link key, so an extension sets them
  for its own link in its `menu.ini.append.php`: `LinkIcons[<key>]` (an icon of the new `design.ini
  [AdminDesignSettings] IconList[]`, every `.a4-bi-*` mask rule of `components.css`; an unlisted name or none draws the
  plug icon), `LinkDescriptions[<key>]` (translated in the menu's context, like `LinkNames[]`) and
  `LinkFigureTemplates[<key>]` (a `design:` template included with the key). The core Setup links carry theirs in
  `settings/menu.ini`. An extension adds an icon of its own with its SVG, a mask rule in a stylesheet of
  `BackendCSSFileList[]` and `IconList[]=<name>`.
- Small figures where one cheap fetch gives them (`setup/setupmenu_figure.tpl`): classes, sections, content
  languages, the active extensions that exist (`expinfo( 'active' )`), signed-in users; one and many are two strings.
  Only on a tile the user may open.
- menu.ini `LeadLinks[]`: links shown before the groups of a grouped left menu, in none of them. `[Leftmenu_setup]`
  starts with `Links[overview]=setup/menu` ("Overview"), its lead link, marked with `aria-current="page"` on the
  overview; the overview itself leaves it out.
- The classic designs show a short page at `setup/menu` (`design/admin/templates/setup/setupmenu.tpl`): the heading,
  a pointer to the Setup menu and the `setup` toolbar a site configures in toolbar.ini, as before.
- 35 icon classes `.a4-bi-*` in both copies of `components.css` and 25 Bootstrap Icons 1.13.1 SVGs, unchanged
  (MIT), in `share/icons/bootstrap-icons/svg/`.
- New strings in design/admin/setup/setupmenu (title, subtitle, the figures) and in design/admin/parts/setup/menu
  ("Overview", one description per function), ger-DE and eng-US.
- Test: `AdminSetupOverviewTest` (the tab's address and the lead link, every Setup link with an icon and a
  description in menu.ini, every icon listed with a rule and a Bootstrap file, `IconList[]` equal to the rules, no
  icon used twice, each grouped link once, the access checks before the tiles, cheap figures in the link branch only,
  no image but the icon masks, the grid and focus rules, the translations; the tiles of a fixture menu drawn by the
  template engine, with an extension's icon, description and figure, an unlisted icon falling back to the plug and a
  tile without access; who opens `setup/menu`, evaluated by `eZUser::hasAccessToView()`). `AdminLeftMenuGroupsTest` allows
  the lead links outside the groups.

### Updated
- `[Topmenu_setup] URL[default]` is `setup/menu` instead of `setup/cache`.
- The view `setup/menu` opens with any one of the setup module's functions (`setup`, `administrate`, `managecache`,
  `managecronjobs`, `preload`, `system_info`) instead of `setup` only, so a role that reached the Setup tab through
  `setup/managecache` keeps it; the page shows only what the user may open. A user with none of them gets the access
  denied page, which names the last function asked (`system_info`).

### Added: tabs into Setup
- `[Leftmenu_setup] GroupTabs[<group>]=<tab>`: a group of the Setup menu and overview that stands for a top tab and
  is drawn only while that tab is not in the top menu (left out of `Tabs[]` or named in `HiddenTabs[]`); its pages
  then mark the Setup tab and draw the Setup menu (`parts/a4_navigation_part.tpl`, used by `page_topmenu.tpl`,
  `page_leftmenu.tpl` and admin4l's header and left menu blocks). `GroupSections[<group>]=<section>` adds the links of
  another left menu to the group with their names, access checks and tile settings; a Setup link to an address that
  menu has too shows there only. [Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/admin4-design.md#moving-a-tab-into-setup).
- Groups Design (`design_node`, the Design tab's page, plus `[Leftmenu_design]`) and Audit (`[Leftmenu_audit]`, its
  channel limits as in the audit menu) in `[Leftmenu_setup]`, for the Design and Audit tabs. Every tab stays by
  default: `HiddenTabs[]` is empty.
- `LinkIcons[]` and `LinkDescriptions[]` in `[Leftmenu_design]` and `[Leftmenu_audit]`; six Bootstrap Icons 1.13.1
  SVGs (archive, brush, exclamation-triangle, list-ul, speedometer, terminal) with their `.a4-bi-*` rules and
  `IconList[]` entries; the descriptions in design/admin/parts/design/menu and design/admin/parts/audit/menu, "Design",
  "Design content" and its description in design/admin/parts/setup/menu, ger-DE and eng-US.
- `[TopAdminMenu] SetupTabs[]` (empty by default): tabs that leave the top menu only for a user who may open
  `setup/menu`, who finds their group under Setup; a user who may not (a role with only the tab's own policy) keeps
  the tab and its menu. Read by the topmenu operator (`eZTopMenuOperator::canOpenSetupOverview()`), the Setup menu,
  `parts/a4_navigation_part.tpl` and the logo. Test: `eZTopMenuOperatorSetupTabsTest`.
- Without the Dashboard tab the logo leads to the dashboard for a user who may open it, instead of the content root.
- A page of a moved tab draws the Setup menu also when its view names a left menu of its own: the audit views
  (`$module_result.left_menu`, `design:audit/menu.tpl`) drew the audit menu beside the marked Setup tab. While the tab
  is in the top menu, the view's own menu stays (`page_leftmenu.tpl`, admin4l's left menu block). Test:
  `AdminSetupTabsLeftMenuTest`.
- `parts/ini_menu_tiles.tpl` reads icon, description (with its context) and figure from each item, which
  `parts/ini_menu.tpl` takes from the section the link comes from.
- Test: `AdminSetupOverviewTest` (the Design and Audit groups, their sections, pages, icons, descriptions and
  translations, no tab hidden by default, the navigation part drawn with the tabs shown and hidden, the logo);
  `AdminLeftMenuGroupsTest` allows a group whose links all come from `GroupSections[]`.

### Upgrade notes
- To take Design, Audit or the Dashboard out of the top menu, list them in `[TopAdminMenu] HiddenTabs[]` of the admin
  siteaccess and clear the INI and template caches.
- A site that wants the Setup tab to open the cache page again sets `[Topmenu_setup] URL[default]=setup/cache` in its
  `menu.ini.append.php`. A site that gave `Leftmenu_setup` its own `Links[]` adds `Links[overview]=setup/menu` and
  `LeadLinks[]=overview` for the overview's left menu entry.
- Clear the INI, template and template-override caches after the update.

### Fixed
- The Healthy Eating landing page showed the Fitness subtitle ("State of complete physical, mental, and social
  well-being"): the text block under its title was a copy of the Fitness one, as in the upstream reference. It now
  reads "Simple, nourishing food for an active life". The layout seed carries the new text in explayouts 1.4.25 and
  sevenx_themes_media 1.5.57 (one block parameter; no row renumbered), so a new installation shows it too.

## 15 October 2026: pausing cronjob parts

### Added
- **Setup > Cronjobs** pauses a cronjob part, or every part, until someone resumes it. `runcronjobs.php` leaves a
  paused part out whoever starts it (the crontab, `./console cron:<part>`, the console), before it asks the
  `cronjob/part/run` filter; a script run alone is left out while a part that lists it is paused. The cards show
  *Paused*, since when and by whom, a notice says so while every part is paused, the **Pauses** section lists who
  paused and resumed what (`var/<vardir>/log/cronjob_pause.log`), and a paused part cannot be run from the console.
  The state is a file per part (`<cache>/cronjob_control/cronpart_deactivated_<part>.txt`, `1` = paused), the
  layout extensions that paused parts already use. New: `expCronjobPause`,
  `cronjob.ini [PauseSettings]`, [Pausing cronjob parts](../../eng-us/features/6.0/cronjob-pause.md). Test:
  `expCronjobPauseTest`.

### Updated
- A part paused while it runs finishes the script that runs, and `runcronjobs.php` leaves out the scripts after it
  (it asks the pause again before each further script) instead of running the part to its end.
- Every run a pause leaves out is written to `<log directory>/cronjob_skipped.log` with the part, the script and the
  siteaccess, besides the notice in the run's output.
- The state file is written to a temporary file and renamed over the old one, and the state file and the history
  get `StorageFilePermissions`, so the command line or Velocity's workers (root) and the web server (the site user)
  can each replace what the other wrote.
- Pauses and resumes are audited: `system.cronjob.pause` and `system.cronjob.resume`.
- The feature page covers several servers (a shared `[PauseSettings] Directory`) and what a pause does not hold
  back (the queue workers of `expqueue` that run as a service).

### Upgrading
- A part an extension paused through such a file (holding `1`) is left out from now on; on Exponential those files
  had been read by nothing. Look at **Setup > Cronjobs** after the update and resume what should run, or set
  `cronjob.ini [PauseSettings] Pause=disabled`.

## 15 October 2026: a note with a cronjob pause, and the paused parts at a glance

### Added
- **Setup > Cronjobs** has a field *Note for a pause or resume (optional)*: the next **Pause** or **Resume** keeps it
  with its line in `cronjob_pause.log` (one line, at most 200 characters). The note shows on the paused part (compact
  row and card), in the notice of a pause of every part, in a *Note* column under **Pauses**, and in the audit record
  (`after.note` of `system.cronjob.pause` and `system.cronjob.resume`). `expCronjobPause::pause()` and `resume()` take
  it as a third argument; new `expCronjobPause::cleanNote()` and `NOTE_LENGTH`.
- The overview of **Setup > Cronjobs** has a **Paused** figure (every part while all are paused).
- The **Cronjobs** tile of the Setup overview says *1 part paused*, *n parts paused* or *All parts paused* while a
  pause holds: `menu.ini [Leftmenu_setup] LinkFigureTemplates[cronjobs]` and the fetch
  `fetch( 'setup', 'cronjob_pauses' )` (enabled, all, count).

### Updated
- The notice `runcronjobs.php` prints for a paused part says since when, by whom and why: `... is paused (Setup >
  Cronjobs). Paused since 2026-10-15 13:05 CEST by admin: Index rebuild until noon.` The same line goes to
  `cronjob_skipped.log`. New `expCronjobPause::describe()`.
- Translations ger-DE and eng-US; [Pausing cronjob parts](../../eng-us/features/6.0/cronjob-pause.md). Tests:
  `expCronjobPauseTest`, `AdminSetupOverviewTest`.
- The history keeps who paused as the user id only; the console and the notice show the user's current login,
  looked up by the id. The history is read again in each request, so a persistent Velocity worker shows a pause
  another process made since. The Setup tile counts only the parts `cronjob.ini` defines.

### Upgrading
- Nothing to do. History lines written before have no note; a site that set its own `LinkFigureTemplates[]` for the
  Setup menu adds `LinkFigureTemplates[cronjobs]=design:setup/setupmenu_figure.tpl` for the new figure.

## 15 October 2026: a documentation link for each cronjob script

### Added
- **Setup > Cronjobs** shows **Docs** beside a script that names its documentation, in the compact list, on the part
  cards and under the available scripts; it opens in a new window. A script names it with the `@link` tag of its
  header (or of its class file, #207): a URL, or a path that is put after `cronjob.ini [ScriptDocumentation]
  DocumentationBase` (empty: Exponential's documentation on GitHub). An installation gives or replaces it per script
  with `[ScriptDocumentation] Link[<script>]`, by the file name; `none` removes it. Only `http` and `https` links are
  shown. New `expCronjobRunner::scriptDocumentation()` and `documentationUrl()`; `scriptDescription()` reads the
  header as before.
- The kernel's scripts `rssimport.php`, `session_gc.php`, `notification.php`, `workflow.php`, `mailpreferences.php`,
  `mailbounces.php` and `querycache_cleanup.php` link to their guide. Translations ger-DE and eng-US, the console's
  feature page and the cronjobs guide (section 11). Test: `expCronjobScriptDocumentationTest`.

### Updated
- With the default `DocumentationBase` a path is linked only when the installation ships that file, so a typo or an
  unpublished page never becomes a dead link; the relative path in the header of an extension's script is not put
  after Exponential's base (it names a file of that extension). A script that names nothing shows no **Docs** until
  `[ScriptDocumentation] Link[]` gives one.

## 15 October 2026: a compact view of the cronjob parts

### Added
- **Setup > Cronjobs** lists the cronjob parts in a compact table by default: one row per part - the part with *n

  schedule in words with the next run below it, the last run from here with its result, and **Pause**/**Resume**

- **Compact** and **Detailed** are two toggle buttons (the shared segmented control) that write the preference by a

## 15 October 2026: the node view's tab box keeps its height

The box of the node view's tabs (View, Details, ...) keeps the height the user drags it to, for every node.
[Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/admin4-design.md#what-you-get) ("Height of the node view's tab box").

### Added
- admin4: a grip at the bottom right edge of the tab box (`parts/a4_tabs_height.tpl`, in `node/view/a4_full_body.tpl`
  and `node/top_node_full.tpl`), a focusable separator for the mouse, touch and the keyboard (Arrow Up and Down 16px,
  Shift+Arrow, Page Up and Page Down 64px, Home and End, a double-click for the default size: the keys and steps of the
  left column's and the debug panel's grips), in place of the browser's resize grip, which neither the keyboard nor a touch
  screen can use. The height is held between 10em and 60em and at most 85% of the window; a phone (below 576px)
  keeps the default. A "Default size" button (icon `arrow-counterclockwise`, added to `IconList[]`) shows while the
  height is not the default and forgets it.
- The eZPreference `admin_node_preview_height` (pixels; one value for all nodes), written on `<html>` by
  `parts/a4_preference_attributes.tpl` outside every cache-block and applied by `admin4.js` before the page is drawn.
  It is saved once at the end of a drag or 400ms after the last key, and only when it changed. Test:
  `AdminNodeTabsHeightTest` (its behaviour runs in node, `tests/tests/design/js/tabs_height.js`).

### Updated
- `window.a4Pref` (`page_head_style.tpl`): a preference that cannot be saved (no `fetch`, a network error, an error
  status, a redirect such as the login page of an expired session) is kept in the browser's `localStorage` instead,
  which is read while the preference is empty; `set( name, '' )` also removes the browser's copy. The request is sent
  with `keepalive`, so a change saved as the page is left still arrives.

## 15 October 2026: the Setup groups AI and Updates

Links that extensions add to the Setup menu no longer land unsorted under "More": the left menu and the Setup overview
have two more groups, AI and Updates, and "More" stays the last group for links that name no group yet.
[Feature](../../eng-us/features/6.0/admin4-design.md#setup-overview).

### Added
- `[Leftmenu_setup]` of `settings/menu.ini`: `Groups[]=ai` ("AI") and `Groups[]=updates` ("Updates") after System.
  AI holds the links of expai (`expai_summary`, `expai_models`, `expai_budgets`), Updates those of ezupdate
  (`ezupdate`) and git_manager (`git_manager`, `git_manager_backup`). The job queue (`expqueue`) and the database
  editor of sevenx_dse (`dse`) go to System. A key whose extension is not active shows nothing, and a group without a
  link the user may open is not drawn, so a site without these extensions sees the Setup menu as before.
- Icons and descriptions for these eight links (`LinkIcons[]`, `LinkDescriptions[]`): `stars`, `cpu`, `piggy-bank`,
  `list-task`, `database`, `cloud-arrow-down`, `git`, `archive`, eight Bootstrap Icons 1.13.1 SVGs, unchanged (MIT),
  in `share/icons/bootstrap-icons/svg/`, with their `.a4-bi-*` rules in both copies of `components.css` and in
  `design.ini [AdminDesignSettings] IconList[]`.
- New strings in design/admin/parts/setup/menu: the group names "AI" and "Updates" and the eight descriptions,
  ger-DE and eng-US (the group names also in `untranslated`).
- An extension sorts its own Setup link into a group with `GroupLinks_<group>[]=<key>` in its
  `menu.ini.append.php`, e.g. `GroupLinks_ai[]` or `GroupLinks_updates[]`; documented in the feature page
  ("Extending it").
- Tests: `AdminLeftMenuGroupsTest` checks the group order (the five core groups, then AI and Updates, "More" built
  last), that AI and Updates hold only extension links that core does not ship itself, and draws the Setup menu with
  the template engine and the real groups for links of active extensions: only groups with a link appear, in their
  order, "More" last with the link of an extension that names no group, the overview before the groups; the same as
  tiles with the new icons; and with no extension link the AI, Updates and More groups are gone.
  `AdminSetupOverviewTest` requires an icon and a description for every grouped link, an extension's too.

### Upgrade notes
- A site that reset `Groups[]` for its own grouping of `Leftmenu_setup` keeps it; to have the new groups, add
  `Groups[]=ai` and `Groups[]=updates` there. Clear the INI and template caches after the update.
