  SQLite database built from Exponential's schema and LoveStack's schema steps 1 to 4 (master `e61e9fdf0c`): the
  same content operations (publishing, new versions, adding, moving and removing locations, copying, a new class
  attribute on a class with objects, the trash) give the same content as on an Exponential database, and an empty
  string, never NULL, in `data_text`, so the column can stay as LoveStack made it. Guide:
  [Coming from LoveStack](../../eng-us/guides/upgrading.md#coming-from-lovestack).
- Moving a site from LoveStack: `./console exp:lovestack migrate` records a LoveStack database as Exponential's.
  The version row `ezpublish-version` = `lovestack` becomes this release's version (`6.0.15stable`), and LoveStack's
  schema version (`db-schema-version`, kept as it is) is recorded as `ezsite_data` `lovestack-schema-version`. It then
  prints the settings and extensions to change by hand, taken from `check`: LoveStack-only settings with their
  Exponential names, `[Session] SameSite` (read as `CookieSameSite` with a deprecation notice until it is renamed),
  an active `legacy_2018` extension and the `mugoweb/ezpublish-legacy` package (`expLoveStack::migrationPlan()`,
  `migrate()`). A dry run unless `--apply` is given; it never changes content or settings, a second run changes
  nothing, and a database that does not come from LoveStack is refused with a message saying so; `--json` for a
  machine-readable report. It follows LoveStack's documented schema steps (master `e61e9fdf0c`,
  `update/run.php` and `update/database/mysql/lovestack/1.sql` to `4.sql`), with thanks to Mugo Web. Guide:
  [Coming from LoveStack](../../eng-us/guides/upgrading.md#coming-from-lovestack).
- L01 (LoveStack commit aa307a7ff3): the node data calls `ezjscnode::load`, `::subtree` and `::updatepriority`
  can require the policy *ezjscore / call* with the function list `ezjscnode`: `ezjscore.ini [ezjscServer_ezjscnode]
  Functions[]=ezjscnode` and the new per server function setting `RequirePolicy` (`enabled`: checked, a request whose
  calls are all refused is answered with 403; `disabled`: not checked; not set: checked as before). New installations
  switch it on and give the Editor role the policy. Upgrade note: the shipped value is `RequirePolicy=disabled`, so
  nothing changes; to switch it on, give *ezjscore / call* with `ezjscnode` to the roles that use the administration
  without full access, then set `RequirePolicy=enabled` in `settings/override/ezjscore.ini.append.php`. Guide:
  [Roles and policies, section 8](../../eng-us/guides/roles-and-policies.md#8-decide-who-may-register-and-who-may-read-node-data).
- L02 (LoveStack commit 98854ab935): registering can require the `user/register` policy. `site.ini [RoleSettings]
  PolicyOmitListExclude[]` takes single entries out of `PolicyOmitList[]` (new: `expPolicyOmitList`), and the login
  pages of admin and admin4, the standard design and its login toolbar, the media and the starter designs show the
  register link or the Sign Up button only to a user who may open `user/register` (`fetch( 'user', 'can_open' )`).
  New installations write `PolicyOmitListExclude[]=user/register`. Upgrade note: `user/register` stays in
  `PolicyOmitList[]`, so registration and its link stay open; to close it, add
  `PolicyOmitListExclude[]=user/register` to `settings/override/site.ini.append.php` (and give the Anonymous role
  *User / Register* where registration should stay open). Guide:
  [Roles and policies, section 8](../../eng-us/guides/roles-and-policies.md#8-decide-who-may-register-and-who-may-read-node-data).
- L03 (LoveStack commit 13ace6c9c8): logging out ends the session and its cookie. `eZUser::logoutCurrent()`
  removes the session (`eZSession::remove()`) instead of giving it a new id with its contents kept. The `destroy()`
  of every session handler sends the session cookie expired with the path, domain, `Secure`, `HttpOnly` and
  `SameSite` that `eZSession::start()` set it with (`eZSession::expiredCookie()`, `eZSession::expireCookie()`);
  `eZSession::remove()` also keeps a handler that only deletes its stored copy (`ezpSessionHandlerDB`) from having
  the session written back, and gives a session started later in the same request a new id, under PHP-FPM and
  Velocity alike. The logout view reads `LastAccessesURI` before logging out and no longer opens a new session only
  to store `force_logout`. On for every installation. Upgrade: nothing to do; code of your own that reads session
  variables after `eZUser::logoutCurrent()` finds the session empty. Guide:
  [Security and audit](../../eng-us/guides/security-and-audit.md).
- L04 (LoveStack commit 826e1d4647): one account per login. `./console exp:user-login-check` lists the logins more
  than one account has, also those that differ only in case, and changes nothing; `--fix-index` adds the unique
  index on `ezuser.login` once the list is empty and refuses otherwise (`expUserLoginIntegrity`). The kernel schema
  of every engine (`kernel/sql/*`, `share/db_schema.dba`) has the unique index, so new installations get it. A user
  account of a first version whose form did not validate is not stored, and a draft, a package import, the content
  API's account string and a first LDAP sign-in no longer give an account a login another account has; two requests
  creating the same account at once end with one account. Upgrade note: the database update does not add the index
  (it would fail on duplicates); run `./console exp:user-login-check`, resolve what it lists, then
  `./console exp:user-login-check --fix-index`. Guide:
  [one account per login](../../eng-us/guides/security-and-audit.md#7-one-account-per-login-2-minutes).
- L05 (LoveStack commit c8e45fced7): uploaded images are checked by their content. PHP's
  fileinfo reads the type, which must be on `image.ini [ImageUploadSettings] AllowedMIMETypes[]` (JPEG, PNG, GIF,
  WebP, AVIF, TIFF, BMP; SVG was added on 15 October 2026, stored sanitized); a refused file is not stored. It applies to the image datatype in
  the edit form, `content/upload`, the rich text editor's upload dialog, the AJAX uploader, WebDAV and imports, and
  `eZContentUpload` refuses such a file before it creates an object. New: `expImageUploadValidator::validate( $path )`.
  On for every site, upgrades included: a file that is not a listed image by content (a renamed file, HEIC, SVG) is
  refused (SVG is accepted from 15 October 2026, see [SVG images](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/svg-images.md)). To accept another type, add it to `AllowedMIMETypes[]` in an override; `ValidateMIMEType=disabled` keeps
  only the getimagesize() check. Without fileinfo that check decides and one warning per process says so.
  See [behaviour changes of October 2026](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-10.md#access-and-security).
- L06 (LoveStack commit 04b3b6985a): the package wizard checks the thumbnail of a new package and the images of
  a style package by their content, with the allow-list of L05 (`eZPackageCreationHandler::httpFileIsImage()`),
  so a file that only looks like an image is refused. On for every installation, upgrades included; nothing to do.
  Guide: [behaviour changes of October 2026](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/behaviour-changes-2026-10.md#access-and-security).
- `[Session] SameSite` (LoveStack commit e56cb305f0): settings written with LoveStack's name for the SameSite
  attribute of the session cookie keep working. `site.ini [Session] SameSite=` is read when `CookieSameSite` is not
  set (`CookieSameSite` is used when both are), and noted once per request at the deprecated level of the debug
  output (`var/log/deprecated.log`). Upgrade: nothing changes for visitors; rename `SameSite` to `CookieSameSite`
  in `settings/override` and the siteaccesses to silence the note. Guide:
  [Security and audit](../../eng-us/guides/security-and-audit.md).
- L07 (LoveStack commits fbbd333a85, e20570a7ad, b7832d110e): PHP deprecations have a debug level of their own,
  `eZDebug::LEVEL_DEPRECATED` with `SHOW_DEPRECATED` (part of `SHOW_ALL`) and `eZDebug::writeDeprecated()`.
  With `site.ini [DebugSettings] DeprecatedLog=enabled` (the default) `E_DEPRECATED` and `E_USER_DEPRECATED` are
  written to `var/log/deprecated.log`, each distinct message once per request, and only counted: the debug bar shows
  their number (`depr`), they stay in the page's warning count, and none is kept in memory or listed with the
  messages. `AlwaysLog[]=deprecated` writes them while debug output is off as well. Upgrade note: on after the
  upgrade; `DeprecatedLog=disabled` keeps them warnings as before. Guide:
  [PHP deprecations and log file sizes](../../eng-us/guides/operating-a-site.md#php-deprecations-and-log-file-sizes).
- L08 (LoveStack commit e39dde30ff): the size and rotation of the log files are settings, `site.ini [FileSettings]
  LogFileMaxSize=2097152` (2 MB) and `LogFileRotateCount=3`, read by eZLog and eZDebug; `setMaxLogSize()`,
  `setLogrotateFiles()` and the constants in `config.php` still win over them. Upgrade note: log files are rotated
  at 2 MB instead of 200 KB after the upgrade; `LogFileMaxSize=204800` keeps the old size. Guide:
  [PHP deprecations and log file sizes](../../eng-us/guides/operating-a-site.md#php-deprecations-and-log-file-sizes).
- L09 (LoveStack commit 0bb0bac618): a template operator parameter that the template leaves out and the operator
  gives no default value for is `null`, with the warning `Operator '<op>' has no default value for parameter
  '<name>'`, once per operator and parameter in a request; a compiled template is warned once when it is compiled
  and its code does not check again. New: `eZTemplate::operatorParametersWithoutDefault()`. Upgrade note: on for
  every site, only the warning is new. Guide:
  [an operator parameter without a default value](../../eng-us/guides/templates-and-design.md#an-operator-parameter-without-a-default-value).
- L10 (LoveStack commit fb20bee0d2): `site.ini [DebugSettings] UsedTemplateEditUrl=` is the `sprintf()` format of
  the "Edit" links in the debug output's list of used templates (`%1$s` editor address with the file, `%2$s`
  siteaccess, `%3$s` template file). Upgrade note: empty, the shipped value, keeps the link the list always had.
  Guide: [edit links in the list of used templates](../../eng-us/guides/templates-and-design.md#edit-links-in-the-list-of-used-templates).
- L11 (LoveStack commit 3d18f47551): `eZDBException::getCode()` is the driver's error number of the failed
  statement on every driver, as `errorNumber()`: MySQL's number, PostgreSQL's SQLSTATE (a string), SQLite's result
  code, Oracle's ORA number; MongoDB keeps the server's error code in `errorNumber()`. MySQL reads the error of the
  connection the statement ran on (the write server, when there is one), and an error is kept through the
  transaction's rollback. Upgrade note: PostgreSQL's `errorNumber()` is the SQLSTATE instead of `1`; with the
  exception handling, MySQL on PHP 8.1 and later throws `eZDBException` instead of `mysqli_sql_exception`. Guide:
  [know your database driver](../../eng-us/guides/operating-a-site.md#7-know-your-database-driver).
  The "database unavailable" page (HTTP 503) also recognises an `eZDBException` whose error number says the
  connection went away or could not be made (MySQL 2002/2003/2006/2013 ..., PostgreSQL SQLSTATE class 08, Oracle
  ORA-03113/03114/12541 ..., SQLite 14/26): `eZExecution::isDatabaseUnavailableCode()`.
- L12 (LoveStack commit f9691221a3): when a change would clear the view caches of `site.ini [ContentSettings]
  CacheThreshold` nodes or more and all view caches are expired instead, `eZContentCacheManager` writes a warning
  with the number of nodes and the threshold (it was a debug message). Upgrade note: on for every site. Guide:
  [caches, usual questions](../../eng-us/guides/caches.md#6-usual-questions).
- L13 (LoveStack commit 9c931192e0): location changes in the audit trail, channel `content`, written from
  `eZContentOperationCollection`: `content.node.add` once per added location (the object; the new node and its parent
  as the target), `content.node.remove` with whether the removed location was the main one, and `content.node.main`
  when an editor chooses another main location or the next location takes the place of a removed main one (`cause`
  `location-remove`). The console shows them as Location add, Location remove and Main location change. The names
  `location-assign`, `location-remove` and `main-node-update` are mapped in `audit.ini [AuditCompatSettings] Map[]`
  and `AuditFileNames[]`, with their attributes, so `eZAudit::writeAudit()` calls and imported files of those names
  land on the same events. Upgrade note: `content.node.remove` is on as before; `content.node.add` and
  `content.node.main` stay off until `Enabled[]=content.node.add` / `Enabled[]=content.node.main` is set. Guide:
  [audit reference](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/audit.md#location-changes-who-added-removed-or-chose-a-location).
- L14 (LoveStack commits 55029bd473, a679a13176, 94b7b6c239): `fetch( 'content', 'site_data', hash( 'name', ... ) )`
  reads one row of `ezsite_data` (the eZSiteData object, or false), read only and only for the names of
  `site.ini [SiteDataSettings] TemplateReadable[]` (empty by default, so nothing is readable until a name is listed).
  Setup > Site data (`setup/sitedata`, policy `setup/system_info`) lists the rows with a filter and paging, in admin,
  admin3 and admin4. New `eZSiteData::createNew()`, `templateReadableNames()`, `isTemplateReadable()`, `fetchList()`
  and `fetchListCount()`. Upgrade note: nothing is readable from templates until a name is listed. Guides:
  [site data in templates](../../eng-us/guides/templates-and-design.md#9-site-data-in-templates),
  [site data](../../eng-us/guides/system-information.md#9-site-data).
- L15 (LoveStack commit 4d56593614): a Copy button on the full view of the administration interface (admin, admin3,
  admin4), next to Move, active with create access at the item's parent as in the context menu; it opens
  `content/copy`. Guide: [copy from the full view](../../eng-us/guides/content-model-and-editing.md#copy-from-the-full-view).
  On for every installation; nothing to do on an upgrade.
- L16 (LoveStack commit a28863d9c0): an image attribute can require an alternative text, the class attribute
  option **Alternative text is required** (`eZImageType::ALT_TEXT_REQUIRED_FIELD`, `data_int2`, off by default).
  The edit form, the online editor's upload dialog (also used by the Tiptap engine) and the AJAX uploader of relation
  fields refuse an image without one; the AJAX uploader has an alternative text field for classes with an image
  attribute and stores it on the new object. Packages carry the option. Upgrade note: off for every class; switch it
  on per attribute on the class edit page. Guide:
  [required alternative text](../../eng-us/guides/content-model-and-editing.md#alternative-text-is-required-image-fields).
- L17 (LoveStack commit 6a5c7dd23e): a text line attribute can trim the stored value, the class attribute option
  **Trim the stored value** (`eZStringType::TRIM_FIELD`, `data_int2`, off by default): spaces at the start and the
  end are removed in the edit form, information collection, imports and uploads. Upgrade note: off for every class.
  Guide: [trim the stored value](../../eng-us/guides/content-model-and-editing.md#trim-the-stored-value-text-line-fields).
- L18 (LoveStack commit 95d86f5990): a PHP class can make object names and URL alias names: `{ClassName}` at the
  start of a class's name pattern, for classes listed in `content.ini [NamePatternSettings] ResolverClasses[]`
  (empty by default) that implement `expNamePatternResolverInterface`; any other name is logged and the pattern is
  resolved from its tokens. New `eZNamePatternResolver::resolve()`, `customResolver()` and `limitName()`; the
  constructor and `resolveNamePattern()` are unchanged. Upgrade note: nothing changes until a class is listed.
  Guide: [names made by a PHP class](../../eng-us/guides/content-model-and-editing.md#names-made-by-a-php-class-developers).
- L19 (LoveStack commit 9c0ff40a78): `content.ini [FetchSettings] DefaultClassFilterType=` is the class filter type
  the fetch functions `list`, `tree`, `list_count`, `tree_count` and `calendar` use when a fetch gives
  `class_filter_array` without `class_filter_type` (`eZContentFunctionCollection::classFilterType()`). Upgrade note:
  empty, the shipped value, keeps today's results (the array is ignored); `include` filters by the array. Guide:
  [class filter default](../../eng-us/guides/content-model-and-editing.md#class-filter-default-for-fetches-template-developers).
- L20 (LoveStack commit f1b7f056b4, EZP-26501): in the edit form's Locations window an existing location starts at
  "Unchanged", not "Visible", so publishing no longer reveals a hidden location; a new location starts as it was
  given. Fix, for every site. Guide:
  [the Locations window](../../eng-us/guides/content-model-and-editing.md#the-locations-window-keeps-a-hidden-location-hidden).
- L21 (LoveStack commits eee7f69fc3, 67eec8f972, 492a946963): the view templates of the selection and matrix
  datatypes write no stray `<br />`; several selected options are separated by one `<br />` each. Fix, for every
  site that does not override these templates.
  Guide: [selection and matrix fields](../../eng-us/guides/content-model-and-editing.md#selection-and-matrix-fields-without-stray-line-breaks).
- L22 (LoveStack commit e21ef46691): `content.ini [CustomTagSettings] IsHidden[]` names custom tags the editors do
  not offer for new tags (TinyMCE 3's `ezoe/tags`, the TinyMCE 8 custom tag dialog, Tiptap). A hidden tag already in
  a text is still rendered, shown, editable and saved back unchanged; its own dialog keeps its name. New:
  `eZOEXMLInput::hiddenCustomTags()`, `eZOEXMLInput::offeredCustomTags()`, the key `hidden` of
  `getCustomTagDefinitions()`. None is hidden by default, so nothing changes on an upgrade. Guide:
  [Hidden custom tags](../../eng-us/guides/content-model-and-editing.md#hidden-custom-tags).
- L23 (LoveStack commit 63334f1681, the custom_tags_controls plugin, Copyright 2014, Mugo Web): custom tag controls
  in the Online Editor with `ezoe.ini [EditorSettings] CustomTagControls=enabled`. A focused custom tag gets a bar to
  move it up or down, edit it and remove it (its content stays), also with Alt+Shift+Up, Alt+Shift+Down, Alt+Shift+E
  and Alt+Shift+Delete; Backspace in an empty block custom tag removes it; every block custom tag shows its name as a
  label (`ezoe_customtags.css`); a tag's dialog shows `images/editor/custom_tags_thumbs/<name>.png` from a design as
  an example. TinyMCE 3 gets the new plugin `exp_customtag_controls` (TinyMCE 3's plugin API and ezjscore's jQuery
  4, no YUI), TinyMCE 8 the buttons in its custom tag context toolbar, Tiptap the same in exp_oe_tiptap 0.1.2.
  New installations write `CustomTagControls=enabled`. Upgrade note: the shipped value is `disabled`, so the editor
  stays as it was; set `CustomTagControls=enabled` in `settings/override/ezoe.ini.append.php` and clear the INI cache
  and the ezjscore packer cache. Guide: [Custom tag controls](../../eng-us/guides/content-model-and-editing.md#custom-tag-controls).
- L24 (LoveStack commit 3a8c14bc0c): the sub/sup custom tag dialog of TinyMCE 3 closes on OK. Exponential's
  `tag_custom.tpl` already had the fix (from the same author's later upstream commit); tested in the browser on
  TinyMCE 3 and Tiptap (TinyMCE 8 has its own sub and sup buttons).
  Nothing changes on an upgrade.
- L25 (LoveStack commit f2284944b2): `eZURI::build( string $url, bool $full = false, bool $htmlEscape = true )`
  builds a link within the site in one call, the same as the `ezurl` operator gives (index file and siteaccess in
  front; with `$full` scheme and host). `$htmlEscape = false` for a URL in a header or a JSON answer. Addition only.
  Guide: [URLs, for developers](../../eng-us/guides/urls-and-aliases.md#for-developers).
- L26 (LoveStack commit df07cb1ef5): `eZMailTransport::send( eZMail $mail, ?string $transportType = null )` sends one
  message with another transport of `site.ini [MailSettings] TransportAlias[]` (for example `file`) without changing
  the setting; the mail gate applies as before. Addition only. Guide:
  [testing without sending mail](../../eng-us/guides/mail-preferences-developer.md#8-testing-without-sending-mail).
- L27 (LoveStack commit 627004e5c4): the template operators `rawurlencode` and `rawurldecode` (`template.ini [PHP]
  PHPOperatorList[]`), RFC 3986 encoding with `%20` for a space. Available in every installation, upgrades
  included; `urlencode` and `urldecode` are unchanged. Guide:
  [encoding a value for a URL](../../eng-us/guides/templates-and-design.md#10-encoding-a-value-for-a-url-rawurlencode-and-rawurldecode).
- L28 (LoveStack commit f7c382bc2b): `[Include <pattern>]` inside a settings file reads other files at that place:
  the pattern is relative to the including file, wildcards are allowed, the files are read in name order, includes
  nest, a loop is read once with a warning and a pattern without a match is a notice. The included files and the
  directories their patterns look in are part of the INI cache (`'includes'`, `eZINI::includedFiles()`), so
  `ezcache.php --clear-tag=ini`, the INI step of `exp:velocity deploy` and `expVelocity::refreshIni()` pick up their
  changes; with the file time check on, a changed, added or removed included file rebuilds the cache by itself. The
  settings page, the classic settings editor and `exp:ini` keep the line as written and do not expand it. Only
  active where a file has such a line; parse time of files without one measured unchanged (an A/B in one process
  over 174 files, within the host's noise). Guide:
  [splitting a large settings file](../../eng-us/guides/settings-page.md#splitting-a-large-settings-file-with-include-).
- L29 (LoveStack commit 97690e9c93): `error.ini [HTTPError-<code>] HeaderList[<name>]=<value>` adds response headers
  to an error page after the status lines of `HTTPName`; they are stored with the page in the content view cache and
  sent again from it (`expErrorHeaderList`). An invalid header name or a value with a line break is left out with a
  warning; `HeaderList[Status]` stands for a missing `HTTPName`. Empty by default, new installations and upgrades
  alike, so nothing changes until an entry is added. Guide:
  [response headers of error pages](../../eng-us/guides/templates-and-design.md#12-response-headers-of-error-pages).
- L30 (LoveStack commits 3d2d337b0d, 3af1ec3aee): the ezjscore search (`ezjsc::search`, used by the editor's embed
  and link dialogs and the relation search) sorts eZ Find results by `ezfind.ini [SearchSettings] SortBy[]`;
  ezjscore ships `SortBy[published]=desc`, the order it always used, so nothing changes on an upgrade. Without
  eZ Find or the setting the order stays newest first. Guide:
  [the order of search results](../../eng-us/guides/content-model-and-editing.md#the-order-of-search-results-in-the-editors-dialogs).
- L31 (LoveStack commit 856a95e240): every node view sets the override key `layout` (`''` outside `layout/set`,
  the layout of `layout/set` kept), through the new `eZTemplateDesignResource::setKeyValue( string $key, $value,
  bool $override = true )`. Guide:
  [the layout key](../../eng-us/guides/templates-and-design.md#11-telling-a-layout-view-apart-the-layout-key).
- L32 (LoveStack commit f435c2f91e): `site.ini [URLTranslator] DecodeOnce` decodes the address of a request once
  instead of twice, so `/(tag)/bar%2Bbar` gives the view parameter `bar+bar` instead of `bar bar`.
  `new eZURI( $uri, $decode )` and `setURIString( $uri, $fullInitialize, $decode )` take whether the string is
  encoded (`null`: follow the setting); `eZURI::decodeOnce()`. PHP-FPM and Velocity hand the kernel the same raw
  address, and the response cache keys are made from it, so they do not change. Upgrade note: off as shipped, on new
  installations and after an upgrade (`DecodeOnce=disabled`); to switch it on, set `DecodeOnce=enabled` in
  `settings/override/site.ini.append.php` after checking links that put an encoded `+` or `%` in a view parameter.
  Guide: [Decoding and cleaning addresses](../../eng-us/guides/urls-and-aliases.md#8-decoding-and-cleaning-addresses-two-optional-settings).
- L33 (LoveStack commit 7517c0e925): `site.ini [URLTranslator] TrailingCleanup` makes the double quote a reserved
  character of the IRI alias cleanup (`urlalias_iri`), so it becomes a separator and is stripped at the ends like
  `!`, dots and separators (`eZCharTransform::trailingCleanup()`); the other transformations already strip them.
  Upgrade note: off as shipped (`TrailingCleanup=disabled`); with `TrailingCleanup=enabled` in
  `settings/override/site.ini.append.php` existing aliases stay until their object is published again, and the old
  alias then redirects. Guide:
  [Decoding and cleaning addresses](../../eng-us/guides/urls-and-aliases.md#8-decoding-and-cleaning-addresses-two-optional-settings).
- L34 (LoveStack commit 3774a54ead): a number in a text beyond the range of the search index's integer column no
  longer stops the built-in search engine from indexing the object; it is stored as the limit of the column. Each
  driver states the range of its integer columns, `MAX_INT` and `MIN_INT` (signed 32-bit for MySQL, PostgreSQL and
  any other driver, signed 64-bit for SQLite and MongoDB), and `eZDBInterface::clampInteger( $value )` keeps a value
  within it, also one written as a float (`1e30`), which a plain `(int)` turned into a wrong number. On for every
  installation; nothing to do on an upgrade. Guide:
  [Numbers in the built-in search index](../../eng-us/guides/operating-a-site.md#numbers-in-the-built-in-search-index).
- L35 (LoveStack commit 5c089f6250): the search index leaves out the relation fields of related objects, and the
  relation datatypes need no recursion guard. Exponential has done the same since 2018 (EZP-27101,
  `eZContentObjectAttribute::metaDataArray( $attributes, true )`, `eZDataType::isRelationType()`), so there is
  nothing to switch and no setting; `tests/tests/kernel/datatypes/eZObjectRelationMetaDataTest.php` now documents
  the behaviour, a cycle of relations included. Upgrade note: none; a site coming from LoveStack gets the same
  search results.
- L36 (LoveStack commits 3298b53584, 89a5a73877): the hide cronjob (`hide.php`) tells the search engine about each
  node it hides (`eZSearch::updateNodeVisibility()`, in Exponential since 2018), and the `content/cache` event gives
  listeners `( $nodeIDs, $objectIDs )` from every sender in the kernel, now also from the node's URL aliases page;
  a listener that takes only the node ids keeps working. On for every installation; nothing to do on an upgrade.
  Guide: [Cronjobs](../../eng-us/guides/cronjobs.md#1-parts-scripts-and-the-global-part).
- PHP 8 review (phase 7): LoveStack's PHP 7.2 to 8.3 fixes (2017 to 2026) reproduced one by one on PHP 8.5 and 8.1,
  each as a regression test; most cases were already handled in Exponential and stay as tests that document them.
  Four still failed and are fixed, on for every installation (plain bug fixes, nothing to do on an upgrade):
  - LoveStack commit e828c54709: the `get_class` and `is_class` template operators accept a value that is not an
    object without template compilation too (`''` and `false`, as the compiled code gives); before, a string,
    number or null ended the request with a TypeError.
  - LoveStack commit 4065ed16b3: `wash(email)` of an empty value gives no "Passing null" deprecation.
  - LoveStack commit 75cbb1daa2: `eZHTTPTool` copes with `$_POST` set to null.
  - LoveStack commit 1bd250d4c6: `update/common/scripts/5.4/cleanupfieldvaluerelations.php` skips relation list
    attributes whose `data_text` is NULL or empty instead of stopping with a ValueError.
- PHP 8 review, four more sites from the same LoveStack commits, each reproduced first on PHP 8.5 and 8.1 and
  fixed; on for every installation (plain bug fixes, nothing to do on an upgrade):
  - LoveStack commit 4065ed16b3: the classic HTML debug report (used when the Exp Debug bar is not there) shows a
    message whose label or text is null without a "Passing null" deprecation, as the debug bar already did.
  - LoveStack commit 4065ed16b3: the Exp Debug bar shows a message whose text is null or a number without a
    "Passing null to strncmp()" deprecation when xdebug is loaded (`expDebugBarReport::messageRows()`).
  - LoveStack commit 4065ed16b3: `ezurl`, `ezroot` and `eZURI::transformURI()` of a number (a node id) give no
    "Trying to access array offset" warning, and `new eZURI( null )` (the command line before a request URI is
    known) is an empty address. `eZURI::build()` gives the same links as before.
  - LoveStack commit 1bd250d4c6: `eZPostgreSQLDB::escapeString( null )` gives `''`, as the MySQLi, SQLite3 and
    MongoDB drivers do, instead of passing null to `str_replace()` and `pg_escape_string()`.
- LoveStack commit 5a17c08bfc: a customer's orders are found from the order lists, the archive, the customer list
  and the shop dashboard when the e-mail address has a "+" (or "?", "#"). The links `rawurlencode` the address and
  shop/customerorderview no longer decodes it a third time; older links still work. On for every installation.
  Upgrade note: a site design of your own that links to `shop/customerorderview` can add `|rawurlencode` to the
  address in the same way.
- LoveStack commit cb8a3a1c58 (the spell checker part): `ezoe/spellcheck_rpc` reads the request body from
  `php://input` and never from the removed `$HTTP_RAW_POST_DATA`, GoogleSpell decodes character references without
  the `/e` modifier, and the spell checker and JSON classes have `__construct()`. Exponential already had all three;
  checked on PHP 8.5, a test keeps them (`ezoeSpellcheckRpcRawInputTest`).
- The template operators `urlencode`, `urldecode`, `rawurlencode` and `rawurldecode` give an empty text for a value
  that is not set, with and without template compilation, instead of handing null to PHP (a "Passing null"
  deprecation on PHP 8.1 and later). Found by the L27 tests; a plain fix for every installation, nothing to do on an
  upgrade (compiled templates take it when the template cache is cleared).
- Tests (phase 8): every feature above is covered in the PHPUnit suite, each test file running on its own and,
  apart from the tests marked live, without a database. New: the settings an upgraded site keeps
  (`expShippedFeatureDefaultsTest`), the Copy button of the full view (L15, `eZAdminFullViewCopyButtonTest`), the
  visibility the Locations window preselects (L20, `eZEditLocationsVisibilityTest`), the selection and matrix view
  templates (L21, `eZSelectionMatrixViewTemplateTest`) and the image check of the AJAX uploader (L05,
  `ezjscServerFunctionsAjaxUploaderImageTest`). LoveStack's own test cases for behaviour Exponential has are in the
  suite as well, with thanks to Mugo Web: the autolink operator (LoveStack commit d894b9e885), sorting by the trash
  date (e280ff5d64), the name length limit through `eZNamePatternResolver::resolve()` (95d86f5990), a bare `&` in a
  link address of the simplified XML input (5286068d5b), the REST request parser's recorded requests (30c1260b84,
  09e49ce1ff), a password change that updates the account details of the user attribute (60a40cdc05, a live
  test that rolls back what it writes) and `eZSiteData::create()` and `createNew()` (55029bd473, 4a27ffecb4; the
  stored row in a live test that rolls back).

## Required packages (root composer.json, 2026-10-10 to 2026-10-12)

The constraints of the root `composer.json` that changed between 2026-10-10 and the last commit of 2026-10-12.

| Package | Required at the end of 2026-10-12 | Why it was added or repinned |
|---|---|---|
| expai | ~0.0.1.0 | new: Exponential Intelligence, the writing assistant of the administration interface |
| expqueue | ~0.1.0 | new: the job queue for Exponential |
| exponential-velocity (suggested) | ~0.0.4.51 | the warm-up's request ends before the pool forks, and a request's `$_SESSION` ends with it (0.0.4.51); a HEAD over HTTP/2 is its headers only and a stop drains its connections (0.0.4.50) |
| exp_adminui | ~1.0.0.9 | the content finder box in the content structure menu (1.0.0.7), the Open Graph image (1.0.0.8) and the license block (1.0.0.9) of its metadata edit form |
| xrowmetadata | ~1.4.10 | an Open Graph image per object and per class; content licenses at installation, class and object level, from a catalogue of 1,332 licenses |
| sevenx-themes-media | ~1.5.54 | one `og:image` per page (1.5.51); the Sign Up button of the login page only for a visitor who may register (1.5.54) |
| cjw_themes_jumper | ~0.1.3 | the starter design prints Open Graph and Twitter tags (0.1.2); the register link of its login page only for a visitor who may register (0.1.3) |
| explayouts / explayouts-core / explayouts-ui / explayouts-ui-api | ~1.4.24 / ~1.0.7 / ~1.3.14 / ~1.3.15 | a block copy takes its collections, query and child blocks along; the layouts lists show the minutes of the modification time; the layouts pages belong to the Layouts tab and the template editor works again, on `.tpl` files in its allowed roots only (1.4.24) |
| ezoracle | ~2.3.6 | long multibyte texts load; the reserved column names `comment` and `mode` |
| xrowextract | ~2.5.9 | passes its CI check on PHP 8.1 to 8.5 |
| sevenx_authentication_2fa | ~1.0.6 | allows PHP 8.0 like the kernel |

## 15 October 2026: 89 new locales and one page for every language

Content can now be written in the major languages of the world: 89 locale files were added to `share/locale`
(138 in all), and every language Exponential supports, at which level, and how to add more is described in one
place, [Languages](../../eng-us/languages.md).

### Added
- 89 locales, generated at build time from Unicode CLDR 44 (ICU 74.2; Unicode License v3, named in each file):
  South Asia `ben-BD`, `ben-IN`, `urd-PK`, `urd-IN`, `mar-IN`, `tel-IN`, `tam-IN`, `pan-IN`, `pnb-PK`, `snd-PK`,
  `guj-IN`, `kan-IN`, `mal-IN`, `ori-IN`, `asm-IN`, `bho-IN`, `nep-NP`, `sin-LK`, `pus-AF`, `eng-IN`; Southeast and
  East Asia `vie-VN`, `tha-TH`, `msa-MY`, `fil-PH`, `mya-MM`, `khm-KH`, `lao-LA`, `jav-ID`, `chi-SG`, `eng-SG`,
  `mon-MN`, `uig-CN`; Middle East and Central Asia `fas-IR`, `ara-EG`, `ara-AE`, `kur-TR`, `aze-AZ`, `kaz-KZ`,
  `uzb-UZ`, `kat-GE`, `hye-AM`, `tgk-TJ`; Africa `swa-KE`, `swa-TZ`, `amh-ET`, `hau-NG`, `yor-NG`, `ibo-NG`,
  `pcm-NG`, `zul-ZA`, `xho-ZA`, `afr-ZA`, `som-SO`, `orm-ET`, `tir-ET`, `kin-RW`, `mlg-MG`, `sna-ZW`, `eng-ZA`,
  `por-AO`; Europe `ron-RO`, `bul-BG`, `slv-SI`, `lit-LT`, `lav-LV`, `est-EE`, `isl-IS`, `gle-IE`, `cym-GB`,
  `eus-ES`, `glg-ES`, `sqi-AL`, `mkd-MK`, `bos-BA`, `bel-BY`, `mlt-MT`, `ltz-LU`, `ger-AT`, `ger-CH`, `dut-BE`,
  `fre-CH`, `ita-CH`, `eng-IE`; Americas `esl-AR`, `esl-CO`, `esl-CL`, `esl-PE`, `esl-US`, `que-PE`. Existing
  language codes are reused for regional variants (`ger`, `esl`, `fre`, `dut`, `ita`, `ara`, `eng`, `chi`, `por`);
  a new language gets its ISO 639-2/T code (`ben`, `urd`, `vie`, `fas`, `ron` ...), Western Punjabi in Shahmukhi
  script `pnb` and Nigerian Pidgin `pcm` (ISO 639-3). Each file is UTF-8 with names in English and in the language,
  the BCP 47 tag, date and time formats, day and month names, the first day of the week, number and currency formats
  and country names. Where CLDR's 12-hour clock uses a marker other than am/pm, the file uses the 24-hour clock,
  since `eZLocale` only prints am/pm. The right-to-left languages (`fas`, `urd`, `pus`, `pnb`, `snd`, `uig`,
  `ara`) carry a comment; `eZLocale` has no direction setting.
- [Languages](../../eng-us/languages.md): locales against administration translations, content languages and siteaccesses;
  the support levels (full interface and content, partial interface, content only); every locale with its English
  and native name, region, direction and release; every administration translation with its completeness measured
  two ways (its own file, and the 13,620 strings of today: 100% `eng-US`, 99% `ger-DE`, 20 to 48% for the others);
  the 38 languages with 50 million or more speakers and their locales (all 38 now, 18 before) and translations;
  the common languages still missing; how to add a locale (every key, the code convention, the generation, the
  test) and an administration translation; the right-to-left status; the limit of 62 content languages. Linked from
  [Translations and languages](../../eng-us/features/6.0/translations-and-languages.md),
  [Content languages](../../eng-us/guides/content-languages.md) and the README.
- Test `expLocaleFilesTest` (no database, no PHP extension): every `share/locale/*.ini` is valid UTF-8, parses with
  `eZINI`, loads in `eZLocale` with every key it reads, formats a date, time, number and amount and reads the number
  back; the generated files also follow the code, charset, HTTP tag, one-byte separator, license and
  right-to-left conventions.

### Updated
- Setup > Languages > Add a language offers 132 locales instead of 43. An entry no longer repeats the country when the name already gives it: `German (Austria) · Deutsch (Österreich) · ger-AT`
  instead of `... · Austria · ger-AT`; the search still finds the country (`Translations::localeChoices()`).
- Setup > Languages says what the limit counts: the add form reads "%used of at most %max content languages are
  in use. Only the languages added here count, not the locales in the list below; removing a language no content
  uses frees a place.", the overview "More can be added (at most %max content languages)", and the refusal at the
  limit says to remove a language that no object or class uses (admin and admin4; eng-US and ger-DE). Why the limit
  is 62 (one bit of the 64-bit `language_mask` per language, bit 0 for "always available") and why it is not
  raised (a schema change for SQL that reads the masks) is in [Languages](../../eng-us/languages.md#11-the-limit-of-62-content-languages).
- `ser-SR.ini`: Thursday was stored in ISO-8859-2 inside a UTF-8 file (an invalid byte instead of `Č`) and misspelt
  `Četvrak`; it is `Čet` and `Četvrtak` now.

No PHP extension is needed at run time: locales are plain INI files, `intl` was used only to generate them. Nothing
changes on an installation until a language is added under Setup > Languages.

## 15 October 2026: references on every removal page, imported ids of the media theme

### Updated
- The removal confirmation of the exp_adminui, ezwebin and ezdemo designs lists the objects, Layouts blocks and
  further references that use the items, like the admin, admin4 and standard designs (the reverse relation check,
  content.ini `[RemoveSettings] ReverseRelationCheck`); with `block` their Confirm/OK button stays disabled.
- explayouts 1.4.27 brings the provider that lists Layouts blocks whose collections name a removed or hidden node.
- sevenx_themes_media 1.5.58 ships its `contentcheck.ini [ReferenceSettings] ImportedIdRemotePatterns[]` and
  `ImportedIdObjectPatterns[]`, so the content checks of a fresh installation resolve the ids its enhanced links and
  layout components keep from the media site demo and report no false broken links.
- Required packages: explayouts ~1.4.27, exp_adminui ~1.0.0.25, ezwebin ~6.0.18 (also the password change page of
  6.0.15), ezdemo ~6.0.10 (likewise), sevenx-themes-media ~1.5.58.
- admin4 and admin4l: under forced colors the left menu's group titles (`#leftmenu-title-*`, the groups' summaries)
  and the content tree's title name `CanvasText`, so a contrast check measures what is painted (axe found 1.35:1 on
  them with a dark canvas: `-webkit-text-fill-color` kept the light ink).

## 15 October 2026: each translation once in the German catalogue

### Updated
- `share/translations/ger-DE/translation.ts`: 88 messages that appeared twice (same context, source and comment, left
  by merges) are now there once, and `eng-US` loses its one duplicate. Where the copies differed (46 messages, almost
  all in the setup wizard and its system checks) the copy kept is the one the translator already showed, the later
  one, with the corrected spelling and German text instead of English; the earlier copy is kept for "Extensions" in
  `design/admin/setup/info` and `design/standard/setup` ("Erweiterungen"), and the registration notice names 7x,
  as its English source does. Both catalogues still validate against `schemas/translation/ts.rng`.
- The German administration's header (search, user menu, site menu, color mode, side bar buttons, phone menu) is
  German again: an installation whose copy of the catalogue did not validate compiled its templates with the English
  texts, which stayed after the catalogue was corrected until the template cache was cleared.

### Added
- Test `eZTSTranslatorTest::testMaintainedTranslationHasNoDuplicateMessage`: the eng-US, ger-DE and untranslated
  catalogues hold no message twice.

## 15 October 2026: no "already defined" warnings from the admin4 menus and translations

### Updated
- admin4's left menus (`parts/ini_menu.tpl`) no longer write "Variable 'section' is already defined" and
  "Variable 'groups' is already defined" into the warning log on every page. The pagelayout shares its namespace
  with the variables a module's view sets for the page (section/view sets `section`, state/groups `groups`), so the
  menu's own `section`, `groups`, `group_id` and `group_list` now carry the prefix `ini_menu_`; its closing undef
  also no longer removes the view's variables.
- The node view's Translations tab (`translations.tpl`) keeps the main language's name in a variable of its own,
  `a4_tr_main_language_name`: the node view (`node/view/a4_full_body.tpl`) still has `a4_main_language_name` when it
  draws the tabs, and each node view wrote "Variable 'a4_main_language_name' is already defined".
- Require ezupdate 1.1.18, whose page header takes an empty error or message without the warnings
  "Variable 'error' is already defined" and "Variable 'message' is already defined" on each of its pages.

## 15 October 2026: the media design's registration completes again

### Updated
- The registration form of the media design (`user/register`) goes on to the success page again, with the
  activation mail where `[UserSettings] VerifyUserType=email`: the form disabled its Register and Discard buttons
  inside the submit event, and a submit button disabled while that event runs is left out of the posted data, so

  1.5.60 disables them on the next tick, as its shop forms already do.

### Added

  submit buttons inside the submit event (as the submit listener itself, in a submit listener or in `onsubmit`).

## 15 October 2026: the German administration in German below the header

### Updated
- `lib/ezi18n/classes/eztstranslator.php`: a message with an empty translation (the placeholder of a message nobody
  translated yet) no longer replaces a translation read before it. Extension catalogues are read after the kernel's,
  and some carry copies of kernel contexts with empty placeholders: ezstarrating's copy of `kernel/content` turned
  "Top Level Nodes" at the top of the content tree back into English. A later message with a translation still wins.
- The German and English catalogues have the messages the administration asked for and did not find: the content
  job pages (section assignment, durations), the template view's override messages, the RAD page's content policy
  limitation and cronjob part filter, the workflow event statuses, the SVG upload error, the content finder and
  package messages, the navigation parts AdminAid and eZFind, and the audit console's labels for the content finder,
  sessions, deactivated users, API keys, paused cronjob parts and removed packages. The setup wizard's editor
  siteaccess texts and package download errors are German too.
- German translations that were English or unfinished: "Erweiterungen", "Sitzungen" and "PDF-Export" in the Setup
  menu, "Ablagen" on the package list, the user and status IDs, "Global (Überschreibung)", the RAD descriptions of
  datatypes and template operators, and the AJAX uploader, wishlist, OAuth admin and search messages that were marked
  unfinished. The active extensions cache is "Cache der aktiven Erweiterungen" (it read "Erweitungen").
- Setup > Cache management: the caches an extension declares in `site.ini` (`[Cache_<name>] name=`) are translated in
  `kernel/cache` like the kernel's own; the REST and ezjscore packer caches have German names.
- Setup > RAD: the labels of the survey counts are translated once, in PHP (`expRADSurvey::groupCounts()`); the page
  translated them a second time, and each one wrote a missing translation to the debug output.
- Additional node view tabs (`admininterface.ini [AdditionalTab_<tab>]`) can name the context of their Title and
  Description with `TranslationContext=`; without it they are shown as written, as before.
- The maintenance block of the dashboard reads "for more information" (it read "infomation"), in the template and in
  every catalogue's source.
- Require explayouts 1.4.28, whose Layouts and Authors tabs name their translation context ("Autoren" in German),
  and xrowextract 2.5.14, whose preset summaries read in the language of the page.
- Require explayouts 1.4.29: in 1.4.28 a comment in `settings/admininterface.ini.append.php` named
  `translations/*/translation.ts`, and its `*/` closed the PHP comment that wraps the file, so the settings parsers
  read a broken file (expIniRoundTripTest failed). 1.4.29 rewords the comment.

## 15 October 2026: the interface language, and a UI switch framework for interface choices

### Added
- **Interface language** (#506): a signed-in user of the administration chooses the language the interface is shown
  in, in the right toolbar right after Bookmarks (box *Interface*, choice *Interface language*). It sets
  `site.ini [RegionalSettings] Locale` in memory for that user's requests: translations, date and number formats,
  the page's `lang` and `Content-Language`. Content languages are never changed. The choice is kept in the session
  for that siteaccess and that user and ends at logout; `RememberPreference=enabled` also keeps it in the user's
  preferences. Keyboard: arrow keys, Home, End and Escape in the list; without JavaScript the list is shown open.
  [Interface language](../../eng-us/features/6.0/ui-switch.md).
- The UI switch framework behind it, open to extensions: `expUISwitchProviderInterface`, the base class
  `expUISwitchProvider`, the registry `expUISwitch` (called from `ezpKernelWeb::requestInit()` after the session
  start, before the locale and any template) and the first provider `expUISwitchLanguageProvider`.
  [The provider API](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/ui-switch.md).
- The module `uiswitch`: `uiswitch/set` (POST only, form token, refuses `Sec-Fetch-Site: cross-site`, strict
  allow-list, redirects only to a local path) and `uiswitch/state` (JSON); one policy function per provider, built
  from `ProviderList[]`, so `uiswitch/language` and any extension's `uiswitch/<identifier>`. Listed in
  `settings/module.ini`.
- `fetch( 'uiswitch', 'toolbar' )`, the toolbar tool `admin_ui_switch` (`design/admin4/templates/toolbar/full/admin_ui_switch.tpl`,
  `uiswitch/choices.tpl`), the page of a refused change (`design/standard/templates/uiswitch/refused.tpl`).
- `settings/uiswitch.ini`: `[UISwitchSettings] Enabled`, `SiteAccessList[]` (empty: every administration
  siteaccess), `ProviderList[]`; `[UISwitch_language] Class`, `Enabled`, `AllowedValues[]` (eng-GB, eng-US, ger-DE;
  a locale is offered only with its locale file and, except English, its translation), `RememberPreference`.
  [UI switch settings](../../eng-us/guides/ui-switch-settings.md).
- AdminUI: the side bar plugin `uiswitch`, the dropdown *Interface* after the bookmarks
  (`exp_adminui.ini [MenuPlugins] Enabled[]=uiswitch`).
- admin4l: the block *Admin: interface choices (language)* (`admin_ui_switch`) for admin layouts, and the block in
  the right zone after Bookmarks in the admin layouts fragment of explayouts.
- The audit event `access.uiswitch.change` (channel `access`, severity info), with the value and siteaccess before
  and after.
- Translations of the new texts in eng-US and ger-DE (contexts `kernel/uiswitch`, `design/admin/toolbar/uiswitch`,
  `design/standard/uiswitch`).
- Tests: `tests/tests/kernel/classes/uiswitch/expUISwitchTest.php` (18 tests, DB-less).

### Updated
- The cache variant key of a choice (`$GLOBALS['expUISwitchVariantKey']`, for example `language-ger-DE`) keeps
  caches apart: `{cache-block}` keys, the content view cache hash (key `uiswitch`) and compiled template names; the
  role-aware HTTP page cache does not store a page rendered with a choice (`ui switch`). Without a choice the key is
  empty and no cache name changes.
- New installations: the editor siteaccess's right toolbar is Bookmarks, the interface choices, the current user and
  the preferences; the admin and adminui toolbars list `admin_ui_switch` after Bookmarks; the Editor role gets
  `uiswitch/language` in the clean data (policy 332) and in the multi-site installers.

### Upgrading
- The choice appears on an existing installation only after `AvailableToolArray[]=admin_ui_switch` and
  `Tool[]=admin_ui_switch` (after `Tool[]=admin_bookmarks` in `[Toolbar_admin_right]`) are added to the admin
  siteaccesses' `toolbar.ini.append.php`, or the block to the admin layout's right zone on admin4l, followed by a
  clear of the INI and template caches. Roles other than Administrator need `uiswitch/language`. The extension
  bcswitchadminlanguage is replaced and must not be installed alongside. See
  [UI switch settings](../../eng-us/guides/ui-switch-settings.md#4-the-toolbar-on-an-existing-installation).

## 15 October 2026: signed links in the administrator's registration mail

### Added
- The administrator's "New user registered" mail (`user/registrationfeedback.tpl`) is an HTML mail with a
  plain-text part (`multipart/alternative`, with every mail transport). It lists the account's name, login, e-mail
  address, registration time and state (waiting for the e-mail confirmation, for an administrator or for an approval
  item, pending, active), and carries signed links into the administration: View account, Activate account (Approve
  when an approval item holds it), Reject and remove, the approval item and the list of unactivated users. A link is
  an HMAC-SHA-256 token under a key derived from the site secret, names one account and one action, expires after
  `[UserSettings] RegistrationActionTTL` (7 days); approve and reject links work once
  (`expRegistrationAdminAction`). Settings: `RegistrationAdminLinks`, `RegistrationAdminBaseURL`,
  `RegistrationAdminSiteAccess`, `RegistrationActionTTL`. Guide:
  [Signed links in the "New user registered" mail](../../eng-us/features/6.0/registration-admin-links.md).
- The page `user/registration/<token>` in the administration: needs a signed-in user with `user/activation` (or the
  approver of the waiting approval item; a view link also with `content/edit` on the account), shows the account and
  its state, and approves or rejects only after a confirmation posted with the form token; opening a link changes
  nothing. Approve activates the account as Users > Unactivated users does, or accepts the approval item and
  publishes the account at once; reject denies the approval item and removes the account; an active account is never
  changed. An invalid, changed, used or wrong link is refused with 403, an expired one with 410. Audited as
  `access.registration.approve`, `access.registration.reject` and `access.registration.refused`.
- One layout for HTML mails, `design:mail/layout.tpl`, with its plain-text twin `design:mail/layout_text.tpl`:
  content templates pass text and links, the layout holds the styles. A design overrides it for its own colors and
  logo; the media design does.

### Updated
- The registration mail no longer links to `http://<host>/content/view/full/<main node>`: the account is not
  published when the mail goes out, so the node did not exist yet, and the link used `http`.
- The mails a new user gets are HTML mails with a plain-text part through the same layout: the activation mail
  (`user/registrationinfo.tpl`, with an Activate account button), the activation mail sent again from Users >
  Unactivated users (`user/unactivated_resend_mail.tpl`) and the "registration approved" mail
  (`user/registrationapproved.tpl`, with a Sign in button). Their links use `https`; the activation mail's
  `http://<host>/content/view/full/` link without a node is gone. The senders set the body through
  `expMailTemplateBody::apply()`, which makes the multipart mail when a template sets `content_type` text/html and
  `body_text`.

- Required packages: sevenx-themes-media ~1.5.65 (the mail layout of the media design, with the site logo).

## 15 October 2026: the country lists start with the most used countries, and autofill picks the right one

### Added
- **Grouped country list** (#517): every country select shows *Most used countries* first, ranked (the United
  States, Canada and Mexico on top, then South America, Europe, the United Kingdom, Asia, New Zealand, Australia,
  Africa), then *All countries* A-Z by the translated name in the reader's collation (intl's Collator, or accents and
  case folded without it), as native option groups. Guide:
  [The grouped country list](../../eng-us/features/6.0/country-groups.md).

  (ISO alpha-2 codes, ranked), `RepeatInAllCountries`. 37 countries ship in the group `common`.

  `design:country/options.tpl`, which every country select includes; group labels in eng-US and ger-DE (context
  `kernel/country`).

### Updated
- Autofill with a United States address picks the United States, not the United States Minor Outlying Islands: the
  United States is the first option, the selects carry `autocomplete="country"` (the country attribute, which posts
  codes) or `autocomplete="country-name"` (the shop, which posts names), and names are no longer shortened to 20
  characters. Checked in Chromium and WebKit.

  `shop/country/edit.tpl` (account form of every design, VAT rules, user country toolbar) use the shared options. A
  single select repeats the ranked countries in the A-Z group and selects a stored value once; a multiple select
  lists each country once. The shop's select starts with an empty *Choose a country* option when no country is
  known, and takes an optional `select_class` and `select_autocomplete`.
- Stored values are unchanged (codes for the attribute, names in the orders), so no migration is needed.

  exp_adminui ~1.0.0.28 (the country attribute's selects in AdminUI).

## 15 October 2026: every language in the interface language list

### Added
- **The interface language list offers every language** (#516): the *Interface language* choice lists the 33 most
  needed locales in three groups (*English*, *Major world languages*, *Regional variants*, ranked by speakers and
  share of web content), then every locale of `share/locale` A–Z (about 130). Each entry gives the language's own
  name, its name in the current interface language and how complete the kernel's translation is ("Deutsch
  (Deutschland) — German (Germany) (99%)"); a language without a translation is offered with English texts and its
  own formats, marked "no translation yet". The A–Z list follows the collation of the current interface language
  (Intl), with a fallback without Intl. See [The interface language list](../../eng-us/features/6.0/ui-switch-languages.md).

  type-ahead and an Apply button, the same in admin4, admin4l, AdminUI and the classic admin design.
- `expUISwitchLanguageCatalogue`: the names, HTTP codes and translation coverage of every locale, cached in memory and
  under the cache directory (`uiswitch/`), built again by itself when a locale or translation file changes.
- uiswitch.ini `[UISwitch_language]`: `GroupList[]`, `GroupLabel[]`, `GroupValues_<group>[]`, `AllLanguages`,
  `ListUntranslated`. See [UI switch settings](../../eng-us/guides/ui-switch-settings.md#uiswitch_language).

### Updated
- The allow-list of the language choice is the siteaccess's Locale, the groups, `AllowedValues[]` and, with
  `AllLanguages=enabled`, every locale file; `AllowedValues[]` is empty by default. Without the new settings the
  choice behaves as before (only translated languages, a list of buttons).

## 15 October 2026: the test language Lorem Ipsum

### Added
- **A test language for content translations** (#523): `share/locale/qlo-ZZ.ini`, *Lorem Ipsum*, a code from the
  ISO 639-2 range reserved for local use, English formats and lorem-style month and day names, marked
  `[LanguageSettings] TestLanguage=true`. It is shipped but added nowhere: Setup > Languages > Add a language offers
  it like any locale, for a test, to be removed again afterwards. The interface language choice leaves test
  languages out of its ranked groups and its A-Z list unless `AllowedValues[]` names them.

  with lorem ipsum text per datatype (text line, text block, rich text, keywords), with `--dry-run`. It refuses a
  real language and never adds one. See [The test language Lorem Ipsum](../../eng-us/guides/test-language-lorem-ipsum.md)
  and [Languages, section 12](../../eng-us/languages.md#12-test-languages-lorem-ipsum).

## 15 October 2026: the package wizard is a step wizard

### Updated
- **The package creation wizard is drawn again** (#520): *Setup > Packages > Create new package* and every step of the
  content class, content object, extension and site style wizards, in admin4, admin4l and the classic administration.
  Each step shows the wizard's steps (the current one marked with `aria-current="step"`), "Step n of m", what it
  asks for, its fields in cards with labels and help, and one primary action (**Continue**, on the last step
  **Create package**) beside **Back** and **Cancel**, in a bar that stays in view. An error of the last Continue is
  listed at the top with a link to its field and shown under the field (`aria-invalid`, `aria-describedby`). The
  last step reviews the whole package (name, version, format, license, maintainer and what it carries) before it is
  written. The classes and extensions are tables of checkboxes with a filter and a count, the chosen nodes a table
  with their class and URL, the siteaccesses and languages of the content export chips; extensions that may not be
  exported are folded away with the reason once. The posted names, what the creators read and the packages they
  write are unchanged (the same packages, byte for byte apart from their dates). See
  [The package wizard](../../eng-us/features/6.0/package-wizard.md).
- Remove selected on the content step with no row ticked removes nothing; it stopped with an error before.

## 16 October 2026: right-to-left languages, Hebrew, Latin, Klingon and 81 more locales

### Added
- **Text direction of locales**: a locale file can say `[LanguageSettings] TextDirection=rtl`;
  `eZLocale::textDirection()`, `isRightToLeft()` and `textDirectionOf()` read it (template attributes
  `text_direction`, `is_right_to_left`), and `$site.text_direction` carries the direction of the interface language.
  See [Right-to-left languages](../../eng-us/features/6.0/right-to-left.md).
- **Right-to-left administration**: with a right-to-left interface language (UI switch) the pagelayouts of admin4,
  admin4l, the classic admin design and AdminUI print `dir="rtl"`, and the layout skeleton (header, side columns,
  toolbar, tables, forms) is mirrored through logical CSS properties; left-to-right pages are unchanged.
- **Right-to-left content**: text in a right-to-left content language gets its language tag and `dir="rtl"` in the
  edit forms, the admin full view and the rich text editors (TinyMCE 3 and 8, Tiptap); the Add language list marks
  right-to-left languages.
- **81 more locales** (219 in all): every right-to-left language with CLDR data (28, among them Yiddish, Central
  Kurdish, Syriac, Dari, Kashmiri, N'Ko and Dhivehi, and nine more Arabic countries), 62 world languages and regional
  variants from CLDR 44, Latin (`lat-VA`) and Klingon (`tlh-QO`, private-use region QO). See [Languages](../../eng-us/languages.md).
- **Interface translations** (first drafts, to be reviewed by native speakers): Hebrew, Latin and Klingon. Hebrew is
  in the *Major world languages* group of the interface language list.
- Complete administration translations for Spanish (Spain and Mexico), Hebrew, Latin and Klingon, machine-assisted,
  with a quality test for placeholders, markup and whitespace.
- Font fallbacks for CJK, Thai, Devanagari, Arabic and Hebrew script after the Latin families of the admin font
  stack, and line-breaking rules for Japanese, Korean and Thai.

### Updated
- Hebrew (`heb-IL`), Hindi (`hin-IN`) and Korean (`kor-KR`) are generated from CLDR again (double-encoded month
  names, English names, an invalid Korean date pattern); Chinese, Greek, Georgian and Ukrainian get small fixes.
- `eZLocale::localeList()` no longer returns whatever the first call of a request asked for, and sees a locale file
  added while a worker runs; the interface language list does the same (no restart needed for a new language).
- The HTTP cache figures of Setup > System information are translatable.
- **Right to left inside the pages**: the module stylesheets of admin4 and the classic design use logical
  properties, the data table pagers' arrows turn with the direction, and AdminUI 1.0.0.32 mirrors its pages too: its
  own stylesheets use logical properties, a right-to-left copy of its reference stylesheet (`style-rtl.css`, built
  from `style.css`) is loaded in place of it, and the icons that point along the line and the content tree
  column's resize handle follow the direction. Left-to-right pages are unchanged.
- **Names keep their own direction**: node names, the path, page headings, the user name, author lines, relation
  lists, dashboard blocks and the content tree isolate their text (`<bdi>`, `dir="auto"`, and Unicode isolates in the
  page title of a right-to-left page), so an English name in a Hebrew page, or a Hebrew name in an English one, keeps
  its punctuation in place. Left-to-right content shown in a right-to-left interface carries `dir="ltr"` and its
  `lang` (`parts/exp_content_direction.tpl`, `exp_dir_ltr`). AdminUI 1.0.0.33 and 1.0.0.35.
- **Accessible names in the edit form and AdminUI**: the classic design's and AdminUI's attribute labels point at
  their fields, the online editor's frame has a title (TinyMCE 3 and 8), the relation search field, the tags field
  (eztags 2.4.13), the image upload field (ezie 6.0.11), the preview toggle (expui 1.0.0.7) and AdminUI's sort, section
  and class list controls have names; every page checked reaches 0 serious or critical accessibility findings, light
  and dark. AdminUI 1.0.0.34, expui 1.0.0.8.
- The accent text color (`--a4-accent-text`, light) is darker, so an accent badge reaches 4.5:1 on a hovered or
  striped table row.
- The package wizard's creator choices wrap inside their cards at narrow widths, with their descriptions in normal
  weight; the node view's action buttons wrap instead of running past the card with long words (Latin, German).

## 15 October 2026: the classic menus in the Design menu, Site and URLs reordered

- **Menus (classic)** (`visual/menuconfig`) left the Setup menu and the Setup overview (#528): it is a link of the
  Design tab's left menu now, after Toolbar management, in admin4, admin4l, the classic administration and AdminUI,
  and its page marks the Design tab and draws the Design menu. Its address, its access (`visual/*`), its icon and
  its description are unchanged; with the Design tab left out it is a tile of the Design group of the overview.
- **Site and URLs** in the Setup menu and the Setup overview opens with Preload Sites, RSS and Packages and ends
  with RAD; the other links keep their order (`menu.ini [Leftmenu_setup] GroupLinks_site[]` and `Links[]`).

## 16 October 2026: the upgrade check names the tables of extensions that are not active

### Updated
- The database part of the upgrade check (Setup > Upgrade check, `expSchemaConsistencyReport`) also reads the schema
  files of the extensions that are installed and not active (`share/db_schema.dba`, and the `CREATE TABLE`
  statements of `sql/<engine>/*.sql`). A table in the database that only such an extension names, for example
  `ezfind_elevate_configuration` of ezfind, is no longer a "Table not in the schema" with a DROP statement: it is
  listed as "Table of an extension that is not active", "belongs to ezfind (not active)", with the advice to
  activate the extension or leave the table, since dropping it removes its data. No SQL is written for it, on its
  card, in All statements or in the SQL download, and it does not fail the check. A table that no schema names
  keeps its DROP statement as before. Guide: [upgrade check](../../eng-us/guides/upgrade-check.md).
- Tests: the upgrade check reads the tables of extensions that are not active and writes no DROP for them; a fresh
  installation creates `ezuser.login` UNIQUE on SQLite (from `share/db_schema.dba`, read back and refusing a second
  account with the same login), MySQL and PostgreSQL. A database installed before the index became unique (12
  October 2026) still reports it, with the two statements that change it; `./console exp:user-login-check
  --fix-index` runs them once no two accounts share a login.

## 16 October 2026: admin4 review, account actions, the site menu, the badge and Broken relations

### Fixed
- **Check user accounts** (`user/hygiene`) carries out a bulk Disable, Enable or Unlock only from its own
  confirmation page: Confirm needs the token of that page, kept in the session (`HygieneToken`), as Deactivate and
  move does, so the actions no longer rest on `ezformtoken` alone.
- Check user accounts and Deactivate and move count an account as disabled or enabled only when the
  `user/setsettings` operation continued. A workflow that cancelled or halted it left the account enabled, while
  the result said it was disabled and Deactivate and move moved it.
- An administration without `RelatedSiteAccessList[]` lists only itself beside the site in the site menu
  (`site.ini [AdminSiteAccessMenu] List=related`), not every siteaccess of `AvailableSiteAccessList[]`.
- The environment badge takes `BadgeDisplay`, `ShowInTitle` and `ShowVersion` in any case and with surrounding
  spaces, as it already took `Level`; `BadgeDisplay=Always` or `ShowInTitle=Disabled` were ignored.
- The links of Broken relations without a filter no longer end in a bare question mark.
  Tests: `expUserHygieneTest`, `expUserDeactivationTest`, `AdminSiteAccessMenuTest`, `AdminEnvironmentBadgeTest`,
  `expContentCheckQueryTest`.

### Upgrade notes
- An installation that relied on the site menu listing every siteaccess without `RelatedSiteAccessList[]` sets
  `RelatedSiteAccessList[]` for its administration, or `[AdminSiteAccessMenu] List=available`.
