## 16 October 2026: a Content Security Policy, report-only on the administration

### Added
- **`site.ini [ContentSecurityPolicy]`**: `Mode` (`disabled`, `report-only`, `enforce`, or `auto`: report-only on
  administration siteaccesses, whose `SiteDesign` begins with `admin`, disabled elsewhere; the default), the policy in
  `Directives[]` (shipped: `default-src`, `script-src` and `style-src` `'self'`, `object-src 'none'`, `base-uri`,
  `form-action` and `frame-ancestors` `'self'`, images and media with `data:`/`blob:`) and `ReportURI`. A report-only
  policy is the header `Content-Security-Policy-Report-Only`; an enforced one is sent as a second policy of
  `Content-Security-Policy` after `SecurityHeaders[Content-Security-Policy]`; `frame-ancestors` and `sandbox` only when
  enforced (browsers ignore them in report-only). The policy is sent also when `SecurityHeaders[]` is not set. Values
  that would split the header are ignored. `object-src 'none'` blocks `<object>`/`<embed>` (the legacy plugin
  previews, inline PDF) once enforced. See [Content Security Policy](../../eng-us/features/6.0/content-security-policy.md).

## 16 October 2026: the administration runs without inline scripts, handlers and styles

### Updated
- **admin4 and admin4l** (and the admin3, admin and standard templates they render) carry no inline `<script>`, no
  `on...=` attribute, no `javascript:` link, no `style=""` attribute and no `<style>` element any more, so they run
  under the shipped Content Security Policy (`script-src 'self'`, `style-src 'self'`). Page scripts are files under
  `design/admin4/javascript/page/`, loaded with `ezscript` where the inline script stood, and take their values
  from `data-*` attributes; the head script is `a4_head.js`. See
  [Content Security Policy](../../eng-us/features/6.0/content-security-policy.md), "The administration without inline code".
- **Context menus** are declared in `data-menu-*` attributes (menu, items, openers) instead of `menuArray` scripts and
  `onmouseover`/`onclick` attributes; menus and items of extensions written the old way keep working, also when they
  add items to a core menu (under report-only; once enforced their inline scripts and handlers do not run - convert
  them before enforcing). A script that assigns `menuArray` anew replaces the declared core menus: add to it instead.
  A menu item links and a redirect entry (`data-menu-redirect`, `ezpopup_SubstituteAndRedirect()`) goes only to an
  address of the site (http or https, the page's origin); a `javascript:`, `data:` or foreign address is refused
  with an error in the console, and the values put into a redirect address are URL-encoded.
- **The content tree** is drawn from `data-tree-*` attributes (JSON) instead of an inline script with
  `document.writeln()`; its open/close buttons have no `onclick`/`onkeydown` attributes.
- **ezjscore**: generated script and style content (server functions with packing off) is a file in the packer's
  cache directory instead of an inline `<script>`/`<style>` (`ezjscPacker::generatedContentURL()`).
- The sub-items status badges use the admin's `a4-badge` (token colours, dark mode) instead of fixed inline colours.
- **ezoe, TinyMCE 8**: the edit template writes the editor settings as JSON data blocks
  (`<script type="application/json" id="ezoe8-settings">`, `id="ezoe8-button-map"`) and each textarea's toolbar in
  `data-ezoe8-*` attributes; `javascript/tinymce8_ez/ezoe8_start.js` starts the editors, so a rich text edit page with
  TinyMCE 8 carries no inline script. `eZOe8Init()`, `eZOe8Toolbar()` and `eZOe8StickyOffset()` stay global; new
  `eZOe8Start()` starts editors added later. TinyMCE 3 and its dialogs are unchanged and still inline (the guide says
  what is left). The data blocks are printed with `<` and `>` as `\u003c` and `\u003e`: `json_encode` leaves `<!--`
  as it is, and `<!--<script>` in a setting would have made the browser read past the block and skip the scripts
  after it, so the editor did not start. Without `tinymce.min.js` the console says why the fields stay plain text.
- **The test of the administration's templates** (`AdminContentSecurityPolicyTest`) follows the chain the kernel
  builds: admin2 is part of it, and the design directories of the active extensions come before the core directory of
  each design. What extensions still have inline is listed per extension (`EXTENSION_ALLOWLIST`) and only shrinks.

### Added
- `design/admin4/javascript/a4_actions.js`: confirmations, select all, dismiss, submit or go on change and focus,
  declared with `data-a4-*` attributes.

### Fixed
- Package > Compare and import: All/None and the class cascade work (their inline script had a syntax error and never
  ran).

## 16 October 2026: oauth/authorize redirects only to a client's end point, the publishing status needs read access

### Fixed
- `oauth/authorize` sent an error for a missing client ID, an unknown client or a mismatching `redirect_uri` to the
  `redirect_uri` of the request, so any address could be reached through the view (an open redirect). Until the
  `redirect_uri` is the end point of a registered client the view now answers `400 Bad Request` without a
  `Location` (RFC 6749, section 4.1.2.1); a `redirect_uri` or `client_id` given as an array is refused the same way.
  Errors after that check (a missing `response_type`, `access_denied`) still go to the client's end point.
- The ezjscore server function `ezpublishingqueue::status` told any caller, also an anonymous one, whether a version
  of an object exists and its publishing status, and the address of the node once it was published. It answers now
  a user who can read the object, or the registered user who created the version and waits for it; the address of
  the node only a user who can read the object. For anyone else the answer is the one of a version that does not
  exist ("Object version not found"), and arguments that are not positive integers get the same answer.
- The asynchronous publishing page (`content/queued`) hides its link "View the published item" when the status
  comes without the node's address, instead of leaving it pointing at `#`.

## 16 October 2026: ezurl and ezroot escape an absolute link in quotes

### Fixed
- `ezurl` and `ezroot` gave a link with a scheme (`https:`, `mailto:`, `javascript:`, `data:`) or a protocol-relative
  `//host` one back unescaped, also when they put it in quotes for an HTML attribute; a stored link with a `"` closed
  the attribute (`<a href={$url|ezurl}>`). In quotes (no quote parameter, `'double'`, `'single'`) such a link is now
  escaped as the links the operators build already are (an `&amp;` already in it stays as it is). Relative links and
  quote `'no'` are unchanged; `eZURI::transformURI()` itself is unchanged. Compiled templates take the change after
  the template cache is cleared.
  In a `<script>` block, JSON or a CSS `url()` such a link in quotes now carries `&amp;` for `&`, as relative links
  already did; there use quote `'no'` with `|wash( 'javascript' )` or `|json_encode`.
- The filter redirects of `package/list`, `package/compare` and `package/view` send their address without HTML
  escaping, so an `&` in it no longer reaches the `Location` header as `&amp;`.

## 16 October 2026: online editor hardening

### Updated
- **The custom tag dialog** (`ezoe/tags/<id>/<version>/custom/<name>`) opens only for a name of content.ini
  `[CustomTagSettings] AvailableCustomTags[]` (or no name, for a new tag); any other name gets the
  "Invalid parameter" message. `tag_custom.tpl` escapes the name in its script string and its class attribute as
  well: a link with a crafted name ran script on the administration for the editor who opened it. The
  "Invalid parameter" messages of the view escape the value they repeat.
- **`ezoe/embed_view`** shows an embed whose EmbedID has no `_`, whose node or object no longer exists, as the
  unknown embed; it ended in a fatal error (HTTP 500) before, which broke the editor on an old draft. The EmbedID is
  read with `Relations::parseEmbedId()` like `ezoe/relations` and `ezoe/load`, the `view` parameter must be the tag's own view
  (`embed`, or `embed-inline` for an inline embed) or one of content.ini `AvailableViewModes[]` of that tag's
  sections only (`[embed]` and `[embed_<class>]`, or `[embed-inline]` and `[embed-inline_<class>]`), otherwise the
  tag's own view is used, and `class`, `size`, `align` and `view` are read as strings.
- **The upload of the online editor refuses executable types for every engine**: a file name with `php`, `phtml`,
  `phar`, `sh`, `cgi`, `.htaccess` and the like anywhere in it is refused in every ezoe.ini
  `[EditorSettings] UploadExtensionCheck` mode, `disabled` included. Every dot-separated part of the name is checked,
  not only the last one, so `report.pl.pdf` or `shell.php.jpg` is refused too; rename such a file before uploading. TinyMCE 3 users skipped the check in the default
  mode `engine`, so `x.php` was accepted, which is dangerous on a web server without the `.htaccess` rules (nginx).
  `UploadExtensionCheck` still decides about the list `UploadFileExtensions[]`.

### Removed
- **The After the Deadline spell checker** of the TinyMCE 3 editor: the view `ezoe/atd_rpc`, which sent the editor's
  text over plain HTTP to `service.afterthedeadline.com` (a service that no longer answers) and appended the debug
  report to the answer, the plugin `javascript/plugins/AtD`, the commented `Plugins[]=AtD` and `Buttons[]=AtD` and
  the `[AtD]` block of ezoe.ini, and the `atd_` options of the editor template. A siteaccess that still lists
  `Plugins[]=AtD` or `Buttons[]=AtD` in an override should drop them; TinyMCE 3 skips a plugin it cannot find.
  The spell checker of TinyMCE (`ezoe/spellcheck_rpc`) stays.

## 16 October 2026: exact link registration, formula text in the online editor, ForceEngine

### Added
- **ezoe.ini `[EditorSettings] ForceEngine=<engine>`** (per siteaccess, not set by default) gives every editor of a
  siteaccess one engine of the online editor, whatever their own choice on `ezoe/engine` says; the engine page then
  shows a note instead of its form. An engine that cannot be used (its files or its extension missing) is ignored, so
  editors keep the usual choice.

### Fixed
- **`eZURL::registerURL()` and `eZURL::urlByURL()` match an address byte for byte.** The `url` column compares with
  its collation (MySQL `utf8mb4_general_ci`: case and accents ignored), and `registerURL()` rewrote the row it found to
  the new spelling, so saving a text that links `.../Present_perfect` re-pointed every link stored as
  `.../Present_Perfect` (another page) to it. A different spelling gets a row of its own now
  (`eZURL::fetchExactByUrl()`, `eZURL::pickExact()`), and `urlByURL()` finds it, where it returned false for two
  collation-equal rows and the rich text datatype stored another row on every save.
- **Online editor**: a custom tag whose text is `0` keeps it (it became a space), and line breaks inside a custom tag
  (a LaTeX formula) become a space instead of gluing the lines together.
- `eZContentObject::versionLanguageName()` with version 0 or a negative version returns false without a PHP warning
  and without a database query.
- `oauth/authorize` refuses an empty `redirect_uri` also for a client stored without an end point (400 Bad Request).
- The online editor's upload refuses an executable type followed by white space, NUL or a dot (`x.php `, `x.php.`)
  and also `phtm`, `php6` and `shtml`; the engine page escapes the engine names it shows.

### Renamed
- `ezpContentSecurityPolicy` (new in this release) is `expContentSecurityPolicy`
  (`kernel/private/classes/expcontentsecuritypolicy.php`). The security headers are sent without the policy while a
  long-running process does not know the class yet.

### Updated
- `AdminContentSecurityPolicyTest` checks the core designs and the extensions this repository ships (expservices,
  ezformtoken, ezjscore, ezoe). Extensions installed from their own repositories are checked with
  `CSP_ALL_EXTENSIONS=1`, an audit of an installation before the policy is enforced (see
  [Content Security Policy](../../eng-us/features/6.0/content-security-policy.md), "Before enforcing").

## 16 October 2026: the SQLite driver counts its statements without SQL output

### Fixed
- The SQLite driver counts every statement in the accumulator `sqlite3_query`, as the MySQL and PostgreSQL drivers
  do, so the debug output and the debug bar's summary give the number of queries and their time also with
  `site.ini [DatabaseSettings] SQLOutput=disabled`; they said "0 SQL / 0 ms" while the SQL query cache counted its
  misses. With `SQLOutput` on, a statement faster than `SlowSQLTimeout` left the accumulator running and was not
  counted either. Which statements are listed is unchanged. Test: `eZSQLite3DBQueryCountTest`.

## 16 October 2026: the debug bar's Velocity tab is called Server

### Updated
- The Exp Debug bar's tab that says where the request entered and which process rendered the page is called
  **Server** instead of Velocity: it shows the same for PHP-FPM, mod_php or the command line, where a tab named
  Velocity read as if Velocity were running. Velocity, when it renders the page, is named in the tab itself. The tab's
  ids (`data-tab="velocity"`, `exp-debug-panel-velocity`) are unchanged. German and American English. See
  [the debug bar](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/debug-bar.md#server-where-the-request-entered-and-what-rendered-it).

## 16 October 2026: panels of extensions in the debug bar

### Added
- An extension can add a tab to the Exp Debug bar, after Memory, with a chip in the summary and data in the page's
  JSON: `debugbar.ini [DebugBarSettings] Panels[]=<id>` and `[Panel_<id>] Class=<class>`, the class implementing
  the new `expDebugBarPanelInterface` (`visible()`, `title()`, `chip()`, `html()`, `data()`). The panel decides who
  may see it; an entry without a usable class and a panel that fails are left out with a warning.
  `expDebugBarRegistry::panels()`, `expDebugBarReport::extensionPanels()`. In the extension point catalogue
  (Setup > RAD). See [Panels of extensions](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/debug-bar.md#panels-of-extensions). Test:
  `expDebugBarPanelsTest`.

## 16 October 2026: OPcache, APCu and the versions in the debug bar's summary

### Added
- The summary of the Exp Debug bar shows the memory of OPcache and APCu in use while they are on for the process
  (`OPc 66 %`, `APCu 25 %`, the details in the title), and after them the versions of PHP and Exponential
  (`PHP 8.4.19 | Exp 6.0.15`), all three at the right end; each opens the Server tab, which lists APCu below OPcache. Only for
  users with `setup/setup` or `setup/managecache`, since the bar also shows to anonymous visitors by IP. New
  thresholds `debugbar.ini [DebugBarSettings] Thresholds[opcache_memory]=80;95` and `Thresholds[apcu_memory]=75;90`;
  the server summary (`expDebugBarSummary`) carries `opcache` and `apcu` with their level. German and American
  English. See [the debug bar](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/debug-bar.md). Test: `expDebugBarReportRuntimeChipsTest`.

## 16 October 2026: the debug bar names the PHP version only to administrators

### Fixed
- The Exp Debug bar gave the version of PHP to everybody who sees it, which includes anonymous visitors when
  `site.ini [DebugSettings] DebugByIP` matches: in its server summary (`expDebugBarSummary::collect()`, the JSON of
  every page with the report) and in the PHP row of the Server tab. The summary has null now unless the user may
  use `setup/setup`, the rule for its other lists; the Server tab shows the version to the users who see its
  privileged rows (`setup/setup` or `setup/managecache`) and only the server API to the others. Tests:
  `expDebugBarSummaryPhpVersionTest`, `expDebugBarReportEngineTest`.

## 16 October 2026: the debug bar's Rendering tab

### Added
- **Rendering tab** of the Exp Debug bar, for users with `setup/setup`: the module views that ran with their view
  class and script, the siteaccess, the navigation part and the design order; what the content view cache did (read
  from the cache with the file's age and size, generated, generated without storing, or not used, each with its
  reason) and its key part by part, with the keys of `content/view/cachekeys` listeners marked; the template
  settings and the templates compiled in this request. Two chips, **View: hit/new/off** and **Compiled N**, open it.
  See [the Exp Debug bar](../../eng-us/bc/6.0/debug-bar.md#rendering-controller-content-view-cache-and-compiled-templates).
- `eZNodeviewfunctions::generateViewCacheFile()` also returns `cache_hash`, `cache_keys` and
  `cache_keys_from_listeners`; the three keys it returned before are unchanged.
- **Cache blocks in the Rendering tab**: every `{cache-block}` of the page with its template and line, hit or
  generated, the time to look it up and to generate it, its size, expiry and keys, and the chip **CB hits/count**.
  The compiled code of a cache-block passes its placement to `eZTemplateCacheBlock::handle()` and stores through the
  new `eZTemplateCacheBlock::store()`; clear the template cache once so compiled templates name their blocks.
- **Template tree in the Rendering tab**: every template the page rendered, nested under the template that included
  it, with the file the overrides chose and the time it took (at most 2000, every template counted).
  `debugbar.ini [DebugBarSettings] RenderTree=disabled` leaves it out.

## 16 October 2026: admin4 review, admin4l

### Fixed
- admin4l keys its cache blocks by the admin generation, so an edit of an admin layout or rule shows at once: the
  page read the key with `.result.0`, which the list the fetch operator returns does not have (a template error on
  every page, and a layout id without the generation); the key list is now imploded.
- The GET parameter `SectionID` in admin4l's page cache keys is a number, so odd values no longer create cache files.
- admin4l's tab menu block (`admin_tab_menu`) draws only the list items, so the page has one menu panel instead of
  two nested ones.

### Added
- Block templates `admin_toolbar_right` and `admin_toolbar_developer`: the toolbars `admin_right` and
  `admin_developer` of `toolbar.ini`, as admin4 draws them. Their block definitions belong to `explayouts.ini` of the
  explayouts extension.
  Tests: `Admin4lReviewTest`.

## 16 October 2026: admin4 review, the design tests

### Updated
- The design tests that run admin4 JavaScript in node look node up through `tests/tests/design/AdminNodeBinary.php`:
  without node they are skipped on a developer machine and fail where `CI` is set (GitHub Actions), so a runner
  without node no longer passes the suite with those checks gone.
- The design tests that assert something is absent read their files through `tests/tests/design/AdminFiles.php`: a
  missing file or an empty file list fails instead of passing without looking at anything.
- `AdminMegaMenuTest` runs the mega menu view as the anonymous user and checks that it is refused.

## 16 October 2026: the left column's tab stays on the window's edge on a phone

### Fixed
- admin4 and admin4l: the open left column below 1024px (a drawer over the page) moved its tab to its far edge, by
  the column's width. With a width dragged or chosen on a wide window that put the tab and the width grip in the
  middle of a phone's screen, over the page, and right to left the tab left the screen. The tab now stays fixed on
  the window's edge, open or closed, in the 24 px the drawer's card leaves there, as the right column's tab does.
- On a phone (below 576px) the left column has no width grip, and below 1024px the drawer leaves 48 px of the page
  visible at any saved width (it could be wider than the screen).
- On a touch screen both sidebar tabs are 48 px high (40 px before; their width stays 24 px, the room the cards
  leave at the window's edge).

## 16 October 2026: the Tab order of admin4l's header is admin4's

### Updated
- **admin4l**: the Tab key goes through the header as it does in admin4: logo, menu button, menu, search, site link,
  light/dark switch, user menu, sidebar controls. The menu button and the sidebar controls were one block
  (`admin_sidebar_toggles`), drawn after the tools; they are two now, `admin_menu_button` and
  `admin_sidebar_controls`, and the page draws the logo and the menu button before the menu panel and the rest of the
  header zone after it. A layout stored with `admin_sidebar_toggles` keeps working and keeps its old order (the block
  is made of the two new ones). To give such a layout admin4's order, replace that block in the layout editor with
  `admin_menu_button` right after the logo and `admin_sidebar_controls` at the end of the header zone; nothing
  migrates stored layouts.

## 16 October 2026: the side columns are there on the first paint

### Fixed
- **No layout shift when an admin4 page loads**: the side columns opened only once the document was parsed
  (`ezadmin_menubar.js` adds `.show`), so a reload first drew the content across the whole window and then moved it
  aside for the columns; the dashboard at 1440 px with the left column open had a cumulative layout shift of 0.87
  (0.73 with the default width and both columns). The page layouts of admin4 and admin4l now write
  `html.a4-preload`, and until the script has applied its state `admin4.css` draws the columns from the user's
  eZPreferences on `<html>` (`admin_left_sidebar`, `admin_right_sidebar`, `admin_right_drawer`) with the script's
  rules: open beside the content unless saved closed (left from 1024 px, right from 1280 px), a right drawer saved
  open is open on a tablet-sized window, and phones start closed. No transition runs before the script has run; it
  removes the class two frames after it has applied the state. Measured on the dashboard: 0.003 to 0.009 at
  1440 px, light and dark, right-to-left, custom and default left width; no shift at 1180, 800 and 390 px. An
  anonymous page keeps its choice in the browser: it starts with both columns open, and a column it keeps closed
  closes when the script runs.

## 16 October 2026: the sub items say why they are missing without Exponential UI

### Fixed
- The node view's sub-items table is drawn by Exponential UI (extension `expui`). On a siteaccess where it is not
  active the table's place stayed empty below "Sub items (3)", without a word. It now says that the sub items cannot
  be listed because Exponential UI is not active or not loaded (admin4: `a4-notice is-warn`, admin:
  `message-warning`), as text and with role status. With Exponential UI nothing changes. German and American English. Test:
  `AdminSubitemsWithoutExpUITest`.

## 16 October 2026: explayouts 1.4.32 and 1.4.33: admin layouts with admin4's header and toolbars, the Exp Layouts debug bar panel

### Updated
- Require explayouts 1.4.32. Its stock admin layouts (the admin layouts fragment of new installations) put the tab
  menu in the `topmenu` zone, order the header as admin4 does (logo, menu button, search, theme switch, sidebar
  controls: the new blocks `admin_menu_button` and `admin_sidebar_controls`), and draw the right column of
  `admin_3col` from the toolbars `admin_right` and `admin_developer` (the new blocks `admin_toolbar_right` and
  `admin_toolbar_developer`; the interface choices come through `admin_right`). The single tool blocks, among them
  `admin_ui_switch`, and the combined `admin_sidebar_toggles` stay available, so admin layouts already stored keep
  working unchanged. `[AdminLayoutSettings] SiteAccessMatch[]` also matches siteaccesses named `<site>_admin`.
- The template editor of explayouts edits only the templates of the layout types and blocks:
  `explayouts.ini [TemplateEditorSettings] AllowedTemplateRoots[]` ships the three folders below
  `extension/explayouts/design/standard/templates/explayouts/` (it shipped `design` and `extension`, and an empty
  list fell back to them), and an empty list allows nothing. A site whose design overrides layout templates adds its
  own folder. `AllowNewTemplates=disabled` (new): a template that does not exist yet is created only with
  `enabled`. Symbolic links are never read or written, and the previous version of a saved template is kept in
  `<var directory>/explayouts/template_backup/` instead of a `.tpl.bak` beside it.
- The Layouts tab of the node view in admin4 has no inline script (the page to return to is in
  `data-explayouts-return-to`).
- Require explayouts 1.4.33, which adds the panel *Exp Layouts* to the Exp Debug bar through the kernel's panel API
  (`debugbar.ini [DebugBarSettings] Panels[]=explayouts`): the layout of the page, the rule or default layout that
  chose it, its zones and blocks with links to edit them, and the available blocks. It shows only on public
  siteaccesses and only to users with `explayouts_ui/edit`, so visitors matched by `DebugByIP` do not see layout
  internals. `explayouts.ini [DebugBarSettings] AdminURL` sets the address of an administration on its own host.

## 16 October 2026: the classic administration design on phones and narrow windows

### Updated
- The right column of the classic administration design keeps its tools inside the column: they no longer ran about
  5px past it and past the window's edge at 1440px and at 200% zoom. The interface choices (the design switch and the
  grouped language list) are one full-width button a row, a long name wrapping inside its button.
- Below 760px the classic design works on a phone (`design/admin/stylesheets/pagelayout.css`): the page's content
  comes first, then the left menu, then the right column's tools; the header wraps (logo and logout, the search,
  the tabs); wide tables with links or fields scroll sideways inside their own box, tables of text wrap; fields and
  pictures keep to the width of the column; buttons, tabs and menu links are 44px high. Both page layouts carry the
  viewport meta, and the left menu's width control leaves the content's margin alone while the columns are stacked.
  Wider windows look as before, left to right and right to left.
- Tests: `tests/tests/design/ClassicAdminNarrowWindowTest.php`.

## 16 October 2026: admin4 review, the admin scripts

### Fixed
- The admin frame saves a remembered choice only when it changes: `window.a4Pref.set()` skips a value already on
  `<html>`, and a page view no longer posts one preference per left menu group that was stored closed.
- Changing the color mode for a signed-in user also changes it in the other open admin tabs.
- A click on the left column's grip that does not move it saves nothing, and a width the server did not take is
  kept in the browser.
- The sign-in button shows its own label again after the browser's back button.
- admin4 always loads its rounded theme stylesheet, also when an old `admin_theme` preference names another one.
- A mega menu whose links do not load within 10 seconds says so and loads again on the next opening.
- A relation search result is added once and can be chosen with Tab, Enter and Space; a failed search shows "The
  search failed. Try again."
- Sub-items table options (presets, rows per page, columns) that could not be saved show "Not saved: ..." and are
  not shown as saved.
- The upload dialog shows an error for an empty server answer instead of stopping.
- The sub-items list without its scripts (Exponential UI) keeps the one note of the same day's fix ("The sub items
  cannot be listed ..."); the merge dropped a second note of its own.
- Context menus fill every placeholder, disable only what the opened menu asks for, and a click on a disabled item
  no longer jumps to the top of the page.
- In the content tree a node on the path to the current page is marked also when it shows no sub items.
- A broken tab cookie no longer breaks the node view's script.

### Updated
- The content tree's open/close buttons have no inline handlers; the tree, tab and sub-items column cookies use
  `encodeURIComponent` and `SameSite=Lax` (old values are still read).
- The RSS export and RAD pages read `event.key` instead of `keyCode`.
  Tests: `AdminScriptsBehaviourTest` (node harnesses in `tests/tests/design/js/`), `AdminScriptFailureTextsTest`,
  `AdminPreferencesTest`, `AdminMegaMenuTest`, `AdminKeyboardTest`, `AdminScriptsTest`.

## 16 October 2026: admin4 templates for the copy step, the publishing queue and the locked class

### Added
- **admin4** has its own `content/copy.tpl` and `content/copy_subtree.tpl` (the choice of versions, creator and
  times after the target was chosen), `content/queued.tpl` (the page shown while asynchronous publishing works) and
  `class/edit_locked.tpl` (a class whose changes wait for a cronjob), built from its components (`.a4-radios`,
  `.a4-notice`, `.a4-facts`, `.a4-toolbar`), so they follow the theme and dark mode instead of design/standard's
  unstyled pages. The form names, the variables and the ids the publishing scripts look for are unchanged; no inline
  code.
- **The image editor (ezie) in admin4**: admin4's `content/datatype/edit/ezimage.tpl` hid the copy of the template
  that ezie ships in design/standard, so the editor's Edit button was missing. It now includes
  `content/datatype/edit/ezimage_editor.tpl` beside a stored image, which draws the editor's scripts and its Edit
  button only when `ezmodule( 'ezie/prepare' )` allows it, and nothing otherwise.

## 16 October 2026: the content job page stops polling when it cannot go on; the relation search keeps its text

### Fixed
- **Content job progress** (`content/job/<id>`, `page/content_job.js`): the page stopped asking for the status only
  when the job finished. A job that was removed or that the user may not see (404, 403) and an ended session (the
  request redirected to the login page, or an HTML page in place of the JSON) made it ask every four seconds for as
  long as the tab stayed open. It now stops with a message (*This job no longer exists or you may not see it ...*,
  *You are no longer signed in ...*; `data-text-gone`, `data-text-signed-out` of the template), and so does a script
  error while it shows the status (`data-text-error`). After a network or
  server error the next request waits 4, 8, 16 ... up to 60 seconds and is back at two seconds after an answer, and a
  hidden tab makes no request until it is shown again.
- **"How to run it"** on the confirmation pages of the content jobs (`page/content_job_mode_choice.js`): the
  remembered choice was saved with a GET of `user/preferences/set/...`, which answered with a redirect that rendered
  a whole page, carried no form token and did not check the answer. It is saved through `window.a4Pref`, as the
  other remembered choices of the frame: a POST to `user/preferences/set_and_exit` with the form token. The
  fieldset's `data-prefs` attribute is gone.
- **Relation search of the edit form** (object relation and object relations attributes,
  `ezajaxrelations_jquery.js` of admin4 and admin): the search text was part of the ezjscore call
  (`ezjsc::search::<text>`), where `strip_tags()` removed what looked like a tag and `::` cut it into arguments, so
  `a<b` or `c::d` searched for something else. It is sent as the POST parameter `SearchStr`, which
  `ezjscServerFunctionsJs::search()` reads.

### Removed
- `data-a4-print` and `data-a4-disable-on-submit` in `a4_actions.js`: no template used them, and disabling the
  buttons on a click would have left a form unusable after a cancelled submit or a page restored from the
  back/forward cache.

## 16 October 2026: content checks: the start location and the storage files

Page: [The content checks](../../eng-us/features/6.0/content-checks.md), "Where a check starts", "Storage files" and
"Quarantine".

### Added
- **The start location** of a content check is any node: the presets of `contentcheck.ini [ScopeSettings]
  PresetList[]` (the whole tree, the content, Media, Media/Images, Users) or any node through the shared node picker
  (`contentcheck/scope/<view>/<node id>`, `browse.ini [ContentCheckScope]`). It is kept as the preference
  `admin_contentcheck_scope` of the signed-in user and in the address. Shared selector `contentcheck/scope.tpl`,
  class `expContentCheckScope`; the views that take it are listed in `ViewList[]`.
- **Storage files** (`contentcheck/storage/<node id>`, Setup menu "Storage files"): a batched scan of
  `var/<site>/storage` (`images`, `images-versioned`, `original`) with progress, kept as the last scan. **Orphan
  files**: no `ezimagefile` row, no image XML of the attribute their directory names, no `ezbinaryfile` or `ezmedia`
  row, and no path found by the reference index in fields, layouts, settings or templates; every version and object
  state counts; files younger than `MinAge` (a day) never count. Size, age and totals. **File problems** of used files
  below the start location: another owner than the storage directory's, missing mode bits of
  `StorageFilePermissions`, empty files, a name that differs only in case, an image url that is not its dirpath and
  filename, a file in another MIME type's directory. With a DFS or database cluster the files come from its table,
  report only.
- **Quarantine**, never a delete in the storage: orphans are moved to `var/<site>/storage-quarantine/<date>/` with
  their path, restored from there (never over an existing file), and deleted from there only after a confirmation
  with its own box. **Fix permissions** sets only the expected owner (as root) and mode. Every action is a dry run
  first, then a POST with the form tokens and a confirmation token bound to its files; each file is checked again
  when it is done. Policies `contentcheck/storage`, `contentcheck/quarantine`, `contentcheck/permissions`. Audit
  events `content.file.orphan.quarantine`, `.restore`, `.delete`, `.refused` and `content.file.permissions.fix`.
- **On the command line**: `./console exp:content-check --storage [--quarantine | --fix-permissions] [--dry-run]
  [--json]`.
- **One layout for every check** (unreferenced objects, broken relations, storage files): the start location, the
  filters with **Select all** / **Select none** per group (buttons after Show, read on the server), the results header
  ("X to Y of N"), the table with a master box that ticks every row of the page (mixed state, "n of m selected"; without
  JavaScript "Select all on this page" / "Select none"), the bulk actions with "all N results" (one batch, always the
  plan first), and the pager. Shared parts `contentcheck/results_head.tpl`, `bulkbar.tpl`, `pager.tpl`,
  `filter_choice.tpl`, `select_all.tpl`, `select_count.tpl`, `scope.tpl` and `contentcheck_select.js`.
- Tests: `expContentCheckStorageTest` (no database): the start location and the presets, the node picker view, paths,
  file problems, image XML and file paths, fixing owner and mode, the action view's decisions, restore and delete in
  a throwaway quarantine, the list parameters and the scan state.

### Updated
- The filter form of **Broken relations** took its node with a redirect that the kernel answered with the same
  query again, endlessly: "Check" ended in "too many redirects". It reads the node of the form directly.
- The reference index (format 3) keeps every storage path it found, for the storage files.

### Upgrade notes
- New settings with safe defaults: `contentcheck.ini [ScopeSettings]`, `[StorageSettings]`, `browse.ini
  [ContentCheckScope]`. The new policies are held by Administrator only. Nothing is scanned or moved until someone
  asks for it. On hosts where two users write the storage (an application server as root, PHP-FPM as the site user),
  owner fixes need root: run the command line as root.

## 16 October 2026: admin4 review, values from the database and from packages are escaped

### Fixed
- A package whose `package.xml` carries markup or spaces in its version, release or type is no longer read
  (`eZPackage::parseDOMTree()` refuses it like a package without a name, with an eZDebug error), and the package
  pages of admin and admin4 wash every value taken from a package, including the error message of a JSON document.
  Before, a `<number>` with markup ran as script on `package/view/files` and in the file browser of
  `package/view/full`.
- The admin and admin4 pages escape the hidden fields of the browse page, the template name of the template creator,
  workflow group names, product options of an order, wait-until class names, toolbar parameters, currencies and the
  preferred currency of the shop errors.
- A class name ending in a backslash no longer breaks the sub-items table's script.
- The content tree menu shows each user the classes they may create below the root node, not those of the user who
  drew it first: the root block's cache key carries the roles and limitations.
- The page cache of the administration no longer writes a new copy for every distinct `SectionID` in the address:
  the parameter enters the cache keys as a number.
- White space around the version, release or type of a hand-written `package.xml` (`<number>\n  1.0\n</number>`)
  is trimmed instead of getting the package refused by the new check. All 905 package definitions of the installed
  and downloadable packages (every content class, site and extension package) are still read.
- design/standard, the fallback of every design without its own package pages, escapes the same package values, the
  action IDs of the install and uninstall errors and the package description, which it printed raw.
- The content tree menu keeps its data attributes (no inline script, Content Security Policy) with the roles and
  limitations in its root block's key; the admin4 sub-items table keeps its JSON options, which escape the class
  names for their context already.
  Tests: `eZPackageDefinitionTest`, `AdminStoredValueEscapingTest`.

## 16 October 2026: missing files and image aliases in the broken relations

### Added
- **Missing files**, a target of **Broken relations** (`contentcheck/brokenrelations`, chip **Missing files in
  var/<site>/storage**, `Kind[]=missing_file`): the file-backed fields of the content below the node whose file is
  not in the storage (through the cluster file handler, so a DFS counts), in the published version and in drafts,
  and the images whose aliases are missing, out of date (another `image.ini` key, expired, or older than the
  original) or, when asked, never made. Each row: the object and its node, the field and language, the version, the
  expected path, the last modified time and the problem. Ticked alone, no relation is checked. With a total and
  pages (`(file_offset)`, the findings kept `ListCacheTTL` seconds). Datatypes through `contentcheck.ini [MissingFileSettings] Handlers[]` (`ezimage`, `ezbinaryfile`,
  `ezmedia`; an extension adds its own `expContentCheckFileHandler`). Command line:
  `./console exp:content-check --missing-files [--problems=...] [--versions=...]`; opt-in count in the cronjob part
  `contentcheck` (`CronjobReport=enabled`). Read only. Guide: [Missing files and image aliases](../../eng-us/guides/missing-files.md).
  Strings in eng-US and German; tests `expContentCheckFilesTest` (no database).
- **Resolving missing files** (`contentcheck/fixfiles`, POST only, policy `contentcheck/fixfiles` with
  `brokenrelations`, and `content/edit` on each object): regenerate the aliases from the original, restore the file
  from another version that still has it, re-upload or replace it (same extension; an image must be an image), remove
  the dead reference (the database only), or mark for review; per row or for the ticked rows, with select all on the
  page (`contentcheck_select.js`, and links without JavaScript), select all and none in the filter groups, and a
  preview for all results. Always a preview first and a confirmation token bound to the rows shown; done in batches
  of `FixBatchSize` (10) with progress; nothing is ever deleted or overwritten. Audited as
  `content.file.alias.regenerate`, `content.file.restore`, `content.file.replace`, `content.file.reference.remove` and
  `content.file.review.mark`. Command line: `--fix=<action> [--items=...] [--replace-with=...] [--dry-run]`. Tests
  `expContentCheckFileFixTest` (no database).

## 17 October 2026: the log viewer

Page: [The log viewer](../../eng-us/features/6.0/log-viewer.md); guide:
[Finding an error by its reference](../../eng-us/guides/finding-an-error-by-its-reference.md).

### Added
- **Download all logs** on Setup > Manage logs: one `.zip` (or `.tar.gz`) of every log, its rotations optional, with a
  `MANIFEST.txt` of sizes, last writes and sha256; masked as the viewer shows them unless "Unredacted" is ticked (only
  with `setup/unmasklogs`); POST with the form token, audited as `system.log.download` (`after.bundle`), built as a
  stream under `var/tmp/logbundle` and removed after it is sent; capped by `logviewer.ini [ActionSettings]
  MaxBundleBytes` (default 256 MB). The row of buttons of the log viewer's pages has room above, below and between its
  lines.
- **Managing logs** (Setup > Log files > Manage logs, `setup/logsmanage`, policy `setup/managelogs`): every log with
  its size, estimated lines, last write, growth, archives and limits; rotate now, empty (archive first), compress the
  archives, remove archives older than N days, rotate all and prune all, each with a preview of every step before it
  is applied under a lock and audited. The limits come from `logviewer.ini [RotationSettings]` per log, else
  `site.ini [FileSettings]`; new switch `[ActionSettings] Compress` (default `disabled`). The work is done by the log
  manager, or by a built-in fallback with the same API.
- **Log health** (`setup/logshealth`): disk use against `[HealthSettings] DiskBudget` (256 MB), the fastest growing
  logs, logs over their limit, files the web server cannot write (mixed ownership), the newest error per log linked to
  its line, and the rotation settings in effect with the file and section each comes from.
- **Error digest** (`setup/logsdigest`): the distinct errors and warnings of the last 24 hours per log, masked,
  normalized, grouped and counted, linked to their filtered view; "Mark as known" per user (preference
  `admin_logviewer_known`). Settings `[DigestSettings]`.
- **Archives** open read only under `/setup/logs/view/<key>/(archive)/N` (`(rotation)/N` still read).
- **Copy messages**: copies the lines shown as plain text (following: every line kept; paused: the lines on screen),
  with a message saying how many.
- Tests without a database: `expLogArchiveViewTest`, `expLogHealthTest`, `expLogDigestTest`,
  `expLogManagerFallbackTest`, `LogViewerCopyMessagesTest`.
- **Wrapping long lines is on by default and kept per viewer**: the Wrap lines switch stores the choice as the user
  preference `admin_logviewer_wrap`, the same for every log, permalink and visit, drawn on first paint; without a
  choice `logviewer.ini [TailSettings] WrapLines` (default `enabled`) decides. The Open and Copy permalink buttons
  are on a line of their own at the start, and Open, Copy permalink, Download and Rotate are primary buttons.
- **Setup > Log files** (`setup/logs`, Setup menu group System, admin4's mega menu): the log files of the
  installation (`var/log`, every site's `var/<site>/log`, the logs of extensions) and their rotations `.1` .. `.N` and
  `.gz` (read only), in a navigator grouped Kernel, Site, Extensions and Rotated archives with size, time and line rate.
- **Following a file live** with short polls (`setup/logsfeed`), never a held request: a `stat()` first, only the
  bytes after the reader's offset, at most `MaxBytesPerPoll` per poll, whole lines only, a server-side cache of the
  last chunk per file, no file locks, an interval that doubles while nothing arrives; pause and resume; rotation and
  emptying detected by inode and size.
- **Search** of a file, every kernel log or every file, with their rotations: literal or regular expression (with
  `pcre.backtrack_limit` and a time limit), levels, time range (a binary search to its start), siteaccess, request,
  client address, error reference; bounded per request, continued with "More results".
- **Cross-references**: the line format `[ time ] [client] [siteaccess] [request] message` read into entries; the
  lines of the same request across the kernel logs; the error reference of an error page (`ERR-XXXXXXXXXX`) found with
  its request; the audit console of the same minute; links to nodes, objects, users, classes, requests and templates.
- **Masking by default**: e-mail addresses, IP addresses, passwords, tokens, keys, session ids and cookies; the values
  of secret settings always. The per-viewer switch "Show unmasked" for `setup/unmasklogs`.
- **Actions**, POST with the form token and audited: download a range (masked, gzip; on), rotate now, empty a file,
  remove an archived rotation (each off until switched on, with a confirmation page).
- **Policies** `setup/logs`, `setup/managelogs`, `setup/unmasklogs` (Administrator only in a new installation). Audit
  events `system.log.read`, `system.log.download`, `system.log.rotate`, `system.log.truncate`, `system.log.delete`
  (channel system, on).
- **A rate limit** per session for the feed (`[RateLimitSettings]`, 429 with Retry-After), kept outside the session.
- **On the command line**: `./console exp:logs` (`bin/php/explogs.php`): `--list`, `--tail [--follow]`, `--search`
  with the same filters, `--reference`, `--related`, `--json`, `--unmask`, level colors.
- Settings `settings/logviewer.ini`: `[LogViewerSettings]`, `[TailSettings]`, `[SearchSettings]`,
  `[RedactionSettings]`, `[ActionSettings]`, `[RateLimitSettings]`, `[ViewerAuditSettings]`, every one documented with
  its default on the page. Classes in `kernel/classes/logviewer/`; template fetch `fetch( 'setup', 'log_files' )`.
- Tests (no database) in `tests/tests/kernel/logviewer/`: the parser, offset tailing and the chunk cache, masking, path
  safety, the regular expression limit, the search, the rate limit and the actions.
- **Permalinks** without a query string, by the log's key: `/setup/logs/view/error`, `/setup/logs/view/debug`,
  `/setup/logs/view/site-storage`, `/setup/logs/view/webserver-access`, with the whole view as view parameters
  (`(rotation)`, `(level)`, `(search)`, `(regex)`, `(case)`, `(from)`, `(to)`, `(siteaccess)`, `(uri)`, `(ip)`,
  `(reference)`, `(scope)`, `(rotations)`, `(cursor)`, `(offset)`, `(follow)`, `(wrap)`), e.g.
  `/setup/logs/view/error/(level)/error/(search)/timeout/(follow)/0`. A value is written as is when it is letters,
  digits and `_ . , -`, any other byte as `~XX`. The page keeps its address in step with the view, and **Copy
  permalink** copies it (never with the unmasked switch). The navigator, the menus and the actions link to permalinks.
  A key names only a listed log, never a path; an unknown key answers 404. `expLogPermalink` writes and reads them.
- **Old links** `/setup/logs?file=var/log/error.log` (with the search fields of that form) answer `301` with the
  permalink of the same file and search; the search form without JavaScript is answered `303` with its permalink.
- **The web server's logs**, configured by key: `logviewer.ini [LogViewerSettings] LogList[]`, `LogPath[<key>]`,
  `LogLabel[<key>]`, `LogGroup[<key>]` (`webserver-access` and `webserver-error` are listed with no path, so they are
  off until a path is set: never guessed). Shown in the navigator group **Web server** with their rotations
  (`<name>.N`, `<name>.N.gz`), read and downloaded only: rotate, empty and remove are refused for a configured log. The
  parser reads access logs (common and combined; the status gives the level) and Apache 2.4 error logs.
- `[LogViewerSettings] DefaultKey` (`error`): the log `/setup/logs` opens.
- Tests: `expLogPermalinkTest` (encoding, view parameters, round trip), `expLogKeyTest` (keys, configured logs, the
  old links), `expLogWebServerFormatsTest` (no database).

### Updated
- The Setup overview (`setup/menu`) also opens with `setup/logs` alone.
- The audit catalogue has 167 names (five `system.log.*` added).

### Fixed
- **A search finds only what the viewer is shown.** The request and client address filters compared the values as
  written while the results showed them masked, so a viewer without `setup/unmasklogs` could find out a masked address
  one search at a time (`203.0.1` finds it, `203.0.2` does not, ...) and likewise a token or e-mail address in a
  request. With masking on, both filters now also compare the masked value (`203.0.` still finds `203.0.113.7`,
  shown as `203.0.x.x`; `203.0.113` finds nothing); the text, the request and the client address are checked against
  the redactor for every viewer, so the values of secret settings and the secrets in an address, hidden even with
  **Show unmasked**, are not found either. Unmasked, the address and the request are compared as written, as before.
  `expLogSearch::run()` takes the viewer's `expLogRedactor` (a callable still works, for the text only);
  `expLogSearch::entryMatches()` takes it as an optional third argument. Tests: `expLogSearchTest` (LV-48),
  `expLogViewerTest` (LV-65).
- **The filters compare only what is shown, so a value copied from the page finds its entries.** With a viewer's
  redactor the request and client address filters are compared with the shown value alone (no longer also with the
  written one), and the client address filter accepts an `x`: `203.0.x.x`, `2001:db8:x:x`, `2001::x`, `::x`,
  `token=***`. Before, the masked addresses of `404.log` and `security.log` (written `203.0.x.x`) could not be filtered
  beyond their first two parts, and a masked value copied from the page was refused or found nothing. A filter with a
  mark of the masks also finds, for an unmasked viewer, what a masked viewer would find. The same holds for the
  archives, the page's script, the command line and the error digest. New: `expLogSearch::shownContains()`,
  `shownStartsWith()`, `expLogRedactor::masked()`, `masksIps()`, `ipFilter()`, `ipFilterOf()`.
- **A shared permalink never carries an unmasked address.** **Copy permalink** gives the link with the request and
  client address filters as the masks of the settings show them (`(ip)/203.0.113.7` becomes `(ip)/203.0.x.x`, an
  e-mail address `j***@***.com`), new `expLogPermalink::shareable()` and the template variable
  `$log_share_permalink` (both designs; an override without it falls back to `$log_permalink`). The address bar keeps
  the filters as typed. With `MaskIPs=disabled` the address stays.
- **A compressed IPv6 address with one group before the `::` is masked.** `2001::7334` and `fe80::1` were shown in
  full by the log viewer; they are shown as `2001::x` and `fe80::x`, and `404.log` and `security.log` write that form
  instead of `x`. "Class::method" in a message is left alone (only the address field is affected).
- **The error digest links a log grouped by `uri` with the request filter** (`GET /blog/#` links with
  `(uri)/~2Fblog~2F`), masked as the group is; `expLogDigest::link()` takes the grouping as an optional third
  argument, `expLogDigest::uriFilterOf()` is new. Tests: `expLogMaskedFilterTest` (LV-220 to LV-227: IPv4, IPv6 and
  its compressed forms, the request logs, the archives, the digest, the permalinks, without a redactor).

### Upgrade notes
- Nothing changes for a site until someone opens the page: no new table, no cronjob. The new policies are held by
  Administrator only; rotate, empty and remove are switched off (`[ActionSettings]`). A site that keeps logs in another
  directory adds it to `[LogViewerSettings] Directories[]` (it must be inside the installation root).
- To show the web server's logs, set their paths, e.g. on Plesk
  `LogPath[webserver-access]=/var/www/vhosts/<domain>/logs/access_ssl_log` and
  `LogPath[webserver-error]=/var/www/vhosts/<domain>/logs/error_log`; the user that runs PHP must be able to read them.
- Bookmarks of the first form (`?file=`) keep working: they are redirected to the permalink.

## 17 October 2026: content checks: quick scope buttons and a row of sites and sections

- Every content check (unreferenced objects, broken relations with the missing files, storage files) starts with the
  quick scope buttons **Whole tree** (node 1), **Websites** (the content root, `content.ini [NodeSettings] RootNode`)
  and **Media** (`MediaRootNode`), then **Choose with the node picker** for any other node, Users among them.
- Beneath them a second row lists the **sites and sections** under the content root, read live from the content tree:
  the visible children you may read, in the content root's sort order, names escaped. At most 12; with more,
  **More…** opens the node picker at that level (`contentcheck/scope/<view>/<node id>/<start node id>`; a cancel
  goes back to the check where it was). The site the start location lies in is outlined.
- All of them are plain links to `contentcheck/<view>/<node id>` (no JavaScript needed), wrap on phones and mark the
  current one with `aria-current`. The storage files keep the list shown (`?Show=`) when the scope changes.
- One shared part, `design:contentcheck/scope.tpl` (admin and admin4), from `expContentCheckScope::forTemplate()`.
- Settings (`contentcheck.ini [ScopeSettings]`): `PresetList[]` now defaults to `tree`, `content`, `media` (was also
  `media/images` and `users`; add them back there if wanted); new `SiteRow=enabled`, `SiteRowParent=content` (a preset
  key, node id or URL alias path) and `SiteRowLimit=12` (1 to 50). `SiteRow=disabled` hides the second row.
- Tests: `expContentCheckStorageTest` CS-15 (no database). Translations: eng-US, ger-DE.
- Page: [The content checks](../../eng-us/features/6.0/content-checks.md#where-a-check-starts).

## 17 October 2026: the siteaccesses on Setup > System information

  the URI wins on any host (the mapped host may not even resolve). It keeps this host unless the host rule comes

## 17 October 2026: clean logs and one log rotation

A crawl of every site siteaccess, the administration and the default cronjobs now leaves nothing in `error.log`,
`warning.log`, `deprecated.log` and `strict.log` but the requests for pages that do not exist (#555).

### Updated
- **One log rotation for the kernel** (`expLogRotator`, `lib/ezfile/classes/explogrotator.php`), used by eZDebug and
  eZLog: an exclusive lock on the log while it is rotated, the size checked again under the lock (two requests that
  both found a log too big rotated it twice, and the archives raced: "rename(storage.log.N): No such file"), and the
  new empty log created at once with the owner, group and mode of the old one, so a log written by Velocity (root)
  and PHP-FPM (the site's user) stays writable for both.
- The last timing point is written before the first message of a request into `debug.log` only; it was an entry of
  its own ahead of every first error, warning and notice.
- The error page's log line names the error and its HTTP status ("(kernel error 2, HTTP 404)"). A login request (401),
  a refused request (403) and a moved page (301) are notices, not errors; the policies a refused request did not
  match are debug output.
- On an eng-US siteaccess a text the eng-US translation does not reword is shown as written and not logged as a
  missing translation (one debug line per text per page before).
- Routine traces are debug output: the online editor's fallback editor, the layout resolver's cache hits and matched
  rules, a version without a name in a language, the upload location match, a skipped preference write, the
  two-factor sign-in copy of `auth.log`.
- `runcronjobs.php -q` no longer prints "No scripts found for execution" for a part without scripts.
- A translation file that does not validate raises one warning naming the file and its first errors (one PHP
  warning per schema complaint before).

### Fixed
- PHP 8.5 deprecations: null passed to `trim()`, `rawurldecode()`, `explode()`, `strlen()` and `createTextNode()`,
  and null as an array offset, in content views, the data map, the module dispatch, advanced search, keywords, the
  class package export and the string template operators (which now take null as an empty string).
- A missing file row of the content checks redefined `cc_action_names` (a warning per row); the classic
  administration's left menu read `LinkNames[]` of sections that have none; a refused form raised an
  `http_response_code()` warning; `content/translations` logged its optional fields as missing; the parent of a
  draft's virtual node logged an empty fetch; a cache callback that returns nothing was reported as an error.
- The translations carry the upgrade check's texts on inactive extensions, the image editor's button, the sub-items
  table's Editorial preset, and six administration texts the untranslated catalogue lacked. A new test
  (`TranslationFilesValidTest`) validates the three kernel translation files against their schema and checks every
  template text is in them.
- Extensions released with their log fixes: exp_oe_tiptap 0.1.9, expai 0.0.1.7, explayouts 1.4.35, eztags 2.4.16,
  git_manager 2.0.19, sevenx_authentication_2fa 1.0.7, ezwebin 6.0.21, exp_adminui 1.0.0.37 (`composer.json`
  requires them).

### Upgrade notes
- New settings with safe defaults in `site.ini [FileSettings]`: `LogFileCompress=disabled` (compress the archives
  from `.2` on) and `LogFileMaxAge=0` (the age in days after which a prune removes an archive; 0: none).
- `eZDebug::rotateLog()` and `eZLog::rotateLog()` take an optional second parameter, the size a log must still exceed
  under the lock; without it they rotate as before.
- A log line of the error page ends with "(<type> error <number>, HTTP <status>)"; a filter that matched the whole
  line "Error ocurred using URI: <uri>" must allow the suffix.

## 17 October 2026: rich text keeps characters above U+FFFF, TeX comments and line breaks in custom attributes

### Fixed
- `eZXMLInputHandler` and `eZXMLInputParser::process()` allow the characters XML 1.0 allows above U+FFFF
  (#x10000-#x10FFFF). Before, opening a stored text in an editor removed each of them without a replacement
  (`<mi>𝑥</mi>` in a MathML attribute became `<mi></mi>`, `𝔸 = \mathbb{A}` in a formula ` = \mathbb{A}`), and saving
  made each of them a space (`a 😀 b` became `a b`). Control characters, U+FFFE and U+FFFF are still removed.
- Online Editor (`eZOEXMLInput::inputXML()`): a line break in a custom attribute value becomes a space instead of
  vanishing; multi-line MathML in an attribute (`</mi>&#10;<mo>`) lost the break and glued the tags together.

### Added
- `content.ini [CustomTagSettings] TeXSource[<tag>]=true` names custom tags whose text is TeX source. When the Online
  Editor opens such a text it reads it as TeX does: an unescaped `%` comment is dropped with the rest of its line, the
  line break and the indentation of the next line (a run of comment lines counts as one); after a control word a space
  stays (`\alpha%⏎beta` gives `\alpha beta`). Before, the line break became a space and `a % comment⏎+ b` turned into
  `a % comment + b`, with the rest of the formula commented out. Leading white space of such a tag is dropped instead
  of being stored as a no-break space, and `AllowMultipleSpaces` adds no `&nbsp;` there. None is set by default; other
  custom tags keep the line-break-to-space rule.

- `ezxml.ini [InputSettings] SupplementaryCharacters=auto` (`enabled`, `disabled`) decides where those characters are
  kept. `auto` keeps them unless the database is MySQL or MariaDB on a connection charset other than `utf8mb4`:
  `Charset=utf-8` connects as `utf8`, three bytes at most, where they would be stored as `????` or fail the save in
  strict SQL mode. There they are replaced as before.
- Rich text that is not valid UTF-8 no longer empties the whole text in the input handler and parser
  (`preg_replace()` returned null); each invalid byte sequence becomes U+FFFD.
- `eZOEXMLInput::texWithoutLineComments()` finds a control word before a comment by walking back over the letters,
  not with a regular expression over the whole text before it for every comment, and takes ASCII letters only.

Tests: `eZXMLInputSupplementaryCharactersTest` (kernel-datatypes), `expOEFormulaTextTest` (ezoe), both without a
database. Documentation: [Rich text: characters above U+FFFF and TeX source](../../eng-us/features/6.0/rich-text-supplementary-characters-and-tex-source.md).

## 17 October 2026: the side columns stay where they are while an admin4 page loads

### Fixed
- **The content column no longer narrows after the first paint**: the first paint's rules for the side columns
  (`html.a4-preload`) found the columns with `body:has(.sidebar.right)`, and the right column stands after the content
  in the source, so a paint of the half-parsed page drew the content 232 px wider and then wrapped it (node views:
  0.12 to 0.14 of layout shift at 1440 px). The page layouts of admin4 and admin4l now name the page's columns on
  `<body data-a4-columns="left right">` (left: the frame's column or the one an edit-type page draws itself);
  `:has()` stays for a page layout without the attribute.
- **The right column's tools no longer move up**: the Interface tool's design list was drawn open and closed by
  `admin4.js` once the document was parsed, and every tool below it moved up 96 px. `a4_head.js` sets `html.a4-js`
  before the first paint, and `admin4.css` draws the disclosure button and the closed list from the start.
- **Trash, the content finder and the width buttons no longer move down while the content tree is built**: the left
  card with the content tree fills the column, the tree scrolls in it (at least 8 rem high), and the parts after it
  stand at the card's foot (they moved 20 to 600 px on every page with the tree). Test: `AdminFirstPaintColumnsTest`
  (FP-05 to FP-07).
- **The newsletter menu's tree no longer covers its Administer and Settings lists** (`newsletter/*`, admin4 and
  admin4l): the rules for the tree card above also reached a tree inside a box of its own (`#newsletter-menu >
  #content-tree`), where the inner boxes with `flex: 1 1 0` had no height, the card ended under its heading and the
  tree was drawn over the lists after it. The rules now start at the column's own card
  (`#leftmenu-design > #content-tree`), so a wrapped tree keeps the ordinary flow. Test: `AdminFirstPaintColumnsTest`
  (FP-08).
