- Added: Twenty-two more audiences of the help, each with a dashboard card, a search filter, a pill, a category, a
  landing page (`[Landing_<audience>]`, start page `help/<folder>/<folder>-start-here.md`) and aliases: **Community**
  (`community`), **Ecosystem** (`ecosystem`), **PHP Developers** (`php-developer`), **Theme designers**
  (`theme-designer`, category Theme design), **Resellers** (`reseller`), **Bundlers** (`bundler`), **AI, agents and
  agentic** (`ai-agents`), **Software Engineers** (`software-engineer`), **Adopters** (`adopter`), **Mobile App
  Developers** (`mobile-app-developer`), **Web Framework Developers** (`web-framework-developer`), **CMS Developers**
  (`cms-developer`), **DXP Developers** (`dxp-developer`), **E-commerce** (`ecommerce`, folder `e-commerce`),
  **Integrators** (`integrator`), **Database Administrators** (`dba`, folder `database-administrators`),
  **Podcasters, radio and streaming** (`podcaster`), **Non-profits** (`non-profit`), **Local businesses**
  (`local-business`), **Individuals** (`individual`), **Non-technical people** (`non-technical`, folder
  `non-technical-people`) and **Students and learners** (`student`, folder `students`). Overlapping audiences name
  each other as related on their landing pages, both ways.

  after "Who is 7x?" and "Who is the Exponential Foundation?", each opening its audience's landing page.

### Updated
- Updated: With forty-one audiences, the dashboard shows their cards in five groups (Use and run Exponential,
  Organizations, Build with Exponential, Host, sell and partner, Community and learning), each a folding section with
  its count: the first is open, the others folded. The search hero shows the filters of the first group and the others

  icons `book`, `chat-left-dots`, `chat-square-text` and `collection-play` (Bootstrap Icons, MIT, already in
  `share/icons`) get their `.a4-bi-*` classes.
- Updated: The new strings are translated in the twenty-three complete catalogues that have the `kernel/exphelp`
  context and added to `untranslated`.

### Tests

  (every audience in exactly one group), the "Who makes Exponential" links and each audience's start page (checked
  once the page exists); `expHelpLandingPagesTest` checks their landing groups and that overlapping audiences relate

## 21 October 2026: the Become an Exponential Partner block of the help pages is a card

### Updated
- Updated: The **Become an Exponential Partner** paragraph that ends almost every help page, in every language
  (**Werden Sie Exponential-Partner** in German), is shown in the help viewer as a card like the footer blocks of the
  help dashboard ("Grow from user to developer", "Found an error?"): the partner icon on a tile, the bold lead as its
  heading, the text, and the two links (**Partners: start here**, **How to apply**) as buttons, the first one primary.
  `expHelpMarkdownRenderer` recognizes the block by the markers every page already carries
  (`<!-- exp-partner-block:start -->` and `<!-- exp-partner-block:end -->`), with option `partner_card`, which the viewer
  sets; the Markdown files are unchanged and read on GitHub as before. A marked block that is not one paragraph with a
  bold lead, or has no end marker, is shown as written. Both admin designs (admin and admin4), light and dark: the CSS
  is in `stylesheets/modules/help.css` (`.a4-help-partner*`), shared tokens only, no inline CSS.
- Updated: The PDF of a help page prints the card as a box with its title, its text and its links on one line. The
  search, the DITA and content exports and the generated example pages (which carry no such block) are unchanged.
  Cached pages render again (render version 7, PDF writer version 2).
- Updated: A page shown in another language than the reader's (`/(lang)/ger-de` for an English reader) resolves its
  links in its own language: they were resolved against the English original's file, so the relative links of a
  German page (`../partner/partner-hier-beginnen.md`) named no page and were shown as text, and the German partner
  card had no buttons. The viewer renders the page with its language's index (`expHelpDocumentIndex::setInstance()`
  for the render, restored after; `fixedInstance()` is new), render version 8. A partner block whose links lead nowhere
  keeps its last sentence as text instead of showing it as buttons.

### Upgrading
- Nothing to do: the cached help pages and PDFs are made again on their next view.

### Tests
- `expHelpPartnerCardTest`: the English and the German block become the card (heading, text, buttons with the links
  kept, the partner icon), other bold paragraphs and broken blocks stay paragraphs, the buttons follow the link
  policy, two real pages render one card each, the CSS of both designs is the same and uses tokens only, and the PDF
  prints the card. PC-08: a German page rendered with the German index has its two buttons to the pages they name,
  with the English reader's index none (the sentence stays text). Without a database.
## 21 October 2026: the layout editor saves a view type change alone and gives added items positions of their own

### Updated
- explayouts_ui_api 1.3.29: on the Design tab, changing only the **View type** of a list is saved also when the new
  view type leaves no item view type to choose (`list_zigzag`, `list_accordion`, one of your own); **+ Add items**
  gives every new item a position of its own (the new items first, in the order chosen) instead of storing all of
  them at the collection's offset, and moving an item moves the ones in between.

  (`doc/eng-us/help/build-two-ways/known-editor-behaviors.md`, the view type and list examples, and their German
  versions) say they are fixed in explayouts_ui_api 1.3.29 and keep the workaround for older versions.

### Upgrading
- Collection items that already share a position are sorted out on the next change to their collection. No such
  items were left on the test installation.

### Tests
- explayouts_ui_api: `CollectionItemPositionsTest` (without a database) and `tests/js/view-type-save.test.mjs` (the
  bundle's own modules with jQuery 4).
